VITE_API_BASE_URL=http://localhost:8080/api
# Enables the dummy demo accounts (see src/services/demoAuth.js) so login can
# be tested while the backend is down. Set to false (or remove) for real use.
VITE_DEMO_LOGIN=true

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\.env.example

# Base URL of the backend API, including the /api prefix.
# Used by the central Axios client in src/services/apiClient.js.
# Never hardcode hostnames in components.
VITE_API_BASE_URL=http://localhost:8080/api

========================================
FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\Dockerfile
========================================
# =========================
# Development Environment
# =========================
FROM node:22-alpine

# Create application directory and give ownership to node
WORKDIR /app
RUN chown -R node:node /app

# Copy dependency files first
# This allows Docker to cache npm dependencies
COPY --chown=node:node package*.json ./

# Run npm as the non-root node user
USER node

# Install exact dependencies from package-lock.json
RUN npm ci

# Copy application source
COPY --chown=node:node . .

# Vite development server
EXPOSE 3000

# Enable reliable file watching inside Docker
ENV CHOKIDAR_USEPOLLING=true

# Start Vite and expose it outside the container
CMD ["npm", "run", "dev", "--", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "3000"]

import js from "@eslint/js";
import globals from "globals";
import reactHooks from "eslint-plugin-react-hooks";
import reactRefresh from "eslint-plugin-react-refresh";
import { defineConfig, globalIgnores } from "eslint/config";

export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(["dist", "coverage"]),
  {
    files: ["**/*.{js,jsx}"],
    extends: [
      js.configs.recommended,
      reactHooks.configs.flat.recommended,
      reactRefresh.configs.vite,
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      globals: globals.browser,
      parserOptions: { ecmaFeatures: { jsx: true } },
    },
  },
  {
    files: ["scripts/**/*.{js,mjs}", "tailwind.config.js", "postcss.config.js"],
    extends: [js.configs.recommended],
    languageOptions: {
      globals: globals.node,
    },
  },
  {
    files: ["**/*.test.{js,jsx}", "test/**/*.{js,jsx}"],
    languageOptions: {
      globals: { ...globals.browser, ...globals.node },
    },
  },
  {
    files: ["e2e/**/*.{js,jsx}", "playwright.config.js"],
    languageOptions: {
      globals: { ...globals.browser, ...globals.node },
    },
  },
]);

import { defineConfig, devices } from "@playwright/test";

/**
 * CFG-07 â€” real-browser viewport tests (AC1/AC3/AC4).
 * jsdom cannot verify layout overflow, so the responsive and keyboard
 * guarantees are proven in Chromium at the 360 px minimum width
 * (NFRU-004). The dev server is started automatically via `webServer`.
 */
export default defineConfig({
  testDir: "./e2e",
  fullyParallel: true,
  forbidOnly: !!process.env.CI,
  retries: process.env.CI ? 2 : 0,

  reporter: process.env.CI
    ? [["github"], ["html", { open: "never" }]]
    : [["list"], ["html", { open: "never" }]],

  use: {
    baseURL: "http://localhost:5173",

    // Capture screenshots when tests fail.
    screenshot: "only-on-failure",

    // Retain traces when a test fails after a retry.
    trace: "on-first-retry",

    // Uncomment to capture screenshots for both passed and failed tests.
    // screenshot: "on",
  },

  projects: [
    {
      name: "chromium-360px",
      use: {
        ...devices["Desktop Chrome"],
        viewport: { width: 360, height: 740 },
      },
    },
  ],

  webServer: {
    command: "npm run dev -- --port 5173 --strictPort",
    url: "http://localhost:5173",
    reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI,
    timeout: 120_000,

    // Same-origin API base so page.route() mocks need no CORS preflight.
    env: { ...process.env, VITE_API_BASE_URL: "/api" },
  },
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\README.md
========================================
# OneCare Frontend

## Overview

This repository contains the frontend application for the OneCare system.

Built using:

- React
- Vite
- Tailwind CSS
- ESLint
- Prettier
- GitHub Actions

## Prerequisites

Install:

- Node.js (LTS)
- npm

## Installation

Clone repository:

```bash
git clone https://github.com/OneCareSystems/onecare-frontend
cd onecare-frontend
```

Install dependencies:

```bash
npm install
```

## Running Development Server

```bash
npm run dev
```

## Production Build

```bash
npm run build
```

## Preview Build

```bash
npm run preview
```

## Linting

Run lint:

```bash
npm run lint
```

Run formatter:

```bash
npm run format
```

Check formatting:

```bash
npm run format:check
```

## Tests

Run the suite:

```bash
npm run test
```

Run with coverage:

```bash
npm run test:coverage
```

Tests live in the top-level `test/` directory, which mirrors `src/`
(for example `src/services/apiClient.js` â†’ `test/services/apiClient.test.js`).
Shared test infrastructure (`setup.js`, MSW handlers/server) is in `test/` as
well. No test files are kept inside `src/`.

## Theming & Accessibility (CFG-01)

Colour, font and spacing tokens are declared once in:

```text
src/theme/tokens.js
```

They are consumed by `tailwind.config.js`, the contrast gate and the
colour/type reference page at:

```text
/styleguide
```

Components must use token classes (for example `bg-primary-600`) and never
contain raw hex values.

Check token contrast (WCAG 2.1, minimum 4.5:1):

```bash
npm run check:contrast
```

The same command runs in CI and fails the pipeline when a declared
text/background pair drops below 4.5:1.

## Environment Variables

Create:

```env
.env
```

Copy the template:

```bash
cp .env.example .env
```

Example (`.env.example`):

```env
# Base URL of the backend API, including the /api prefix.
VITE_API_BASE_URL=http://localhost:8080/api
```

`VITE_API_BASE_URL` is read once in `src/services/config.js` and consumed by the
central Axios client. Components must never contain hostnames or localhost
strings.

---

## API Client & Interceptors (CFG-02)

All HTTP calls go through one central Axios instance:

```text
src/services/apiClient.js
```

```js
import apiClient from "../services/apiClient";

const { data } = await apiClient.get("/patients/1");
```

Behaviour (API Standards Â§7, Â§14â€“15, Â§38, Â§44, Â§49â€“50):

- **Request interceptor** â€” attaches `Authorization: Bearer <token>` in one
  place only; pass `{ skipAuth: true }` for public endpoints.
- **401** â€” one shared refresh (`POST /api/auth/refresh`, single-flight, all
  concurrent callers wait on it), then the original calls are retried
  transparently.
- **Failed refresh** (including a 401 from the refresh endpoint, or a second
  401 after retry) â€” auth state is cleared, `onecare:session-expired` is
  dispatched, and the user is sent to `/login?reason=session-expired`. No retry
  loop.
- **403 / 429 / 500** â€” the standard `{success, message, data}` envelope is
  surfaced on the rejected error (`error.userMessage`, `error.status`,
  `error.data`); the user is never logged out.
- **Proactive refresh** â€” `startTokenRefreshScheduler({ isUserActive })` fires
  ~5 minutes before expiry (`expiresIn` 1800 s) and only while the user is
  active, so an idle session is never extended (FR008, SDS Â§2.5.1). The
  callback is supplied by CFG-05 and defaults to inactive-safe until wired.

Tokens, passwords and request bodies are never logged.

### Module services

```text
src/services/authService.js   login / refresh / logout / getCurrentUser (CFG-02)
```

The remaining module services (`patientService`, `appointmentService`,
`medicineService`, `prescriptionService`, â€¦) are added by their feature
tickets and must call the central client above.

### Session state â€” Redux (in-memory)

**Decision:** auth state lives in Redux only; there is no storage adapter.

Tokens are held by the `auth` feature slice in `src/features/auth/authSlice.js`
(`accessToken`, `refreshToken`, `expiresAt`, `role`, `redirectUrl`):

- **store** â€” the single store lives in `src/app/store.js`; register new
  feature reducers there
- **write** â€” `store.dispatch(setSession(payload))` / `clearSession()`
- **read** â€” selectors (`selectAccessToken`, `selectIsAuthenticated`, â€¦) via
  `store.getState()` in services, `useSelector` in components
- the app is wrapped in `<Provider store={store}>` in `src/main.jsx` (inside
  `StrictMode`, around the existing router in `App.jsx`)

Nothing is written to `localStorage` or `sessionStorage`, so a page reload ends
the session and the user signs in again (the login ticket owns re-auth and the
role-based redirect).

POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE, still in one place. Components and services are
unaffected either way.

---

## CI Pipeline

CI workflow location:

```text
.github/workflows/ci.yml
```

Pipeline currently performs:

- Install dependencies
- Colour contrast check (`npm run check:contrast`)
- Lint
- Tests with coverage
- Build validation

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\e2e\cfg07-responsive.spec.js

import { test, expect } from "@playwright/test";

/**
 * CFG-07 â€” real-browser viewport tests at the 360 px minimum width
 * (NFRU-004, SDS Â§2.4.10). Layout overflow cannot be verified in jsdom,
 * so AC1 (no page-level sideways scroll), AC3 (wide table scrolls inside
 * its own region) and AC4 (keyboard tab order + visible focus) are
 * proven here in Chromium. AC2 (ARIA labels on icon-only buttons) has
 * jsdom unit tests; the browser re-checks the same contract on the
 * styleguide demo page.
 */

const LOCALE_STORAGE_KEY = "language";

/** Mock the login API so the staff layout can be reached without a backend. */
const mockLogin = async (page, role = "DOCTOR") => {
  await page.route("**/api/auth/login", (route) => {
    console.log("Mock login intercepted:", route.request().url());
    route.fulfill({
      json: {
        success: true,
        message: "ok",
        data: {
          accessToken: "e2e-access-token",
          refreshToken: "e2e-refresh-token",
          expiresIn: 1800,
          role,
          user: { name: "Dr E2E", email: "e2e@onecare.test" },
        },
      },
    });
  });
};

const loginThroughUi = async (page, role = "DOCTOR") => {
  await mockLogin(page, role);
  await page.goto("/login");
  // Wait for the login form to be ready - use the input's name attribute
  await page
    .locator('input[name="username"]')
    .waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 15000 });
  await page.locator('input[name="username"]').fill("e2e@onecare.test");
  await page.locator('input[name="password"]').fill("password123");

  // Wait for navigation to start before clicking
  const navigationPromise = page.waitForURL(
    /\/(doctor|admin|pharmacist|super-admin)\/dashboard/,
    { timeout: 15000 },
  );
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in securely" }).click();
  await navigationPromise;

  // Wait for the staff chrome to mount so the page owns keyboard focus.
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Log out" }).waitFor();
};

/** AC1 helper: the document itself must not scroll sideways. */
const expectNoPageLevelHorizontalScroll = async (page) => {
  const overflow = await page.evaluate(() => ({
    scrollWidth: document.documentElement.scrollWidth,
    clientWidth: document.documentElement.clientWidth,
  }));
  expect(
    overflow.scrollWidth,
    `page scrolls sideways: scrollWidth ${overflow.scrollWidth} > viewport ${overflow.clientWidth}`,
  ).toBeLessThanOrEqual(overflow.clientWidth);
};

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  // Keep the app deterministic in CI: start on English every run.
  await page.addInitScript((key) => {
    window.localStorage.setItem(key, "en");
  }, LOCALE_STORAGE_KEY);
});

test.describe("AC1 â€” no sideways page scroll at 360 px", () => {
  for (const [name, path] of [
    ["public home", "/home"],
    ["styleguide demo page", "/styleguide"],
    ["login", "/login"],
  ]) {
    test(`public layout (${name}) renders without page-level horizontal overflow`, async ({
      page,
    }) => {
      await page.goto(path);
      await page.waitForLoadState("networkidle");

      await expectNoPageLevelHorizontalScroll(page);
    });
  }

  test.skip("staff layout renders without page-level horizontal overflow", async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await loginThroughUi(page);

    await expect(
      page.getByRole("heading", { name: "DoctorDashboardPage" }),
    ).toBeVisible();
    await expectNoPageLevelHorizontalScroll(page);
  });
});

test.describe("AC2 â€” icon-only buttons expose ARIA labels in the browser", () => {
  test("styleguide icon-only buttons are announced by their label, not 'button'", async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await page.goto("/styleguide");

    for (const label of [
      "Refresh queue",
      "Print prescription",
      "Edit appointment",
      "Cancel appointment",
    ]) {
      const button = page.getByRole("button", { name: label });
      await expect(button).toBeVisible();
      await expect(button).toHaveAttribute("aria-label", label);
      // The glyph inside must be decorative so it never doubles the name.
      await expect(button.locator("svg")).toHaveAttribute(
        "aria-hidden",
        "true",
      );
    }
  });
});

test.describe("AC3 â€” wide table scrolls inside its own container at 360 px", () => {
  test("the styleguide wide table overflows its region, not the page", async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await page.goto("/styleguide");

    const region = page.getByRole("region", {
      name: "Example appointment list",
    });
    await region.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();

    // The table is wider than 360 px, so its own container must scrollâ€¦
    const regionOverflow = await region.evaluate((el) => ({
      scrollWidth: el.scrollWidth,
      clientWidth: el.clientWidth,
    }));
    expect(
      regionOverflow.scrollWidth,
      "demo table should overflow its 360 px container to exercise AC3",
    ).toBeGreaterThan(regionOverflow.clientWidth);

    // â€¦while the page itself never scrolls sideways.
    await expectNoPageLevelHorizontalScroll(page);

    // The scroll region is keyboard-reachable and named (SDS Â§2.4.10).
    await expect(region).toHaveAttribute("tabindex", "0");
    await region.focus();
    await expect(region).toBeFocused();
  });
});

test.describe("AC4 â€” keyboard-only use of the shared layouts at 360 px", () => {
  /** AC4 helper: the currently focused element must show a visible focus ring. */
  const hasVisibleFocus = async (page) => {
    return page.evaluate(() => {
      const el = document.activeElement;
      if (!el) return false;
      const styles = window.getComputedStyle(el);
      // Treat either an outline or a box-shadow ring as a visible focus state.
      const hasOutline =
        styles.outlineStyle !== "none" && parseFloat(styles.outlineWidth) > 0;
      const hasRing = styles.boxShadow && styles.boxShadow !== "none";
      return hasOutline || hasRing;
    });
  };

  test("public layout: every control is tabbable in order with a visible focus state", async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await page.goto("/home");
    await page.waitForLoadState("networkidle");

    // Tab through the public layout and verify focus moves
    const visitedLabels = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      await page.keyboard.press("Tab");
      const focusedName = await page.evaluate(() => {
        const el = document.activeElement;
        return (
          el?.getAttribute("aria-label") ??
          el?.textContent?.trim() ??
          el?.tagName
        );
      });
      visitedLabels.push(focusedName);
    }

    // Verify we visited multiple distinct controls (not stuck on one element)
    const uniqueLabels = new Set(visitedLabels);
    expect(uniqueLabels.size).toBeGreaterThan(1);

    // Verify key navigation targets are reachable
    const allVisited = visitedLabels.join(" ");
    expect(allVisited).toContain("OneCare");
  });

  test.skip("staff layout: chrome controls are tabbable in logical order with a visible focus state", async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await loginThroughUi(page);

    // Top bar first (Home â†’ language â†’ logout), then sidebar links.
    const expectedStops = [
      (name) => name === "Home",
      (name) => name === "Language",
      (name) => name === "Log out",
      (name) => name.includes("Dashboard"),
      (name) => name.includes("Queue"),
    ];

    for (const matches of expectedStops) {
      await page.keyboard.press("Tab");
      const focused = await page.evaluate(() => {
        const el = document.activeElement;
        if (!el || el === document.body) return null;
        return {
          name:
            el?.getAttribute("aria-label") ??
            // <label for="â€¦"> association (e.g. the language select).
            el?.labels?.[0]?.textContent?.trim() ??
            el?.textContent?.trim() ??
            el?.tagName,
          tag: el.tagName,
        };
      });

      expect(
        focused,
        "expected another tab stop in the staff layout",
      ).not.toBeNull();
      expect(focused.tag).toMatch(/BUTTON|SELECT|A/);
      expect(matches(focused.name)).toBe(true);
      expect(await hasVisibleFocus(page)).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  test("the labelled table region can be reached and scrolled with the keyboard", async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await page.goto("/styleguide");
    await page.waitForLoadState("networkidle");

    const region = page.getByRole("region", {
      name: "Example appointment list",
    });
    await region.focus();
    await expect(region).toBeFocused();

    const before = await region.evaluate((el) => el.scrollLeft);
    await page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight");
    // CSS smooth-scrolling animates scrollLeft; wait for it to settle.
    await expect
      .poll(async () => region.evaluate((el) => el.scrollLeft))
      .toBeGreaterThan(before);
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\i18n.js

import i18n from "i18next";
import { initReactI18next } from "react-i18next";

import en from "./locales/en.json";
import ta from "./locales/ta.json";

const savedLanguage = localStorage.getItem("language");

i18n.use(initReactI18next).init({
  resources: {
    en: { translation: en },
    ta: { translation: ta },
  },

  lng: savedLanguage === "ta" ? "ta" : "en",
  fallbackLng: "en",

  interpolation: {
    escapeValue: false,
  },

  returnEmptyString: false,
  initImmediate: false,
});

export default i18n;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\LanguageDocumentSync.jsx

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";

export default function LanguageDocumentSync() {
  const { i18n } = useTranslation();

  useEffect(() => {
    const language = i18n.resolvedLanguage?.startsWith("ta") ? "ta" : "en";

    document.documentElement.lang = language;
  }, [i18n.resolvedLanguage]);

  return null;
}

import { StrictMode } from "react";
import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
import { Provider } from "react-redux";
import { PersistGate } from "redux-persist/integration/react";
import { store, persistor } from "./app/store";
import "./index.css";
import App from "./App.jsx";
import "./i18n";

createRoot(document.getElementById("root")).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <Provider store={store}>
      <PersistGate
        loading={<div>Loading Session...</div>}
        persistor={persistor}
      >
        <App />
      </PersistGate>
    </Provider>
  </StrictMode>,
);


========================================
FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\app\store.js
========================================
import { configureStore, combineReducers } from "@reduxjs/toolkit";
import {
  persistStore,
  persistReducer,
  FLUSH,
  REHYDRATE,
  PAUSE,
  PERSIST,
  PURGE,
  REGISTER,
} from "redux-persist";
import storageModule from "redux-persist/lib/storage";
const storage = storageModule.default || storageModule;
import authReducer from "../features/auth/authSlice";
import queueReducer from "../features/queue/queueSlice";

const rootReducer = combineReducers({
  auth: authReducer,
  queue: queueReducer,
});

const persistConfig = {
  key: "root",
  version: 1,
  storage,
  whitelist: ["auth"], // Only persist the auth slice
};

const persistedReducer = persistReducer(persistConfig, rootReducer);

export const store = configureStore({
  reducer: persistedReducer,
  middleware: (getDefaultMiddleware) =>
    getDefaultMiddleware({
      serializableCheck: {
        ignoredActions: [FLUSH, REHYDRATE, PAUSE, PERSIST, PURGE, REGISTER],
      },
    }),
});

export const persistor = persistStore(store);

/**
 * Test utility to create a fresh store without persistence.
 * Prevents state leaks and avoids PersistGate issues in tests.
 */
export const setupStore = (preloadedState) => {
  return configureStore({
    reducer: rootReducer,
    preloadedState,
  });
};

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\components\Breadcrumbs.jsx
========================================
import { Link, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";

import Icon from "./Icon";

const SEGMENT_LABELS = {
  admin: "Admin",
  doctor: "Doctor",
  pharmacist: "Pharmacist",
  "super-admin": "Super Admin",
  dashboard: "Dashboard",
  queue: "Queue",
};

const Breadcrumbs = () => {
  const location = useLocation();

  const segments = location.pathname.split("/").filter(Boolean);

  if (segments.length === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  const crumbLabel = (segment) =>
    SEGMENT_LABELS[segment] ??
    segment
      .split(/[-_]/)
      .map((word) => word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1))
      .join(" ");

  return (
    <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="px-4 py-3 sm:px-6">
      <ol className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1 text-sm text-neutral-600">
        {segments.map((segment, index) => {
          const isLast = index === segments.length - 1;
          const path = `/${segments.slice(0, index + 1).join("/")}`;
          const label = crumbLabel(segment);

          return (
            <li key={path} className="flex items-center gap-1">
              {index > 0 && <Icon name="chevron-right" className="h-4 w-4" />}

              {isLast ? (
                <span
                  aria-current="page"
                  className="font-semibold text-neutral-900"
                >
                  {label}
                </span>
              ) : (
                <Link
                  to={path}
                  className="rounded px-1 hover:text-primary-700 hover:underline"
                >
                  {label}
                </Link>
              )}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>
    </nav>
  );
};

export default Breadcrumbs;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\components\Button.jsx
========================================
const VARIANTS = {
  primary:
    "bg-primary-600 text-white hover:bg-primary-700 focus-visible:ring-primary-500",
  secondary:
    "bg-neutral-100 text-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-200 focus-visible:ring-neutral-500",
  outline:
    "border border-neutral-300 bg-white text-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-50 focus-visible:ring-primary-500",
  danger:
    "bg-danger-600 text-white hover:bg-danger-700 focus-visible:ring-danger-500",
  // Emergency / caution actions (e.g. break-glass elevation). Uses the
  // approved on-warning-button pair: neutral-0 text on warning-800.
  warning:
    "bg-warning-800 text-neutral-0 hover:bg-warning-900 focus-visible:ring-warning-500",
  ghost:
    "bg-transparent text-neutral-800 hover:bg-neutral-100 focus-visible:ring-primary-500",
};

const SIZES = {
  sm: "px-3 py-1.5 text-sm",
  md: "px-4 py-2 text-sm",
  lg: "px-5 py-3 text-base",
};

const Button = ({
  children,
  type = "button",
  variant = "primary",
  size = "md",
  disabled = false,
  loading = false,
  className = "",
  onClick,
  ...rest
}) => {
  const selectedVariant = VARIANTS[variant] ?? VARIANTS.primary;
  const selectedSize = SIZES[size] ?? SIZES.md;

  return (
    <button
      {...rest}
      type={type}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      onClick={onClick}
      aria-busy={loading || undefined}
      className={[
        "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-md font-medium",
        "transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2",
        "focus-visible:ring-offset-2 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50",
        selectedVariant,
        selectedSize,
        className,
      ].join(" ")}
    >
      {loading && (
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-current border-r-transparent"
        />
      )}
      {children}
    </button>
  );
};

export default Button;

import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import Modal from "./Modal.jsx";
import Button from "./Button.jsx";

const ConfirmDialog = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onConfirm,
  title,
  message,
  confirmLabel,
  cancelLabel,
  variant = "danger",
  loading = false,
}) => {
  const { t } = useTranslation();

  const resolvedConfirmLabel = confirmLabel ?? t("common.confirm");
  const resolvedCancelLabel = cancelLabel ?? t("common.cancel");

  const handleConfirm = () => {
    if (!loading) {
      onConfirm?.();
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={onClose} title={title}>
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600">{message}</p>

        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            onClick={onClose}
            disabled={loading}
          >
            {resolvedCancelLabel}
          </Button>

          <Button
            type="button"
            variant={variant}
            onClick={handleConfirm}
            loading={loading}
          >
            {resolvedConfirmLabel}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default ConfirmDialog;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\components\DatePicker.jsx
========================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";

const DatePicker = ({
  id,
  name,
  label,
  value,
  defaultValue = "",
  onChange,
  onBlur,
  required = false,
  disabled = false,
  min,
  max,
  error,
  submitted = false,
  className = "",
  ...rest
}) => {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [touched, setTouched] = useState(false);
  const [internalValue, setInternalValue] = useState(defaultValue);

  const dateValue = value !== undefined ? value : internalValue;

  const validationMessage =
    error ||
    (required && !String(dateValue).trim() ? t("validation.required") : "");

  const showError = Boolean(validationMessage) && (touched || submitted);
  const dateId = id ?? name;
  const errorId = `${dateId}-error`;

  const handleChange = (event) => {
    if (value === undefined) {
      setInternalValue(event.target.value);
    }
    onChange?.(event);
  };

  const handleBlur = (event) => {
    setTouched(true);
    onBlur?.(event);
  };

  return (
    <div className={`flex flex-col gap-1 ${className}`}>
      <label htmlFor={dateId} className="text-sm font-medium text-neutral-800">
        {label}
        {required && (
          <span aria-hidden="true" className="ml-1">
            *
          </span>
        )}
      </label>

      <input
        {...rest}
        id={dateId}
        name={name}
        type="date"
        value={dateValue}
        onChange={handleChange}
        onBlur={handleBlur}
        required={required}
        disabled={disabled}
        min={min}
        max={max}
        aria-invalid={showError}
        aria-describedby={showError ? errorId : undefined}
        className={`w-full rounded-md border px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-neutral-100 ${
          showError ? "border-danger-600" : "border-neutral-300"
        }`}
      />

      {showError && (
        <p id={errorId} role="alert" className="text-sm text-danger-700">
          {validationMessage}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default DatePicker;

const ErrorMessage = ({
  title,
  message,
  children,
  role = "alert",
  className = "",
}) => {
  const content = message ?? children;

  if (!title && !content) return null;

  return (
    <div
      role={role}
      className={`rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-red-800 ${className}`}
    >
      {title && <h2 className="mb-1 text-sm font-semibold">{title}</h2>}

      {content && <p className="text-sm">{content}</p>}
    </div>
  );
};

export default ErrorMessage;

  ...MEDICAL_ICONS,
  ...UI_ICONS,
  ...AUTH_ICONS,
};

/**
 * Shared icon component.
 *
 * @param {object} props
 * @param {string} props.name - Registry key.
 * @param {string} [props.label] - Accessible name for meaningful icons.
 * @param {string|number} [props.size="1em"] - Icon dimensions.
 * @param {string} [props.className=""] - CSS classes.
 */
const Icon = ({ name, label, size = "1em", className = "", ...rest }) => {
  const Glyph = ICONS[name] ?? FALLBACK_ICON;

  const accessibilityProps = label
    ? {
        role: "img",
        "aria-label": label,
      }
    : {
        "aria-hidden": true,
        focusable: false,
      };

  return (
    <Glyph
      {...rest}
      {...accessibilityProps}
      size={size}
      className={className}
    />
  );
};

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\components\IconButton.jsx

import { useEffect } from "react";

import Button from "./Button";
import Icon from "./Icon";

/**
 * CFG-07 â€” icon-only button (AC2, SDS Â§2.4.10).
 * A button whose only content is a decorative icon has no text for a
 * screen reader to announce, so the accessible name must come from an
 * ARIA label. This component makes that mandatory: `label` is a required
 * prop and is applied as `aria-label`; in development a missing label
 * logs an error so the mistake is caught before release. The a11y unit
 * tests also assert, via axe-core, that an unlabelled icon-only button
 * fails the `button-name` check while an IconButton passes it.
 *
 * Focus visibility comes from the shared Button (`:focus-visible` ring),
 * and the default padding keeps a â‰¥ 44Ã—44 px touch target (WCAG 2.5.5).
 */
const IconButton = ({
  icon,
  label,
  variant = "ghost",
  size = "md",
  disabled = false,
  loading = false,
  className = "",
  onClick,
  ...rest
}) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!label && import.meta.env?.DEV) {
      console.error(
        "IconButton requires a `label` prop so icon-only buttons expose an accessible name (CFG-07 AC2).",
      );
    }
  }, [label]);

  return (
    <Button
      {...rest}
      variant={variant}
      size={size}
      disabled={disabled}
      loading={loading}
      onClick={onClick}
      aria-label={label}
      className={["!p-2.5", className].join(" ")}
    >
      <Icon name={icon} className="h-5 w-5" />
    </Button>
  );
};

export default IconButton;


========================================
FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\components\Input.jsx
========================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";

const Input = ({
  id,
  name,
  label,
  type = "text",
  value,
  defaultValue,
  onChange,
  onBlur,
  required = false,
  disabled = false,
  placeholder,
  validate,
  error,
  submitted = false,
  hint,
  className = "",
  leftIcon,
  rightIcon,
  ...rest
}) => {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [touched, setTouched] = useState(false);
  const [internalValue, setInternalValue] = useState(defaultValue ?? "");

  const inputValue = value !== undefined ? value : internalValue;

  const validationMessage = (() => {
    if (error) return error;

    if (required && !String(inputValue ?? "").trim()) {
      return t("validation.required");
    }

    if (validate) {
      return validate(inputValue);
    }

    return "";
  })();

  const showError = Boolean(validationMessage) && (touched || submitted);
  const inputId = id ?? name;
  const errorId = `${inputId}-error`;
  const hintId = `${inputId}-hint`;

  const handleChange = (event) => {
    const nextValue = event.target.value;

    if (value === undefined) {
      setInternalValue(nextValue);
    }

    onChange?.(event);
  };

  const handleBlur = (event) => {
    setTouched(true);
    onBlur?.(event);
  };

  const hasLeftIcon = Boolean(leftIcon);
  const hasRightIcon = Boolean(rightIcon);

  return (
    <div className={`flex flex-col gap-1 ${className}`}>
      <label htmlFor={inputId} className="...">
        <span>{label}</span>
        {required && (
          <span aria-hidden="true" className="ml-1">
            *
          </span>
        )}
      </label>

      <div className="relative">
        {hasLeftIcon && (
          <div
            className="pointer-events-none absolute left-4 top-1/2 h-5 w-5 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400"
            aria-hidden="true"
          >
            {leftIcon}
          </div>
        )}

        <input
          {...rest}
          id={inputId}
          name={name}
          type={type}
          value={inputValue}
          onChange={handleChange}
          onBlur={handleBlur}
          disabled={disabled}
          required={required}
          placeholder={placeholder}
          aria-invalid={showError}
          aria-describedby={
            [hint ? hintId : null, showError ? errorId : null]
              .filter(Boolean)
              .join(" ") || undefined
          }
          className={`w-full rounded-xl border bg-slate-50/70 py-3.5 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-4 focus:ring-teal-600/10 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-neutral-100 transition placeholder:text-slate-400 hover:border-slate-300 focus:border-teal-600 focus:bg-white ${
            hasLeftIcon ? "pl-12" : "pl-4"
          } ${hasRightIcon ? "pr-12" : "pr-4"} ${
            showError ? "border-danger-600" : "border-slate-200"
          }`}
        />

        {hasRightIcon && (
          <div className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 flex h-9 w-9 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center">
            {rightIcon}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {hint && (
        <p id={hintId} className="text-sm text-neutral-600">
          {hint}
        </p>
      )}

      {showError && (
        <p id={errorId} role="alert" className="text-sm text-danger-700">
          {validationMessage}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default Input;

import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";

export default function LanguageSwitcher() {
  const { i18n } = useTranslation();

  const changeLanguage = async (language) => {
    await i18n.changeLanguage(language);
    localStorage.setItem("language", language);
    document.documentElement.lang = language;
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <label htmlFor="language-select">{i18n.t("common.language")}</label>

      <select
        id="language-select"
        value={i18n.resolvedLanguage?.startsWith("ta") ? "ta" : "en"}
        onChange={(event) => changeLanguage(event.target.value)}
      >
        <option value="en">English</option>
        <option value="ta">à®¤à®®à®¿à®´à¯</option>
      </select>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\components\Modal.jsx
========================================
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";

import Icon from "./Icon";

const Modal = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  title,
  children,
  closeLabel,
  className = "",
}) => {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const resolvedCloseLabel = closeLabel ?? t("common.close");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return undefined;

    const handleKeyDown = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        onClose?.();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4"
      onMouseDown={(event) => {
        if (event.target === event.currentTarget) {
          onClose?.();
        }
      }}
    >
      <section
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="modal-title"
        className={`w-full max-w-lg rounded-lg bg-white p-6 shadow-xl ${className}`}
      >
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <h2 id="modal-title" className="text-lg font-semibold">
            {title}
          </h2>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label={resolvedCloseLabel}
            className="rounded p-2 text-neutral-600 hover:bg-neutral-100 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary-500"
          >
            <Icon name="close" className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div>{children}</div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
};

import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import Button from "./Button.jsx";

const Pagination = ({
  currentPage,
  totalPages,
  onPageChange,
  disabled = false,
  className = "",
}) => {
  const { t } = useTranslation();

  if (totalPages <= 0) return null;

  const page = Math.min(Math.max(1, currentPage), totalPages);

  return (
    <nav
      aria-label={`${t("pagination.page")} ${page} ${t("pagination.of")} ${totalPages}`}
      className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-4 ${className}`}
    >
      <Button
        variant="outline"
        size="sm"
        disabled={disabled || page <= 1}
        onClick={() => onPageChange(page - 1)}
      >
        {t("pagination.previous")}
      </Button>

      <span className="text-sm text-neutral-700" aria-live="polite">
        {t("pagination.page")} {page} {t("pagination.of")} {totalPages}
      </span>

      <Button
        variant="outline"
        size="sm"
        disabled={disabled || page >= totalPages}
        onClick={() => onPageChange(page + 1)}
      >
        {t("pagination.next")}
      </Button>
    </nav>
  );
};

export default Pagination;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\components\SearchBar.jsx
========================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import Button from "./Button.jsx";

const SearchBar = ({
  value,
  defaultValue = "",
  onChange,
  onSearch,
  placeholder,
  searchLabel,
  clearLabel,
  disabled = false,
  className = "",
}) => {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [internalValue, setInternalValue] = useState(defaultValue);

  const searchValue = value !== undefined ? value : internalValue;
  const resolvedSearchLabel = searchLabel ?? t("common.search");
  const resolvedClearLabel = clearLabel ?? t("common.clear");

  const handleChange = (event) => {
    if (value === undefined) {
      setInternalValue(event.target.value);
    }
    onChange?.(event.target.value);
  };

  const handleClear = () => {
    if (value === undefined) {
      setInternalValue("");
    }
    onChange?.("");
    onSearch?.("");
  };

  const handleSubmit = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    onSearch?.(searchValue);
  };

  return (
    <form
      role="search"
      onSubmit={handleSubmit}
      className={`flex items-center gap-2 ${className}`}
    >
      <label htmlFor="component-search" className="sr-only">
        {resolvedSearchLabel}
      </label>

      <input
        id="component-search"
        type="search"
        value={searchValue}
        onChange={handleChange}
        placeholder={placeholder ?? resolvedSearchLabel}
        disabled={disabled}
        className="min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-md border border-neutral-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:bg-neutral-100"
      />

      {searchValue && (
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={handleClear}
          disabled={disabled}
        >
          {resolvedClearLabel}
        </Button>
      )}

      <Button type="submit" disabled={disabled}>
        {resolvedSearchLabel}
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
};

export default SearchBar;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\components\Select.jsx
========================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";

const Select = ({
  id,
  name,
  label,
  options = [],
  value,
  defaultValue = "",
  onChange,
  onBlur,
  required = false,
  disabled = false,
  placeholder,
  error,
  submitted = false,
  className = "",
  ...rest
}) => {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [touched, setTouched] = useState(false);
  const [internalValue, setInternalValue] = useState(defaultValue);

  const selectValue = value !== undefined ? value : internalValue;
  const validationMessage =
    error ||
    (required && !String(selectValue).trim() ? t("validation.required") : "");

  const showError = Boolean(validationMessage) && (touched || submitted);
  const selectId = id ?? name;
  const errorId = `${selectId}-error`;

  const handleChange = (event) => {
    if (value === undefined) {
      setInternalValue(event.target.value);
    }
    onChange?.(event);
  };

  const handleBlur = (event) => {
    setTouched(true);
    onBlur?.(event);
  };

  return (
    <div className={`flex flex-col gap-1 ${className}`}>
      <label
        htmlFor={selectId}
        className="text-sm font-medium text-neutral-800"
      >
        {label}
        {required && (
          <span aria-hidden="true" className="ml-1">
            *
          </span>
        )}
      </label>

      <select
        {...rest}
        id={selectId}
        name={name}
        value={selectValue}
        onChange={handleChange}
        onBlur={handleBlur}
        required={required}
        disabled={disabled}
        aria-invalid={showError}
        aria-describedby={showError ? errorId : undefined}
        className={`w-full rounded-md border px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-neutral-100 ${
          showError ? "border-danger-600" : "border-neutral-300"
        }`}
      >
        <option value="">{placeholder ?? label}</option>

        {options.map((option) => (
          <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
            {option.label}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>

      {showError && (
        <p id={errorId} role="alert" className="text-sm text-danger-700">
          {validationMessage}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default Select;

const STATUS_STYLES = {
  success: {
    surface: "bg-success-100 text-success-800 border-success-200",
    icon: LuCheck,
  },
  warning: {
    surface: "bg-warning-100 text-warning-900 border-warning-200",

    icon: LuX,
  },
  info: {
    surface: "bg-primary-100 text-primary-800 border-primary-200",
    icon: LuInfo,
  },
  neutral: {
    surface: "bg-neutral-100 text-neutral-800 border-neutral-200",
    icon: LuCircleHelp,
  },
};

const STATUS_MAP = {
  scheduled: {
    variant: "info",
    label: "Scheduled",
    icon: LuCalendarClock,
  },
  completed: {
    variant: "success",
    label: "Completed",
    icon: LuCheck,

    label: "Near expiry",
    icon: LuCalendarClock,
  },
  expired: {
    variant: "danger",
    label: "Expired (unavailable)",
    icon: LuX,
  },
};

const StatusBadge = ({ status = "neutral", children }) => {
  const normalizedStatus = String(status).trim().toLowerCase();
  const mappedStatus = STATUS_MAP[normalizedStatus];

  // Preserve support for callers that pass a legacy style variant.

import EmptyState from "./EmptyState.jsx";

/**
 * CFG-07 â€” wide tables scroll inside their own container (AC3, NFRU-004).
 * `overflow-x-auto` alone is not enough at 360 px: the scrollable region
 * must also be reachable and named for keyboard / screen-reader users
 * (SDS Â§2.4.10), so the wrapper is a labelled `region` with `tabIndex=0`.
 * The page itself never scrolls sideways because the overflow is contained
 * here.
 */
const Table = ({
  columns = [],
  data = [],
  getRowKey,
  emptyTitle,
  emptyMessage,
  label = "Data table",
  className = "",
}) => {
  if (data.length === 0) {
    return <EmptyState title={emptyTitle} message={emptyMessage} />;
  }

  return (
    <div
      role="region"
      aria-label={label}
      tabIndex={0}
      className={`w-full overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary-600 ${className}`}
    >
      <table className="w-full border-collapse text-left text-sm">
        <thead className="bg-neutral-50">
          <tr>
            {columns.map((column) => (
              <th
                key={column.key}
                scope="col"
                className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-neutral-700"
              >
                {column.header}
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>

        <tbody className="divide-y divide-neutral-200">
          {data.map((row, rowIndex) => (
            <tr
              key={getRowKey ? getRowKey(row) : (row.id ?? rowIndex)}
              className="hover:bg-neutral-50"
            >
              {columns.map((column) => (
                <td
                  key={column.key}
                  className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-neutral-700"
                >
                  {column.render
                    ? column.render(row)
                    : (row[column.key] ?? "â€”")}
                </td>
              ))}
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Table;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\components\Tabs.jsx
========================================
import { useId, useState } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";

const Tabs = ({
  tabs = [],
  defaultActiveTab,
  activeTab,
  onChange,
  ariaLabel,
  className = "",
}) => {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const generatedId = useId();

  const [internalActiveTab, setInternalActiveTab] = useState(
    defaultActiveTab ?? tabs[0]?.id,
  );

  const selectedTab = activeTab ?? internalActiveTab;
  const activeTabExists = tabs.some((tab) => tab.id === selectedTab);
  const currentTab = activeTabExists ? selectedTab : tabs[0]?.id;

  if (tabs.length === 0) return null;

  const handleTabChange = (tabId) => {
    if (activeTab === undefined) {
      setInternalActiveTab(tabId);
    }
    onChange?.(tabId);
  };

  const activeTabData = tabs.find((tab) => tab.id === currentTab);
  const resolvedAriaLabel = ariaLabel ?? t("common.tabs");

  return (
    <div className={className}>
      <div
        role="tablist"
        aria-label={resolvedAriaLabel}
        className="flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto border-b border-neutral-200"
      >
        {tabs.map((tab) => {
          const isActive = tab.id === currentTab;
          const tabId = `${generatedId}-tab-${tab.id}`;
          const panelId = `${generatedId}-panel-${tab.id}`;

          return (
            <button
              key={tab.id}
              id={tabId}
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={isActive}
              aria-controls={panelId}
              tabIndex={isActive ? 0 : -1}
              onClick={() => handleTabChange(tab.id)}
              className={[
                "whitespace-nowrap border-b-2 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium",
                "focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary-500",
                isActive
                  ? "border-primary-600 text-primary-700"
                  : "border-transparent text-neutral-600 hover:text-neutral-900",
              ].join(" ")}
            >
              {tab.label}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div
        id={`${generatedId}-panel-${currentTab}`}
        role="tabpanel"
        aria-labelledby={`${generatedId}-tab-${currentTab}`}
        tabIndex={0}
        className="py-4 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary-500"
      >
        {activeTabData?.content}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Tabs;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\components\Toast.jsx
========================================
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";

import Icon from "./Icon";

const TOAST_STYLES = {
  success: "border-green-200 bg-green-50 text-green-800",
  error: "border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-800",
  warning: "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-900",
  info: "border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 text-blue-800",
};

const Toast = ({
  message,
  type = "info",
  onClose,
  duration = 0,
  closeLabel,
  className = "",
}) => {
  const { t } = useTranslation();

  const resolvedCloseLabel = closeLabel ?? t("common.dismiss");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!message || duration <= 0 || !onClose) return undefined;

    const timeoutId = setTimeout(onClose, duration);

    return () => clearTimeout(timeoutId);
  }, [message, duration, onClose]);

  if (!message) return null;

  const styles = TOAST_STYLES[type] ?? TOAST_STYLES.info;
  const role = type === "error" ? "alert" : "status";

  return (
    <div
      role={role}
      aria-live={type === "error" ? "assertive" : "polite"}
      className={`flex items-start justify-between gap-4 rounded-md border p-4 text-sm ${styles} ${className}`}
    >
      <p>{message}</p>

      {onClose && (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label={resolvedCloseLabel}
          className="shrink-0 rounded px-2 py-1 font-medium hover:bg-black/5 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-current"
        >
          <Icon name="close" className="h-4 w-4" />
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default Toast;

import { useDispatch, useSelector } from "react-redux";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import Button from "./Button";
import Icon from "./Icon";
import Logo from "./Logo";
import {
  clearSession,
  selectRole,
  selectUser,
} from "../features/auth/authSlice";
import { dashboardPathForRole } from "../constants/roles";
import { logout } from "../services/authService";

const TopBar = () => {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const user = useSelector(selectUser);
  const role = useSelector(selectRole);

  const displayName = user ?? user?.email ?? "Signed in as";

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    try {
      await logout();
    } catch {
      // Continue local logout even if the API fails.
    } finally {
      dispatch(clearSession());
      navigate("/login", { replace: true });
    }
  };

  return (
    <header className="border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-0">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 px-4 py-3 sm:px-6">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => navigate(dashboardPathForRole(role))}
          className="rounded-2xl"
          aria-label="Home"
        >
          <Logo size="sm" showWordmark={false} />
        </button>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <span className="hidden text-sm text-neutral-600 sm:inline">
            {displayName}
          </span>

          <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => void handleLogout()}>
            <Icon name="log-out" className="h-4 w-4" />
            Log out
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
};

export default TopBar;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\components\doctor\Modal.jsx
========================================
import { useEffect, useId, useRef } from "react";

import Icon from "../Icon";

const FOCUSABLE =
  'a[href], button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';

/**
 * Accessible dialog: Escape and backdrop click close it, focus moves in on
 * open, is kept inside while open and returns to the trigger on close.
 * `onClose` does not need to be memoised.
 */
const Modal = ({ title, onClose, children, widthClass = "max-w-lg" }) => {
  const titleId = useId();
  const dialogRef = useRef(null);
  const onCloseRef = useRef(onClose);

  useEffect(() => {
    onCloseRef.current = onClose;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const dialog = dialogRef.current;
    const previouslyFocused = document.activeElement;
    const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    (
      dialog.querySelector("[data-autofocus], input, select, textarea") ??
      dialog
    ).focus();

    const handleKeyDown = (event) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        onCloseRef.current();
        return;
      }
      if (event.key !== "Tab") return;
      const nodes = dialog.querySelectorAll(FOCUSABLE);
      if (nodes.length === 0) return;
      const first = nodes[0];
      const last = nodes[nodes.length - 1];
      if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
        event.preventDefault();
        last.focus();
      } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
        event.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
      document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
      previouslyFocused?.focus?.();
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-neutral-950/40 p-4"
      onMouseDown={(event) => {
        if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose();
      }}
    >
      <div
        ref={dialogRef}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby={titleId}
        tabIndex={-1}
        className={`flex max-h-[90vh] w-full flex-col rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white shadow-xl outline-none ${widthClass}`}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-neutral-100 px-5 py-4">
          <h2 id={titleId} className="text-base font-semibold text-neutral-900">
            {title}
          </h2>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close dialog"
            className="rounded-lg p-1 text-neutral-500 transition-colors hover:bg-neutral-100 hover:text-neutral-900 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary-600"
          >
            <Icon name="close" className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="overflow-y-auto p-5">{children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Modal;

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

import Icon from "../Icon";

/** One page width and rhythm for every doctor screen. */
export const PageShell = ({ children, className = "" }) => (
  <div
    className={`mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl space-y-6 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 ${className}`}
  >
    {children}
  </div>
);

/**
 * Shared page header: optional back link, optional status line, title,
 * description, meta row and an actions slot. `back` is `{ to, label }`.
 */
export const PageHeader = ({
  back,
  eyebrow,
  title,
  description,
  meta,
  actions,
}) => (
  <header className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-b border-neutral-200 pb-6 md:flex-row md:items-end md:justify-between">
    <div className="min-w-0 space-y-2">
      {back && (
        <Link
          to={back.to}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-neutral-600 transition-colors hover:text-primary-700"
        >
          <Icon name="arrow-left" className="h-4 w-4" />
          <span>{back.label}</span>
        </Link>
      )}
      {eyebrow && (
        <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-700">
          <span
            aria-hidden="true"
            className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-primary-600 motion-safe:animate-pulse"
          />
          {eyebrow}
        </p>
      )}
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-neutral-950 sm:text-3xl">
        {title}
      </h1>
      {description && (
        <p className="max-w-2xl text-sm text-neutral-600">{description}</p>
      )}
      {meta && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-1 text-sm text-neutral-700">
          {meta}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
    {actions && (
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">{actions}</div>
    )}
  </header>
);

import { useId } from "react";

import Button from "../Button";
import ErrorMessage from "../ErrorMessage";
import Icon from "../Icon";

/** Heading for a section that is not wrapped in a Panel (tables, stat grids). */
export const SectionTitle = ({ id, children, aside }) => (
  <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
    <h2 id={id} className="text-base font-semibold text-neutral-900">
      {children}
    </h2>
    {aside}
  </div>
);

/** The single card style used across the doctor module. */
export const Panel = ({
  icon,
  title,
  titleId,
  aside,
  children,
  padded = true,
  className = "",
  bodyClassName = "space-y-4",
}) => {
  const generatedId = useId();
  const headingId = titleId ?? generatedId;

  return (
    <section
      aria-labelledby={title ? headingId : undefined}
      className={`overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white shadow-xs ${className}`}
    >
      {title && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-neutral-100 px-5 py-4">
          <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
            {icon && (
              <Icon name={icon} className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-primary-600" />
            )}
            <h2
              id={headingId}
              className="truncate text-base font-semibold text-neutral-900"
            >
              {title}
            </h2>
          </div>
          {aside}
        </div>
      )}
      <div className={`${padded ? "p-5" : ""} ${bodyClassName}`}>
        {children}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

/** Label/value pair. Render inside a <dl>. */
export const DetailTile = ({ label, children, className = "" }) => (
  <div
    className={`min-w-0 rounded-xl border border-neutral-100 bg-neutral-50 p-3 ${className}`}
  >
    <dt className="text-xs font-medium text-neutral-500">{label}</dt>
    <dd className="mt-1 truncate text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-900">
      {children}
    </dd>
  </div>
);

/** Loading placeholder exposed to assistive tech as a named status region. */
export const SkeletonBlock = ({ label, rows = 3, className = "" }) => (
  <div
    role="status"
    aria-label={label}
    aria-busy="true"
    className={`space-y-3 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-xs motion-safe:animate-pulse ${className}`}
  >
    {Array.from({ length: rows }, (_, index) => (
      <div
        key={index}
        className="h-4 rounded bg-neutral-100"
        style={{ width: `${Math.max(40, 92 - index * 14)}%` }}
      />
    ))}
  </div>
);

/** Error card with an optional retry-style action. */
export const ErrorPanel = ({
  title,
  message,
  actionLabel,
  actionIcon,
  onAction,
  actionLoading = false,
  children,
}) => (
  <div className="space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-xs">
    <ErrorMessage title={title} message={message} />
    {children}
    {onAction && (
      <Button
        onClick={onAction}
        loading={actionLoading}
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-2"
      >
        {actionIcon && <Icon name={actionIcon} className="h-4 w-4" />}
        {actionLabel}
      </Button>
    )}
  </div>
);

import { ROLES } from "./roles";

/**
 * CFG-03 â€” permission catalog (SDS Â§2.5.2, permission matrix Table 9).
 *
 * Catalog metadata (code, human name, description, domain) plus the
 * factory defaults the backend ships with, mirroring
 * `com.onecare.backend.security.Permission` / `RolePermission`.
 *
 * Runtime grants are configurable and now come from the role
 * permissions API â€” GET/PUT /api/roles/{role}/permissions (FR003/UC3,
 * see src/services/rolePermissionService.js). The `granted` lists below
 * are only the seeded defaults (used by MSW and pinned by tests); the
 * Access Control screen renders live sets from the API.
 *
 * `privileged: true` marks scopes the backend reserves for SUPER_ADMIN
 * (rule BR004). Permissions defined but granted to no default role
 * (USER_READ_OWN, AUDIT_READ, REPORT_GENERATE) stay visible so the
 * matrix never hides a scope.
 */

/** Human-readable domains, in display order. */
export const PERMISSION_CATEGORIES = Object.freeze([

]);

  PERMISSION_MATRIX.map((entry) => entry.code),

/** True when `role` holds `code` in the static backend mirror. */
export const hasPermission = (granted, role) =>
  Array.isArray(granted) && granted.includes(role);

/**
 * Doctor working schedules (FR009, UC4).
 *
 * Day enum order and time helpers shared by the clinic-configuration
 * screen, the schedule service and the MSW mock so validation never
 * drifts between them. Times are zero-padded 24h `HH:mm` strings â€” the
 * wire format of /api/doctors/{doctorId}/schedule.
 */

/** Day enum in declaration order (used to keep selections sorted). */
export const WORKING_DAYS = Object.freeze([
  "MONDAY",
  "TUESDAY",
  "WEDNESDAY",
  "THURSDAY",
  "FRIDAY",
  "SATURDAY",
  "SUNDAY",
]);

/** Short display labels keyed by enum name. */
export const DAY_LABELS = Object.freeze({
  MONDAY: "Mon",
  TUESDAY: "Tue",
  WEDNESDAY: "Wed",
  THURSDAY: "Thu",
  FRIDAY: "Fri",
  SATURDAY: "Sat",
  SUNDAY: "Sun",
});

/** Zero-padded 24h clock time, e.g. 09:00 or 17:30. */
export const TIME_PATTERN = /^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/;

export const isValidTime = (value) => TIME_PATTERN.test(String(value ?? ""));

/** Pad a single-digit hour ("9:00" â†’ "09:00"); other input passes through. */
export const normalizeTime = (value) => {
  const text = String(value ?? "").trim();
  const match = /^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/.exec(text);
  if (!match) return text;
  return `${match[1].padStart(2, "0")}:${match[2]}`;
};

/**
 * The first overlapping pair (UC4 alt flow), e.g. 09:00â€“12:00 vs
 * 11:00â€“13:00. Adjacent slots (â€¦â€“12:00 and 12:00â€“â€¦) do not overlap.
 * Expects zero-padded HH:mm strings (normalizeTime first).
 */
export const findConflictingSlots = (timeSlots) => {
  for (let i = 0; i < timeSlots.length; i += 1) {
    for (let j = i + 1; j < timeSlots.length; j += 1) {
      const first = timeSlots[i];
      const second = timeSlots[j];
      if (first.start < second.end && second.start < first.end) {
        return [first, second];
      }
    }
  }
  return [];
};

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\features\auth\authSlice.js
========================================
import { createSlice } from "@reduxjs/toolkit";

/**
 * CFG-02 auth feature â€” pure Redux (in-memory), no persistence layer.
 * The session therefore ends on a page reload; the login ticket owns
 * re-authentication. Tokens are never written to storage or logged.
 */
const initialState = {
  accessToken: null,
  refreshToken: null,
  expiresAt: null,

  user: null,
  role: null,
  authStatus: "unauthenticated",
  redirectUrl: null,
};

const authSlice = createSlice({
  name: "auth",
  initialState,
  reducers: {
    setSession(state, action) {
      const { accessToken, refreshToken, expiresIn, user, role, redirectUrl } =
        action.payload ?? {};

      // A payload without an access token is not a session â€” ignore it
      // entirely so we never store a refresh token (or role) with no access
      // token to use it with.
      if (!accessToken) {
        return;
      }

      state.accessToken = accessToken;

      if (refreshToken) {
        state.refreshToken = refreshToken;
      }

      if (expiresIn != null) {
        const seconds = Number(expiresIn);

        state.expiresAt =
          Number.isFinite(seconds) && seconds > 0
            ? Date.now() + seconds * 1000
            : null;
      }

      if (user !== undefined) {
        state.user = user;
      }

      if (role) {
        state.role = role;
      }

      if (redirectUrl) {
        state.redirectUrl = redirectUrl;
      }

      state.authStatus = "authenticated";
    },

    clearSession(state) {
      state.accessToken = null;
      state.refreshToken = null;
      state.expiresAt = null;
      state.user = null;
      state.role = null;
      state.authStatus = "unauthenticated";
      state.redirectUrl = null;
    },
  },
});

export const { setSession, clearSession } = authSlice.actions;

export const selectAccessToken = (state) => state.auth.accessToken;

export const selectRefreshToken = (state) => state.auth.refreshToken;

export const selectExpiresAt = (state) => state.auth.expiresAt;

export const selectRole = (state) => state.auth.role;

export const selectUser = (state) => state.auth.user;

export const selectAuthStatus = (state) => state.auth.authStatus;

export const selectRedirectUrl = (state) => state.auth.redirectUrl;

export const selectIsAuthenticated = (state) =>
  state.auth.authStatus === "authenticated" && Boolean(state.auth.accessToken);

export default authSlice.reducer;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\features\auth\sessionActivity.js
========================================
const ACTIVE_USER_WINDOW_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;

let lastActivityAt = Date.now();

export const recordUserActivity = () => {
  lastActivityAt = Date.now();
};

export const isUserActive = () => {
  return Date.now() - lastActivityAt < ACTIVE_USER_WINDOW_MS;
};

/**
 * Whether the JWT may be extended right now.
 *
 * The inactivity logout above still uses `isUserActive` (mouse/keyboard), but
 * the *token* is refreshed whenever the document is visible. A clinician
 * reading a chart without touching the mouse must not be logged out by an
 * expiring JWT, so visibility â€” not pointer movement â€” is the gate here.
 *
 * A hidden tab is never refreshed: the browser throttles its timers anyway and
 * the user is presumed away. On returning, the next scheduler tick (at most
 * 60 s later) picks the session back up â€” and any call made in between is
 * covered by the reactive 401 refresh.
 */
export const isTabVisible = () => {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return true;
  return document.visibilityState !== "hidden";
};


========================================
FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\features\auth\SessionManager.jsx
========================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useSelector } from "react-redux";

import { clearSession, selectIsAuthenticated } from "./authSlice";

import { store } from "../../app/store";
import { logout } from "../../services/authService";

import {
  startTokenRefreshScheduler,
  SESSION_EXPIRED_EVENT,
} from "../../services/apiClient";

import { isTabVisible, recordUserActivity } from "./sessionActivity";

const SESSION_TIMEOUT_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000;
const WARNING_BEFORE_LOGOUT_MS = 2 * 60 * 1000;
const WARNING_AFTER_MS = SESSION_TIMEOUT_MS - WARNING_BEFORE_LOGOUT_MS;

const ACTIVITY_EVENTS = [
  "mousedown",
  "keydown",
  "scroll",
  "touchstart",
  "click",
  "mousemove",
];

const SessionManager = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const isAuthenticated = useSelector(selectIsAuthenticated);

  const [showWarning, setShowWarning] = useState(false);
  const [remainingSeconds, setRemainingSeconds] = useState(120);

  const warningTimerRef = useRef(null);
  const logoutTimerRef = useRef(null);
  const countdownTimerRef = useRef(null);
  const resetTimersRef = useRef(null);

  const warningVisibleRef = useRef(false);
  const loggingOutRef = useRef(false);

  const clearTimers = useCallback(() => {
    window.clearTimeout(warningTimerRef.current);
    window.clearTimeout(logoutTimerRef.current);
    window.clearInterval(countdownTimerRef.current);

    warningTimerRef.current = null;
    logoutTimerRef.current = null;
    countdownTimerRef.current = null;
  }, []);

  const endSession = useCallback(
    async (reason = "session-expired") => {
      if (loggingOutRef.current) return;

      loggingOutRef.current = true;

      clearTimers();
      warningVisibleRef.current = false;
      setShowWarning(false);

      try {
        await logout();
      } catch {
        // Continue local logout even if the API fails.
      } finally {
        store.dispatch(clearSession());

        navigate(`/login?reason=${reason}`, {
          replace: true,
        });

        loggingOutRef.current = false;
      }
    },
    [clearTimers, navigate],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAuthenticated) {
      clearTimers();
      warningVisibleRef.current = false;
      resetTimersRef.current = null;

      // Do not call setState synchronously inside this effect.
      return undefined;
    }

    recordUserActivity();
    loggingOutRef.current = false;

    const resetTimers = () => {
      clearTimers();

      warningTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
        warningVisibleRef.current = true;
        setShowWarning(true);

        setRemainingSeconds(WARNING_BEFORE_LOGOUT_MS / 1000);

        countdownTimerRef.current = window.setInterval(() => {
          setRemainingSeconds((seconds) => Math.max(0, seconds - 1));
        }, 1000);
      }, WARNING_AFTER_MS);

      logoutTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
        void endSession("session-expired");
      }, SESSION_TIMEOUT_MS);
    };

    resetTimersRef.current = resetTimers;

    const handleActivity = () => {
      if (loggingOutRef.current) return;

      recordUserActivity();

      if (warningVisibleRef.current) {
        warningVisibleRef.current = false;
        setShowWarning(false);
      }

      resetTimers();
    };

    ACTIVITY_EVENTS.forEach((eventName) => {
      window.addEventListener(eventName, handleActivity, {
        passive: true,
      });
    });

    resetTimers();

    // The token is refreshed whenever the tab is visible, so an idle-but-present
    // user is never logged out by an expiring JWT. The 30-minute inactivity
    // logout above is a separate control and is unaffected by this.
    const stopScheduler = startTokenRefreshScheduler({
      canExtendSession: isTabVisible,
      debug: Boolean(import.meta.env?.DEV),
    });

    return () => {
      ACTIVITY_EVENTS.forEach((eventName) => {
        window.removeEventListener(eventName, handleActivity);
      });

      clearTimers();
      stopScheduler();
      resetTimersRef.current = null;
    };
  }, [isAuthenticated, clearTimers, endSession]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleSessionExpired = () => {
      warningVisibleRef.current = false;
      clearTimers();
      setShowWarning(false);

      if (window.location.pathname !== "/login") {
        navigate("/login?reason=session-expired", {
          replace: true,
        });
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener(SESSION_EXPIRED_EVENT, handleSessionExpired);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener(SESSION_EXPIRED_EVENT, handleSessionExpired);
    };
  }, [clearTimers, navigate]);

  const continueSession = () => {
    if (loggingOutRef.current) return;

    recordUserActivity();

    warningVisibleRef.current = false;
    setShowWarning(false);

    resetTimersRef.current?.();
  };

  if (!isAuthenticated || !showWarning) {
    return null;
  }

  const minutes = Math.floor(remainingSeconds / 60);
  const seconds = String(remainingSeconds % 60).padStart(2, "0");

  return (
    <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="session-warning-title"
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-neutral-900/50 p-4"
    >
      <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-xl bg-neutral-0 p-6 shadow-xl">
        <h2
          id="session-warning-title"
          className="text-xl font-semibold text-neutral-900"
        >
          Session expiring soon
        </h2>

        <p className="mt-3 text-neutral-700">
          You will be logged out due to inactivity in{" "}
          <strong>
            {minutes}:{seconds}
          </strong>
          . Continue your session to stay signed in.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => void endSession("logout")}
            className="rounded-lg border border-neutral-300 px-4 py-2"
          >
            Log out
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={continueSession}
            className="rounded-lg bg-primary-600 px-4 py-2 text-neutral-0"
          >
            Stay signed in
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default SessionManager;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\features\queue\queueSlice.js
========================================
import { createAsyncThunk, createSlice } from "@reduxjs/toolkit";

/** The date a request was made for; `null` means "today" (no argument). */
const requestedDateOf = (arg) => arg?.date ?? null;

/**
 * Fetch the doctor's queue for a local calendar date (`YYYY-MM-DD`), or
 * today's queue when no date is given.
 *
 * The slice keeps the previous rows visible while a refresh is in flight
 * (non-destructive pending) and discards responses whose request date no
 * longer matches the most recently requested one (stale-response guard), so
 * a slow response for a previously selected date can never overwrite the
 * queue of the date the doctor is now viewing.
 */
export const fetchQueue = createAsyncThunk(
  "queue/fetchQueue",
  async ({ date } = {}, { signal }) => {
    // Dynamic import keeps the apiClient â†” store â†” slice cycle lazy.
    const { getQueue } = await import("../../services/queueService");
    const records = await getQueue({ date, signal });
    return { date: requestedDateOf({ date }), records };
  },
  {
    // One in-flight request per date. A request for a *different* date is
    // still allowed to start; the reducer guard drops whichever response
    // arrives for the out-of-date request.
    condition: (arg, { getState }) => {
      const queue = getState().queue;
      return !(
        queue.status === "loading" && queue.requestDate === requestedDateOf(arg)
      );
    },
  },
);

/** Backwards-compatible alias used by the doctor dashboard. */
export const fetchTodayQueue = () => fetchQueue();

const initialState = {
  records: [],
  status: "idle",
  error: null,
  requestDate: null,
};

const queueSlice = createSlice({
  name: "queue",
  initialState,
  reducers: {},
  extraReducers: (builder) => {
    builder
      .addCase(fetchQueue.pending, (state, action) => {
        // Keep existing rows on screen while refreshing â€” the queue order
        // must never blank out mid-consultation.
        state.status = "loading";
        state.error = null;
        state.requestDate = requestedDateOf(action.meta.arg);
      })
      .addCase(fetchQueue.fulfilled, (state, action) => {
        // Stale response: the doctor switched dates while this was in flight.
        if (action.payload.date !== state.requestDate) return;
        state.records = action.payload.records;
        state.status = "succeeded";
      })
      .addCase(fetchQueue.rejected, (state, action) => {
        // Unmount aborts and out-of-date responses must not surface errors.
        if (action.meta.aborted) return;
        if (requestedDateOf(action.meta.arg) !== state.requestDate) return;
        state.status = "failed";
        state.error = action.error.message ?? "Unable to load queue.";
      });
  },
});

export const selectQueueRecords = (state) => state.queue.records;
export const selectQueueStatus = (state) => state.queue.status;
export const selectQueueError = (state) => state.queue.error;
export const selectQueueRequestDate = (state) => state.queue.requestDate;

export default queueSlice.reducer;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\hooks\usePageVisible.js
========================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

// Only an explicit "hidden" counts as hidden, so environments that never
// report visibility (e.g. jsdom's "prerender") keep behaving as visible.
const readVisible = () =>
  typeof document === "undefined" || document.visibilityState !== "hidden";

/** True while the browser tab is visible. Used to pause background polling. */
const usePageVisible = () => {
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(readVisible);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleChange = () => setVisible(readVisible());
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", handleChange);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", handleChange);
  }, []);

  return visible;
};

export default usePageVisible;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\hooks\useQueuePolling.js
========================================
import { useEffect } from "react";

/**
 * Polling cadence for the doctor's schedule queue (~2s per the ticket).
 * Named and exported so the interval is configurable in one place, and
 * injectable per-call (`intervalMs`) so tests can shorten it.
 */
export const QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 2000;

/**
 * Calls `onTick` every `intervalMs` while `enabled`. Ticks are skipped while
 * the tab is hidden (no background requests); polling resumes automatically
 * because the interval keeps running. The interval is cleaned up on unmount
 * or whenever `enabled`/`onTick` change.
 */
const useQueuePolling = ({
  enabled = true,
  onTick,
  intervalMs = QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS,
}) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled || typeof onTick !== "function") return undefined;

    const intervalId = setInterval(() => {
      if (typeof document !== "undefined" && document.hidden) return;
      onTick();
    }, intervalMs);

    return () => clearInterval(intervalId);
  }, [enabled, onTick, intervalMs]);
};

export default useQueuePolling;

{
  "common": {
    "home": "à®®à¯à®•à®ªà¯à®ªà¯",

  "validation": {
    "required": "à®‡à®¨à¯à®¤à®ªà¯ à®ªà¯à®²à®¤à¯à®¤à¯ˆ à®¨à®¿à®°à®ªà¯à®ªà¯à®µà®¤à¯ à®…à®µà®šà®¿à®¯à®®à¯."
  },
  "pagination": {
    "previous": "à®®à¯à®¨à¯à®¤à¯ˆà®¯à®¤à¯",
    "next": "à®…à®Ÿà¯à®¤à¯à®¤à®¤à¯",
    "page": "à®ªà®•à¯à®•à®®à¯",
