========================================
FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\pages\doctor\ConsultationPage.jsx
========================================
import { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";

import Button from "../../components/Button";
import EmptyState from "../../components/EmptyState";
import ErrorMessage from "../../components/ErrorMessage";
import Icon from "../../components/Icon";
import Input from "../../components/Input";
import StatusBadge from "../../components/StatusBadge";
import Toast from "../../components/Toast";
import { PageHeader, PageShell } from "../../components/doctor/PageLayout";
import {
  DetailTile,
  ErrorPanel,
  Panel,
  SkeletonBlock,
} from "../../components/doctor/Panel";
import {
  getAppointmentById,
  getPatientHistory,
  saveClinicalNotes,
} from "../../services/appointmentService";
import { getPatient } from "../../services/patientService";
import { searchMedicines } from "../../services/medicineService";
import {
  createPrescription,
  getPrescriptionById,
} from "../../services/prescriptionService";
import { completeAppointment } from "../../services/queueService";
import { formatDisplayDate } from "../../utils/dateUtils";
import {
  formatElapsed,
  formatTimeSlot,
  toDateOnly,
} from "../../utils/timeUtils";

/** Backend limit: clinical notes are capped at 2000 characters. */
export const CLINICAL_NOTES_MAX_LENGTH = 2000;

/**
 * Shown when the appointment changed status between render and the Complete
 * request (another staff member acted on it). Both 409 (per ticket) and 400
 * (what this backend returns for conflicting transitions) map here.
 */
export const CONCURRENT_CHANGE_MESSAGE =
  "This appointment's status has changed. The queue has been refreshed.";

const SAVED_NOTES_MESSAGE = "Clinical notes saved.";
const COMPLETED_TOAST_MESSAGE = "Consultation marked as completed.";
const COMPLETE_FAILED_MESSAGE =
  "Unable to complete the consultation. Please try again.";
const PRESCRIPTION_CREATED_MESSAGE = "Prescription issued.";

const EMPTY_ITEM = {
  medicineName: "",
  dosage: "",
  frequency: "",
  durationDays: "1",
  quantity: "1",
};

const DRAFT_FIELDS = [
  {
    key: "medicineName",
    id: "rx-medicine",
    label: "Medicine Name",
    wide: true,
  },
  { key: "dosage", id: "rx-dosage", label: "Dosage", placeholder: "1 tablet" },
  {
    key: "frequency",
    id: "rx-frequency",
    label: "Frequency",
    placeholder: "Twice daily",
  },
  {
    key: "durationDays",
    id: "rx-duration",
    label: "Duration (days)",
    type: "number",
    min: "1",
  },
  {
    key: "quantity",
    id: "rx-quantity",
    label: "Quantity",
    type: "number",
    min: "1",
  },
];

/**
 * The elapsed-time clock lives in its own component so its once-a-second
 * state update re-renders only this badge, never the whole consultation form.
 * It runs only while the appointment is still open.
 */
const ConsultationTimer = memo(function ConsultationTimer({ running }) {
  const [seconds, setSeconds] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!running) return undefined;

    const id = setInterval(() => {
      setSeconds((value) => value + 1);
    }, 1000);

    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, [running]);

  return (
    <span
      role="timer"
      aria-label="Elapsed time"
      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-xs font-semibold tabular-nums text-amber-900"
    >
      <Icon
        name="clock"
        className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 text-amber-600 ${
          running ? "motion-safe:animate-pulse" : ""
        }`}
      />
      {formatElapsed(seconds)}
    </span>
  );
});

const MedicationRow = memo(function MedicationRow({ item, index, onRemove }) {
  return (
    <li className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 p-3">
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-900">
          {item.medicineName}{" "}
          <span className="font-mono font-medium text-primary-600">
            ({item.dosage})
          </span>
        </p>
        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-neutral-500">
          {item.frequency}, {item.durationDays} day
          {item.durationDays === 1 ? "" : "s"}, quantity {item.quantity}
        </p>
      </div>

      <button
        type="button"
        aria-label={`Remove ${item.medicineName}`}
        onClick={() => onRemove(index)}
        className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-neutral-400 transition-colors hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary-600"
      >
        <Icon name="trash" className="h-4 w-4" />
      </button>
    </li>
  );
});

const PreviousNote = memo(function PreviousNote({ record }) {
  return (
    <li className="space-y-2 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 p-4 text-sm">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-800">
          {formatDisplayDate(toDateOnly(record.appointmentDate))}
        </span>
        <StatusBadge status={record.status} />
      </div>

      <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed text-neutral-700">
        {record.clinicalNotes}
      </p>
    </li>
  );
});

/**
 * DOC-04 - Consultation screen.
 *
 * 1. Review the booking and the patient's previous clinical notes.
 * 2. Record this encounter's clinical notes.
 * 3. Issue a prescription if needed.
 * 4. Mark the appointment COMPLETED.
 */
const ConsultationPage = () => {
  const { appointmentId } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [appointment, setAppointment] = useState(null);
  const [patientName, setPatientName] = useState(null);
  const [loadStatus, setLoadStatus] = useState("loading");

  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");
  const [notesSaving, setNotesSaving] = useState(false);
  const [notesError, setNotesError] = useState(null);
  const notesDirtyRef = useRef(false);

  const [history, setHistory] = useState(null);

  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [itemDraft, setItemDraft] = useState(EMPTY_ITEM);
  const [medicineSuggestions, setMedicineSuggestions] = useState([]);
  const [medicineSuggestionsForQuery, setMedicineSuggestionsForQuery] =
    useState("");

  const [creatingPrescription, setCreatingPrescription] = useState(false);
  const [prescriptionError, setPrescriptionError] = useState(null);
  const [createdPrescriptions, setCreatedPrescriptions] = useState([]);
  const [relatedPrescriptions, setRelatedPrescriptions] = useState(null);

  const [completing, setCompleting] = useState(false);
  const completingRef = useRef(false);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState(null);

  const loadIdRef = useRef(0);

  // Medicine autocomplete.
  // Suggestions are displayed only when they match the current query.
  // This avoids synchronously clearing state inside the effect.
  useEffect(() => {
    const query = itemDraft.medicineName.trim();

    if (query.length < 2) {
      return undefined;
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();

    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
      searchMedicines(query, { signal: controller.signal })
        .then((suggestions) => {
          if (controller.signal.aborted) return;

          setMedicineSuggestions(suggestions);
          setMedicineSuggestionsForQuery(query);
        })
        .catch(() => {
          if (controller.signal.aborted) return;

          setMedicineSuggestions([]);
          setMedicineSuggestionsForQuery("");
        });
    }, 250);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [itemDraft.medicineName]);

  const medicineQuery = itemDraft.medicineName.trim();

  const visibleMedicineSuggestions =
    medicineQuery.length >= 2 && medicineSuggestionsForQuery === medicineQuery
      ? medicineSuggestions
      : [];

  const isClosed =
    appointment?.status === "COMPLETED" || appointment?.status === "CANCELLED";

  const isCompleted = appointment?.status === "COMPLETED";
  const canComplete = appointment?.status === "SCHEDULED";

  const load = useCallback(() => {
    const loadId = ++loadIdRef.current;
    const isStale = () => loadId !== loadIdRef.current;

    getAppointmentById(appointmentId)
      .then((found) => {
        if (isStale()) return;

        // Update related prescription state after the request resolves,
        // rather than synchronously when the loading effect starts.
        setRelatedPrescriptions(null);

        setAppointment(found);

        if (!notesDirtyRef.current) {
          setNotes(
            typeof found.clinicalNotes === "string" ? found.clinicalNotes : "",
          );
        }

        setLoadStatus("succeeded");

        if (found.patientId === null || found.patientId === undefined) {
          setPatientName(null);
          setHistory(null);
          setRelatedPrescriptions([]);
          return;
        }

        getPatient(found.patientId)
          .then((patient) => {
            if (!isStale()) {
              setPatientName(patient.fullName);
            }
          })
          .catch(() => {
            if (!isStale()) {
              setPatientName(null);
            }
          });

        getPatientHistory(found.patientId)
          .then((response) => {
            if (isStale()) return;

            setHistory(response);

            if (found.status !== "COMPLETED") {
              setRelatedPrescriptions([]);
              return;
            }

            const ids = response.prescriptions
              .filter(
                (prescription) =>
                  String(
                    prescription.appointmentId ??
                      prescription.appointment?.appointmentId,
                  ) === String(found.appointmentId),
              )
              .map((prescription) => prescription.prescriptionId)
              .filter((id) => id !== undefined && id !== null);

            Promise.all(ids.map((id) => getPrescriptionById(id)))
              .then((prescriptions) => {
                if (!isStale()) {
                  setRelatedPrescriptions(prescriptions);
                }
              })
              .catch(() => {
                if (!isStale()) {
                  setRelatedPrescriptions("failed");
                }
              });
          })
          .catch(() => {
            if (!isStale()) {
              setHistory(null);
              setRelatedPrescriptions("failed");
            }
          });
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (!isStale()) {
          setLoadStatus("failed");
        }
      });
  }, [appointmentId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    load();

    return () => {
      loadIdRef.current += 1;
    };
  }, [load]);

  const handleNotesChange = useCallback((event) => {
    notesDirtyRef.current = true;
    setNotes(event.target.value);
    setNotesError(null);
  }, []);

  const handleSaveNotes = useCallback(() => {
    if (notesSaving) return;

    setNotesSaving(true);
    setNotesError(null);

    saveClinicalNotes(appointmentId, notes)
      .then(() => {
        notesDirtyRef.current = false;
        setToast({
          type: "success",
          message: SAVED_NOTES_MESSAGE,
        });
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        const status = error?.response?.status;

        setNotesError(
          status === 400
            ? (error?.userMessage ??
                "Clinical notes cannot be added to this appointment.")
            : "Unable to save the clinical notes. Please try again.",
        );
      })
      .finally(() => {
        setNotesSaving(false);
      });
  }, [appointmentId, notes, notesSaving]);

  const handleDraftChange = useCallback(
    (key) => (event) =>
      setItemDraft((draft) => ({
        ...draft,
        [key]: event.target.value,
      })),
    [],
  );

  const handleAddItem = useCallback(() => {
    const draft = itemDraft;

    if (
      !draft.medicineName.trim() ||
      !draft.dosage.trim() ||
      !draft.frequency.trim() ||
      !(Number(draft.durationDays) >= 1) ||
      !(Number(draft.quantity) >= 1)
    ) {
      setPrescriptionError(
        "Fill in medicine name, dosage, frequency, duration (days) and quantity.",
      );
      return;
    }

    setPrescriptionError(null);

    setItems((current) => [
      ...current,
      {
        medicineName: draft.medicineName.trim(),
        dosage: draft.dosage.trim(),
        frequency: draft.frequency.trim(),
        durationDays: Number(draft.durationDays),
        quantity: Number(draft.quantity),
      },
    ]);

    setItemDraft(EMPTY_ITEM);
  }, [itemDraft]);

  const handleRemoveItem = useCallback((index) => {
    setItems((current) => current.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  }, []);

  const handleCreatePrescription = useCallback(() => {
    if (creatingPrescription || items.length === 0) return;

    setCreatingPrescription(true);
    setPrescriptionError(null);

    createPrescription({
      patientId: appointment.patientId,
      appointmentId: appointment.appointmentId,
      items,
    })
      .then((prescription) => {
        setCreatedPrescriptions((current) => [...current, prescription]);

        setRelatedPrescriptions((current) =>
          Array.isArray(current) ? [...current, prescription] : [prescription],
        );

        setItems([]);
        setToast({
          type: "success",
          message: PRESCRIPTION_CREATED_MESSAGE,
        });
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        setPrescriptionError(
          error?.userMessage ??
            "Unable to create the prescription. Please try again.",
        );
      })
      .finally(() => {
        setCreatingPrescription(false);
      });
  }, [appointment, creatingPrescription, items]);

  const handleComplete = useCallback(() => {
    if (completingRef.current) return;

    completingRef.current = true;
    setCompleting(true);

    completeAppointment(appointmentId)
      .then((updated) => {
        setAppointment((current) => ({
          ...current,
          status: updated?.status ?? "COMPLETED",
        }));

        setToast({
          type: "success",
          message: COMPLETED_TOAST_MESSAGE,
        });
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        const status = error?.response?.status;

        if (status === 409 || status === 400) {
          setToast({
            type: "warning",
            message: CONCURRENT_CHANGE_MESSAGE,
          });

          load();
        } else {
          setToast({
            type: "error",
            message: COMPLETE_FAILED_MESSAGE,
          });
        }
      })
      .finally(() => {
        completingRef.current = false;
        setCompleting(false);
      });
  }, [appointmentId, load]);

  const goToSchedule = useCallback(
    () => navigate("/doctor/today-schedule"),
    [navigate],
  );

  const retryLoad = useCallback(() => {
    setLoadStatus("loading");
    load();
  }, [load]);

  const previousNotes = useMemo(
    () =>
      history?.appointments?.filter(
        (record) =>
          String(record.appointmentId) !== String(appointmentId) &&
          typeof record.clinicalNotes === "string" &&
          record.clinicalNotes.trim().length > 0,
      ) ?? [],
    [history, appointmentId],
  );

  return (
    <PageShell>
      <PageHeader
        eyebrow={isCompleted ? "Completed Appointment" : "Active Consultation"}
        title={isCompleted ? "Consultation Details" : "Consultation Room"}
        description={
          isCompleted
            ? "Review the completed visit, clinical notes and linked prescriptions."
            : "Review the booking, record clinical notes, issue prescriptions and complete the visit."
        }
        meta={
          <>
            <span className="rounded-full bg-primary-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-primary-700">
              Appt #{appointmentId}
            </span>
            <ConsultationTimer running={canComplete} />
          </>
        }
        actions={
          canComplete ? (
            <Button
              onClick={handleComplete}
              loading={completing}
              disabled={completing}
              size="sm"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2"
            >
              <Icon name="circle-check" className="h-4 w-4" />
              Complete Consultation
            </Button>
          ) : (
            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={goToSchedule}>
              Back to Schedule
            </Button>
          )
        }
      />

      {toast && (
        <Toast
          message={toast.message}
          type={toast.type}
          onClose={() => setToast(null)}
        />
      )}

      {loadStatus === "loading" ? (
        <SkeletonBlock label="Loading consultation details" rows={6} />
      ) : loadStatus === "not-found" ? (
        <ErrorPanel
          title="Appointment not available"
          message="This appointment is not part of your schedule. It may have been removed or assigned to another doctor."
        />
      ) : loadStatus === "failed" ? (
        <ErrorPanel
          title="Unable to load the consultation"
          message="Please check your connection and try again."
          actionLabel="Retry"
          onAction={retryLoad}
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 items-start gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
          <div className="space-y-6 lg:col-span-7">
            <Panel
              icon="user"
              title="Patient Details"
              aside={<StatusBadge status={appointment.status} />}
            >
              <dl className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
                <DetailTile label="Patient name">
                  {patientName ??
                    `Patient ${appointment.patientId ?? "\u2014"}`}
                </DetailTile>

                <DetailTile label="Date and time">
                  {formatDisplayDate(toDateOnly(appointment.appointmentDate))}{" "}
                  at {formatTimeSlot(appointment.timeSlot)}
                </DetailTile>

                <DetailTile label="Reason">
                  {appointment.reason || "None specified"}
                </DetailTile>
              </dl>
            </Panel>

            <Panel
              icon="clipboard"
              title="Clinical Examination & Notes"
              titleId="clinical-notes-heading"
              aside={
                <span className="text-xs font-medium tabular-nums text-neutral-500">
                  {notes.length}/{CLINICAL_NOTES_MAX_LENGTH}
                </span>
              }
              bodyClassName="space-y-3"
            >
              {isClosed ? (
                <>
                  <p className="rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 p-4 text-sm text-neutral-600">
                    {appointment.status === "CANCELLED"
                      ? "Clinical notes cannot be added to a cancelled appointment."
                      : "This consultation is completed; its clinical notes are read-only."}
                  </p>

                  {appointment.status === "COMPLETED" && notes.trim() && (
                    <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed text-neutral-800">
                      {notes}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <textarea
                    id="clinical-notes"
                    aria-labelledby="clinical-notes-heading"
                    rows={7}
                    maxLength={CLINICAL_NOTES_MAX_LENGTH}
                    value={notes}
                    onChange={handleNotesChange}
                    className="w-full resize-y rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50/60 p-4 text-sm leading-relaxed text-neutral-900 focus:border-primary-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500/20"
                    placeholder="Diagnosis, advice, follow-up..."
                  />

                  <div className="flex justify-end">
                    <Button
                      onClick={handleSaveNotes}
                      loading={notesSaving}
                      disabled={notesSaving}
                      size="sm"
                    >
                      Save Notes
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </>
              )}

              {notesError && <ErrorMessage message={notesError} />}
            </Panel>

            <Panel icon="history" title="Previous Clinical Notes">
              {previousNotes.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
                  No previous clinical notes for this patient.
                </p>
              ) : (
                <ul className="max-h-80 space-y-3 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                  {previousNotes.map((record) => (
                    <PreviousNote key={record.appointmentId} record={record} />
                  ))}
                </ul>
              )}
            </Panel>
          </div>

          <div className="lg:col-span-5">
            <Panel
              icon="pill"
              title="E-Prescription Builder"
              aside={
                <span className="rounded-full bg-primary-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-primary-700">
                  Pharmacy Queue
                </span>
              }
            >
              {createdPrescriptions.length > 0 && (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium text-neutral-600">
                    Issued prescriptions
                  </p>

                  <ul className="space-y-2">
                    {createdPrescriptions.map((prescription) => (
                      <li
                        key={prescription.prescriptionId}
                        className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-3 text-sm text-emerald-900"
                      >
                        <span className="font-medium">
                          Prescription #{prescription.prescriptionId} &mdash;{" "}
                          {prescription.items?.length ?? 0}{" "}
                          {prescription.items?.length === 1 ? "item" : "items"}
                        </span>

                        <span className="rounded-full bg-emerald-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800">
                          {prescription.status}
                        </span>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              )}

              {isClosed ? (
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  <p className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
                    This consultation is closed; no further prescriptions can be
                    issued here.
                  </p>

                  {appointment.status === "COMPLETED" && (
                    <div className="space-y-2" aria-live="polite">
                      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-neutral-700">
                        Prescriptions for this appointment
                      </p>

                      {relatedPrescriptions === null ? (
                        <p className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
                          Loading prescriptions...
                        </p>
                      ) : relatedPrescriptions === "failed" ? (
                        <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600">
                          Prescription details are unavailable right now.
                        </p>
                      ) : relatedPrescriptions.length === 0 ? (
                        <p className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
                          No prescriptions are linked to this appointment.
                        </p>
                      ) : (
                        <ul className="space-y-2">
                          {relatedPrescriptions.map((prescription) => (
                            <li
                              key={prescription.prescriptionId}
                              className="rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 p-3"
                            >
                              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-sm">
                                <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900">
                                  Prescription #{prescription.prescriptionId}
                                </span>

                                <span className="rounded-full bg-neutral-200 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700">
                                  {prescription.status}
                                </span>
                              </div>

                              <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-sm text-neutral-700">
                                {prescription.items.map((item, index) => (
                                  <li
                                    key={
                                      item.itemId ??
                                      `${item.medicineName}-${index}`
                                    }
                                  >
                                    <span className="font-medium">
                                      {item.medicineName}
                                    </span>
                                    {item.dosage ? ` â€” ${item.dosage}` : ""}
                                    {item.frequency
                                      ? `, ${item.frequency}`
                                      : ""}
                                    {item.durationDays
                                      ? ` for ${item.durationDays} days`
                                      : ""}
                                    {item.quantity
                                      ? ` (qty ${item.quantity})`
                                      : ""}
                                  </li>
                                ))}
                              </ul>
                            </li>
                          ))}
                        </ul>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <fieldset className="space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 p-4">
                  <legend className="px-1 text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-800">
                    Add Medication Item
                  </legend>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    <div className="relative col-span-2">
                      <Input
                        id="rx-medicine"
                        label="Medicine Name"
                        placeholder="Type at least 2 characters to search"
                        value={itemDraft.medicineName}
                        onChange={handleDraftChange("medicineName")}
                        autoComplete="off"
                        role="combobox"
                        aria-autocomplete="list"
                        aria-expanded={visibleMedicineSuggestions.length > 0}
                      />

                      {visibleMedicineSuggestions.length > 0 && (
                        <ul
                          role="listbox"
                          aria-label="Available medicines"
                          className="absolute z-20 mt-1 max-h-56 w-full overflow-y-auto rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white py-1 shadow-lg"
                        >
                          {visibleMedicineSuggestions.map((medicine, index) => {
                            const name = medicine.medicineName ?? medicine.name;

                            return (
                              <li
                                key={
                                  medicine.medicineId ??
                                  medicine.id ??
                                  `${name}-${index}`
                                }
                              >
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  role="option"
                                  aria-selected={false}
                                  onMouseDown={(event) =>
                                    event.preventDefault()
                                  }
                                  onClick={() => {
                                    setItemDraft((draft) => ({
                                      ...draft,
                                      medicineName: name,
                                    }));

                                    setMedicineSuggestions([]);
                                    setMedicineSuggestionsForQuery("");
                                  }}
                                  className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-sm text-neutral-800 hover:bg-primary-50 focus:bg-primary-50 focus:outline-none"
                                >
                                  {name}
                                </button>
                              </li>
                            );
                          })}
                        </ul>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {DRAFT_FIELDS.filter(
                      (field) => field.key !== "medicineName",
                    ).map((field) => (
                      <div
                        key={field.key}
                        className={field.wide ? "col-span-2" : ""}
                      >
                        <Input
                          id={field.id}
                          label={field.label}
                          type={field.type}
                          min={field.min}
                          placeholder={field.placeholder}
                          value={itemDraft[field.key]}
                          onChange={handleDraftChange(field.key)}
                        />
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    size="sm"
                    onClick={handleAddItem}
                    className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                  >
                    <Icon name="plus" className="h-4 w-4" />
                    Add Medication
                  </Button>
                </fieldset>
              )}

              {items.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                  title="No medicines added yet."
                  message="Add a medicine above to build this prescription."
                />
              ) : (
                <ul className="max-h-64 space-y-2 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                  {items.map((item, index) => (
                    <MedicationRow
                      key={`${item.medicineName}-${index}`}
                      item={item}
                      index={index}
                      onRemove={handleRemoveItem}
                    />
                  ))}
                </ul>
              )}

              {!isClosed && items.length > 0 && (
                <Button
                  onClick={handleCreatePrescription}
                  loading={creatingPrescription}
                  disabled={creatingPrescription}
                  size="sm"
                  className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                >
                  <Icon name="arrow-up-right" className="h-4 w-4" />
                  Transmit Prescription
                </Button>
              )}

              {prescriptionError && (
                <ErrorMessage message={prescriptionError} />
              )}
            </Panel>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </PageShell>
  );
};

export default ConsultationPage;

import { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import { useDispatch, useSelector } from "react-redux";

import Button from "../../components/Button";
import EmptyState from "../../components/EmptyState";
import Icon from "../../components/Icon";
import StatusBadge from "../../components/StatusBadge";
import { PageHeader, PageShell } from "../../components/doctor/PageLayout";
import {
  ErrorPanel,
  Panel,
  SectionTitle,
  SkeletonBlock,
} from "../../components/doctor/Panel";
import {
  fetchTodayQueue,
  selectQueueRecords,
  selectQueueStatus,
} from "../../features/queue/queueSlice";
import { calculateQueueStatusCounts } from "../../utils/queueStatusCounts";
import { formatTimeSlot } from "../../utils/timeUtils";

const METRICS_LABEL = "Loading today\u2019s metrics...";

const QueueItem = memo(function QueueItem({ record }) {
  const timeLabel = record.timeSlot
    ? formatTimeSlot(record.timeSlot)
    : record.appointmentTime || "Scheduled today";

  return (
    <li className="flex flex-col gap-3 px-5 py-4 transition-colors hover:bg-neutral-50 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
      <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 min-w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-primary-50 px-2 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-700">
          {`#${record.appointmentId}`}
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <p className="truncate font-semibold text-neutral-900">
            {record.patientFullName || `Appointment #${record.appointmentId}`}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-sm text-neutral-500">{timeLabel}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <StatusBadge status={record.status}>{record.status}</StatusBadge>
    </li>
  );
});

const StatCard = memo(function StatCard({ header, value }) {
  return (
    <article className="rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-xs">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">{header}</div>
      <p className="mt-4 text-3xl font-bold tabular-nums tracking-tight text-neutral-950">
        {value}
      </p>
    </article>
  );
});

const DoctorDashboardPage = () => {
  const dispatch = useDispatch();
  const queue = useSelector(selectQueueRecords);
  const queueStatus = useSelector(selectQueueStatus);

  const loading = queueStatus === "idle" || queueStatus === "loading";
  const error = queueStatus === "failed";
  const initialLoading = loading && queue.length === 0;

  const handleRefresh = useCallback(() => {
    dispatch(fetchTodayQueue());
  }, [dispatch]);

  useEffect(() => {
    handleRefresh();
  }, [handleRefresh]);

  const statusCounts = useMemo(
    () => calculateQueueStatusCounts(queue),
    [queue],
  );

  return (
    <PageShell>
      <PageHeader
        title={"Today\u2019s Appointments"}
        description="Monitor and manage your active clinical queue in real time."
        actions={
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            onClick={handleRefresh}
            disabled={loading}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-white"
          >
            <Icon
              name="refresh"
              className={`h-4 w-4 ${loading ? "text-primary-600 motion-safe:animate-spin" : ""}`}
            />
            Refresh Queue
          </Button>
        }
      />

      {error ? (
        <ErrorPanel
          title="Unable to load summary metrics"
          message="Please check your connection and try refreshing."
          actionLabel="Retry Connection"
          actionIcon="refresh"
          onAction={handleRefresh}
          actionLoading={loading}
        />
      ) : (
        <>
          <section aria-labelledby="queue-summary-heading">
            <SectionTitle id="queue-summary-heading">
              Overview Statistics
            </SectionTitle>
            {initialLoading ? (
              <div
                role="status"
                aria-label={METRICS_LABEL}
                aria-busy="true"
                className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4"
              >
                {Array.from({ length: 4 }, (_, index) => (
                  <div
                    key={index}
                    className="h-28 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-100 motion-safe:animate-pulse"
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
                <StatCard
                  value={queue.length}
                  header={
                    <>
                      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-neutral-500">
                        Total Patients
                      </p>
                      <div className="rounded-xl bg-neutral-100 p-2.5 text-neutral-700">
                        <Icon name="users" className="h-4 w-4" />
                      </div>
                    </>
                  }
                />
                {statusCounts.map(({ status, count }) => (
                  <StatCard
                    key={status}
                    value={count}
                    header={<StatusBadge status={status}>{status}</StatusBadge>}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          {initialLoading ? (
            <SkeletonBlock label="Loading active queue records..." rows={4} />
          ) : (
            <Panel
              icon="users"
              title="Active Patient Queue"
              titleId="today-queue-heading"
              padded={false}
              bodyClassName=""
              aside={
                queue.length > 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
                    {queue.length}{" "}
                    {queue.length === 1 ? "consultation" : "consultations"}{" "}
                    today
                  </p>
                ) : null
              }
            >
              {queue.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                  title="No Appointments Scheduled"
                  message="Your queue is clear for today. New appointments appear here as they are booked."
                />
              ) : (
                <ul className="divide-y divide-neutral-100">
                  {queue.map((record) => (
                    <QueueItem key={record.appointmentId} record={record} />
                  ))}
                </ul>
              )}
            </Panel>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </PageShell>
  );
};

export default DoctorDashboardPage;

  useEffect,

  useRef,
  useState,
} from "react";

  "Cardiology",

const AddPdfModal = ({ onCancel, onAdd }) => {
  const fileRef = useRef(null);
  const urlRef = useRef("");
  const consumedRef = useRef(false);

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    title: "",
    category: CATEGORIES[0],
    badge: "Guideline",
    updated: "Updated today",
    desc: "",
    critical: false,
  });
  const [fileName, setFileName] = useState("");
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState({});

  // Free the temporary PDF URL if the dialog closes without adding it.
  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      if (!consumedRef.current && urlRef.current)
        URL.revokeObjectURL(urlRef.current);
    },
    [],
  );

  const setField = useCallback(
    (key) => (event) => {
      const { type, checked, value } = event.target;
      setForm((current) => ({
        ...current,
        [key]: type === "checkbox" ? checked : value,
      }));
    },
    [],
  );

  const handleFile = (event) => {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    if (file.type !== "application/pdf") {
      setErrors((current) => ({
        ...current,
        file: "Please select a PDF file.",
      }));
      return;
    }
    if (urlRef.current) URL.revokeObjectURL(urlRef.current);
    urlRef.current = URL.createObjectURL(file);
    setFileName(file.name);
    setErrors((current) => ({ ...current, file: undefined }));
  };

  const handleSubmit = (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const nextErrors = {};
    if (!form.title.trim()) nextErrors.title = "Enter a title.";
    if (!urlRef.current) nextErrors.file = "Attach a PDF file.";
    setErrors(nextErrors);
    if (Object.keys(nextErrors).length > 0) return;

    consumedRef.current = true;
    onAdd({
      ...form,
      title: form.title.trim(),
      desc: form.desc.trim() || "No description provided.",
      pdf: urlRef.current,
    });
  };

  return (
    <Modal title="Add PDF reference" onClose={onCancel}>
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
        <div>
          <Input
            id="kb-title"
            label="Title"
            value={form.title}
            onChange={setField("title")}
            placeholder="e.g. 2024 Sepsis Management Guideline"
          />
          {errors.title && (
            <p role="alert" className="mt-1 text-sm text-rose-600">
              {errors.title}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <div>
            <label htmlFor="kb-category" className={LABEL_CLASS}>
              Category
            </label>
            <select
              id="kb-category"
              value={form.category}
              onChange={setField("category")}
              className={FIELD_CLASS}
            >
              {CATEGORIES.map((category) => (
                <option key={category} value={category}>
                  {category}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <Input
            id="kb-badge"
            label="Badge"
            value={form.badge}
            onChange={setField("badge")}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <Input
            id="kb-updated"
            label="Updated label"
            value={form.updated}
            onChange={setField("updated")}
          />
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 self-end pb-2 text-sm font-medium text-neutral-700">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={form.critical}
              onChange={setField("critical")}
              className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-neutral-300"
            />
            Mark as critical
          </label>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label htmlFor="kb-desc" className={LABEL_CLASS}>
            Description
          </label>
          <textarea
            id="kb-desc"
            value={form.desc}
            onChange={setField("desc")}
            rows={3}
            className={`${FIELD_CLASS} resize-none`}
            placeholder="Short summary of the reference..."
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className={LABEL_CLASS}>PDF file</span>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5"
            >
              <Icon name="plus" className="h-4 w-4" />
              Choose PDF
            </Button>
            <span className="truncate text-sm text-neutral-600">
              {fileName || "No file selected"}
            </span>
            <input
              ref={fileRef}
              type="file"
              accept="application/pdf"
              onChange={handleFile}
              className="hidden"
              aria-label="PDF file"
            />
          </div>
          {errors.file && (
            <p role="alert" className="mt-1 text-sm text-rose-600">
              {errors.file}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-neutral-100 pt-4">
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={onCancel}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="rounded-xl bg-primary-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-primary-700 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary-600"
          >
            Add reference
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
};

const ConfirmRemoveModal = ({ article, onCancel, onConfirm }) => (
  <Modal title="Remove reference" onClose={onCancel} widthClass="max-w-md">
    <p className="text-sm text-neutral-700">
      Remove <span className="font-semibold">{article.title}</span> from the
      knowledge base?
    </p>
    <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end gap-2">

        Cancel
      </Button>
      <button
        type="button"
        data-autofocus
        onClick={onConfirm}
        className="rounded-xl bg-rose-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-rose-700 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-rose-600"
      >
        Remove
      </button>
    </div>
  </Modal>
);

/**
 * Clinical reference library. Articles live in component state only: PDFs
 * added here are held as temporary browser URLs and are lost on reload.
 */
const KnowledgeBasePage = () => {
  const [articles, setArticles] = useState(SEED_ARTICLES);
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState("All");
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const deferredQuery = useDeferredValue(searchQuery);
  const [selectedPdf, setSelectedPdf] = useState(null);
  const [showAddModal, setShowAddModal] = useState(false);
  const [pendingRemoval, setPendingRemoval] = useState(null);

  // Release any browser-held PDF URLs when leaving the page.
  const articlesRef = useRef(articles);
  useEffect(() => {
    articlesRef.current = articles;
  }, [articles]);
  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      articlesRef.current.forEach((article) => {
        if (isBlobUrl(article.pdf)) URL.revokeObjectURL(article.pdf);
      });
    },
    [],
  );

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const query = deferredQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    return articles.filter(
      (article) =>
        (selectedCategory === "All" || article.category === selectedCategory) &&
        (!query ||
          article.title.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
          article.desc.toLowerCase().includes(query)),
    );
  }, [articles, selectedCategory, deferredQuery]);

  const handleAdd = useCallback((newArticle) => {
    setArticles((current) => [...current, { ...newArticle, id: Date.now() }]);
    setShowAddModal(false);
  }, []);

  const handleConfirmRemove = useCallback(() => {
    if (!pendingRemoval) return;
    if (isBlobUrl(pendingRemoval.pdf)) URL.revokeObjectURL(pendingRemoval.pdf);
    setArticles((current) =>
      current.filter((item) => item.id !== pendingRemoval.id),
    );
    setPendingRemoval(null);
  }, [pendingRemoval]);

  const openAdd = useCallback(() => setShowAddModal(true), []);
  const closeAdd = useCallback(() => setShowAddModal(false), []);
  const closeRemove = useCallback(() => setPendingRemoval(null), []);
  const closeViewer = useCallback(() => setSelectedPdf(null), []);

  if (selectedPdf) {
    return (
      <PageShell>
        <PageHeader
          title={selectedPdf.title}
          description={`${selectedPdf.category}. ${selectedPdf.updated}.`}
          actions={
            <>
              <a
                href={selectedPdf.pdf}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-neutral-700 transition-colors hover:bg-neutral-50"
              >
                <Icon name="arrow-up-right" className="h-4 w-4" />
                Open in new tab
              </a>
              <Button
                size="sm"
                onClick={closeViewer}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5"
              >
                <Icon name="arrow-left" className="h-4 w-4" />
                Back to list
              </Button>
            </>
          }
        />
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white shadow-xs">
          <iframe
            src={selectedPdf.pdf}
            title={selectedPdf.title}
            className="h-[75vh] w-full border-0"
          />
        </div>
      </PageShell>
    );
  }

  return (
    <PageShell>
      <PageHeader
        title="Medical Knowledge Base"
        description="Evidence-based clinical guidelines, pharmacology references and emergency protocols."
        meta={
          <p className="font-medium text-neutral-800">
            {articles.length}{" "}
            {articles.length === 1 ? "reference" : "references"} in library
          </p>
        }
        actions={
          <Button onClick={openAdd} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
            <Icon name="plus" className="h-4 w-4" />
            Add PDF Reference
          </Button>
        }
      />

      <Panel bodyClassName="space-y-4">
        <div className="relative">
          <label htmlFor="kb-search" className="sr-only">
            Search references
          </label>
          <Icon
            name="search"
            className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-neutral-400"
          />
          <input
            id="kb-search"
            type="search"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(event) => setSearchQuery(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search guidelines, drug interactions, protocols..."
            className={`${FIELD_CLASS} pl-9`}
          />
        </div>
        <div
          role="group"
          aria-label="Filter by category"
          className="flex flex-wrap gap-2"
        >
          {FILTERS.map((category) => (
            <button
              key={category}
              type="button"
              aria-pressed={selectedCategory === category}
              onClick={() => setSelectedCategory(category)}
              className={`rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium transition-colors focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary-600 ${
                selectedCategory === category
                  ? "bg-primary-600 text-white"
                  : "bg-neutral-100 text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-200"
              }`}
            >
              {category}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Panel>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white shadow-xs">
          <EmptyState
            title="No matching references found."
            message="Try a different search or category, or add a PDF reference."
          />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
          {filtered.map((article) => (
            <ArticleCard
              key={article.id}
              article={article}
              onOpen={setSelectedPdf}
              onRemove={setPendingRemoval}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {showAddModal && <AddPdfModal onCancel={closeAdd} onAdd={handleAdd} />}
      {pendingRemoval && (
        <ConfirmRemoveModal
          article={pendingRemoval}
          onCancel={closeRemove}
          onConfirm={handleConfirmRemove}
        />
      )}
    </PageShell>
  );
};

export default KnowledgeBasePage;

import { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";

import Button from "../../components/Button";
import Icon from "../../components/Icon";
import StatusBadge from "../../components/StatusBadge";
import { PageHeader, PageShell } from "../../components/doctor/PageLayout";
import {
  DetailTile,
  ErrorPanel,
  Panel,
  SkeletonBlock,
} from "../../components/doctor/Panel";
import { getPatientHistory } from "../../services/appointmentService";
import { formatDisplayDate } from "../../utils/dateUtils";
import { formatTimeSlot, toDateOnly } from "../../utils/timeUtils";

const RECORDS_PATH = "/doctor/patient-records";

/** Profile fields shown only when the backend actually returns them. */
const PROFILE_FIELDS = [
  ["age", "Age"],
  ["gender", "Gender"],
  ["bloodGroup", "Blood group"],
  ["dateOfBirth", "Date of birth"],
  ["contactNo", "Phone"],
  ["email", "Email"],
  ["address", "Address"],
];

const visitSortKey = (visit) =>
  `${visit.appointmentDate ?? ""}${visit.timeSlot ?? ""}`;

const VisitEntry = memo(function VisitEntry({ visit }) {
  const notes =
    typeof visit.clinicalNotes === "string" ? visit.clinicalNotes.trim() : "";

  return (
    <li className="relative space-y-2">
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="absolute -left-[31px] top-1.5 h-3 w-3 rounded-full border-2 border-white bg-primary-600 ring-1 ring-primary-200"
      />
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-900">
          {formatDisplayDate(toDateOnly(visit.appointmentDate))} at{" "}
          {formatTimeSlot(visit.timeSlot)}
        </p>
        <StatusBadge status={visit.status} />
      </div>
      {visit.reason && (
        <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600">{visit.reason}</p>
      )}
      {notes && (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 p-3">
          <p className="mb-1 text-xs font-medium text-neutral-500">
            Clinical notes
          </p>
          <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed text-neutral-800">
            {notes}
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </li>
  );
});

const PrescriptionEntry = memo(function PrescriptionEntry({ prescription }) {
  const items = Array.isArray(prescription.items) ? prescription.items : [];
  const appointment = prescription.appointment;

  return (
    <li className="space-y-2 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 p-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-sm">
        <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900">
          Prescription #{prescription.prescriptionId}
        </span>
        {prescription.status && (
          <span className="rounded-full bg-neutral-200 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-neutral-700">
            {prescription.status}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      {appointment && (
        <div className="rounded-lg border border-primary-100 bg-white px-3 py-2">
          <p className="text-xs font-medium text-neutral-500">
            Related appointment
          </p>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-medium text-neutral-800">
            {appointment.appointmentDate
              ? formatDisplayDate(toDateOnly(appointment.appointmentDate))
              : `Appointment #${appointment.appointmentId}`}
            {appointment.timeSlot
              ? ` at ${formatTimeSlot(appointment.timeSlot)}`
              : ""}
          </p>
          {appointment.reason && (
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-sm text-neutral-600">
              {appointment.reason}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      {items.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="space-y-1 text-sm text-neutral-700">
          {items.map((item, index) => (
            <li
              key={`${item.medicineName}-${index}`}
              className="flex items-center gap-2"
            >
              <Icon
                name="pill"
                className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-primary-600"
              />
              <span>
                {item.medicineName}
                {item.dosage ? ` (${item.dosage})` : ""}
              </span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </li>
  );
});

/**
 * The record opened from the patient directory. The patient profile, visit
 * history, and prescriptions are loaded together from the patient history API.
 */
export const PatientDetails = () => {
  const { patientId } = useParams();

  const [outcome, setOutcome] = useState(null);
  const [historyOutcome, setHistoryOutcome] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!patientId) return undefined;

    let cancelled = false;

    getPatientHistory(patientId)
      .then((data) => {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setOutcome({ id: patientId, patient: data.patient });
          setHistoryOutcome({ id: patientId, history: data });
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setOutcome({ id: patientId, error: true });
          setHistoryOutcome({ id: patientId, error: true });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [patientId]);

  const current = outcome && outcome.id === patientId ? outcome : null;
  const failed = !patientId || Boolean(current?.error);
  const loading = !failed && !current;
  const patient = failed ? null : (current?.patient ?? null);

  const historyState =
    historyOutcome && historyOutcome.id === patientId ? historyOutcome : null;
  const history = historyState?.history ?? null;

  const visits = useMemo(
    () =>
      [...(history?.appointments ?? [])].sort((a, b) =>
        visitSortKey(b).localeCompare(visitSortKey(a)),
      ),
    [history],
  );
  const prescriptions = history?.prescriptions ?? [];

  const profileFields = useMemo(
    () =>
      patient
        ? PROFILE_FIELDS.filter(
            ([key]) =>
              patient[key] !== undefined &&
              patient[key] !== null &&
              patient[key] !== "",
          )
        : [],
    [patient],
  );

  const handlePrint = useCallback(() => window.print(), []);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <PageShell>
        <SkeletonBlock label="Loading patient record" rows={2} />
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
          <SkeletonBlock
            label="Loading clinical history"
            rows={6}
            className="lg:col-span-8"
          />
          <SkeletonBlock
            label="Loading patient information"
            rows={4}
            className="lg:col-span-4"
          />
        </div>
      </PageShell>
    );
  }

  if (failed || !patient) {
    return (
      <PageShell>
        <ErrorPanel
          title="Unable to load this patient record"
          message="The record may have moved, or you may not have access to it."
        >
          <Link
            to={RECORDS_PATH}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-primary-700 transition-colors hover:text-primary-800"
          >
            <Icon name="arrow-left" className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>Back to all patients</span>
          </Link>
        </ErrorPanel>
      </PageShell>
    );
  }

  return (
    <PageShell>
      <PageHeader
        back={{ to: RECORDS_PATH, label: "All patients" }}
        title={patient.fullName}
        meta={
          <span className="rounded-lg bg-neutral-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-800">
            #{patient.patientId}
          </span>
        }
        actions={
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            onClick={handlePrint}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2"
          >
            <Icon name="print" className="h-4 w-4" />
            Print record
          </Button>
        }
      />

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 items-start gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
        <div className="lg:col-span-8">
          <Panel icon="clock" title="Visit history">
            {!historyState ? (
              <p role="status" className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
                Loading visit history...
              </p>
            ) : historyState.error ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600">
                Visit history is unavailable right now. Reload the page to try
                again.
              </p>
            ) : visits.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600">
                No visits are on record for this patient yet.
              </p>
            ) : (
              <ol className="relative space-y-6 border-l border-neutral-200 pl-6">
                {visits.map((visit) => (
                  <VisitEntry key={visit.appointmentId} visit={visit} />
                ))}
              </ol>
            )}
          </Panel>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-6 lg:col-span-4">
          <Panel icon="user" title="Patient information">
            {profileFields.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600">
                No additional details are on file.
              </p>
            ) : (
              <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                {profileFields.map(([key, label]) => (
                  <DetailTile key={key} label={label}>
                    {String(patient[key])}
                  </DetailTile>
                ))}
              </dl>
            )}
          </Panel>

          <Panel icon="pill" title="Prescriptions">
            {!historyState ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
                Loading prescriptions...
              </p>
            ) : prescriptions.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600">
                No prescriptions on record.
              </p>
            ) : (
              <ul className="space-y-2">
                {prescriptions.map((prescription, index) => (
                  <PrescriptionEntry
                    key={prescription.prescriptionId ?? index}
                    prescription={prescription}
                  />
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}
          </Panel>
        </div>
      </div>
    </PageShell>
  );
};

export default PatientDetails;

import {
  useCallback,
  useDeferredValue,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import Button from "../../components/Button";
import Icon from "../../components/Icon";
import Pagination from "../../components/Pagination";
import Table from "../../components/Table";
import { PageHeader, PageShell } from "../../components/doctor/PageLayout";
import {
  ErrorPanel,
  SectionTitle,
  SkeletonBlock,
} from "../../components/doctor/Panel";
import { getPatients } from "../../services/patientService";

/** `GET /patients` returns the whole directory, so paging is client-side. */
const PAGE_SIZE = 10;
const RECORDS_BASE_PATH = "/doctor/patient-records";

const matchesQuery = (patient, query) =>
  String(patient.fullName ?? "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .includes(query) || String(patient.patientId).includes(query);

/**
 * Patient directory: search, page and open a record. Fetch status is
 * `loading | ready | failed`; a failed load keeps the page on screen with a
 * retry instead of blanking it.
 */
const PatientRecordsPage = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [patients, setPatients] = useState([]);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("loading");
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [reloadKey, setReloadKey] = useState(0);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const deferredQuery = useDeferredValue(query);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    getPatients()
      .then((data) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setPatients(data);
        setPage(1);
        setStatus("ready");
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setStatus("failed");
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [reloadKey]);

  const retry = useCallback(() => {
    setStatus("loading");
    setReloadKey((key) => key + 1);
  }, []);

  const handleQueryChange = useCallback((event) => {
    setQuery(event.target.value);
    setPage(1);
  }, []);

  const normalizedQuery = deferredQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
  const filtered = useMemo(
    () =>
      normalizedQuery
        ? patients.filter((patient) => matchesQuery(patient, normalizedQuery))
        : patients,
    [patients, normalizedQuery],
  );

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / PAGE_SIZE));
  const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages);
  const startIndex = (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;
  const visiblePatients = useMemo(
    () => filtered.slice(startIndex, startIndex + PAGE_SIZE),
    [filtered, startIndex],
  );

  const columns = useMemo(
    () => [
      {
        key: "patientId",
        header: "Patient ID",
        render: (row) => (
          <span className="font-mono text-neutral-900">#{row.patientId}</span>
        ),
      },
      {
        key: "fullName",
        header: "Name",
        render: (row) => (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <span
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary-50 text-xs font-semibold text-primary-700"
            >
              {String(row.fullName ?? "?")
                .charAt(0)
                .toUpperCase()}
            </span>
            <span className="font-medium text-neutral-900">{row.fullName}</span>
          </div>
        ),
      },
      {
        key: "actions",
        header: "Actions",
        render: (row) => (
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="outline"
            onClick={() => navigate(`${RECORDS_BASE_PATH}/${row.patientId}`)}
          >
            View More
          </Button>
        ),
      },
    ],
    [navigate],
  );

  const total = patients.length;
  const countLabel = normalizedQuery
    ? `${filtered.length} of ${total} patients`
    : `${total} ${total === 1 ? "patient" : "patients"}`;

  return (
    <PageShell>
      <PageHeader
        title="Patient records"
        description={
          "All patients you can access. Choose View More to open a patient\u2019s record."
        }
        meta={
          status === "ready" ? (
            <p className="font-medium text-neutral-800">{countLabel}</p>
          ) : null
        }
      />

      {status === "loading" ? (
        <SkeletonBlock label="Loading patients" rows={6} />
      ) : status === "failed" ? (
        <ErrorPanel
          title="Unable to load patients"
          message="Please check your connection and try again."
          actionLabel="Retry"
          onAction={retry}
        />
      ) : (
        <section aria-labelledby="patient-directory-heading">
          <SectionTitle
            id="patient-directory-heading"
            aside={
              <div className="relative w-full max-w-xs">
                <label htmlFor="patient-search" className="sr-only">
                  Search patients
                </label>
                <Icon
                  name="search"
                  className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-neutral-400"
                />
                <input
                  id="patient-search"
                  type="search"
                  value={query}
                  onChange={handleQueryChange}
                  placeholder="Search by name or ID"
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white py-2 pl-9 pr-3 text-sm text-neutral-900 focus:border-primary-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500/20"
                />
              </div>
            }
          >
            All patients
          </SectionTitle>

          <Table
            columns={columns}
            data={visiblePatients}
            getRowKey={(row) => row.patientId}
            emptyTitle="No patients found."
            emptyMessage={
              normalizedQuery
                ? "No patients match your search. Try a different name or ID."
                : "There are no patient records available to you yet."
            }
            label="All patients"
          />

          {totalPages > 1 && (
            <Pagination
              className="mt-4"
              currentPage={currentPage}
              totalPages={totalPages}
              onPageChange={setPage}
            />
          )}
        </section>
      )}
    </PageShell>
  );
};

export default PatientRecordsPage;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\pages\doctor\TodaySchedulePage.jsx

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import Button from "../../components/Button";
import DatePicker from "../../components/DatePicker";
import Icon from "../../components/Icon";
import StatusBadge from "../../components/StatusBadge";
import Table from "../../components/Table";
import { PageHeader, PageShell } from "../../components/doctor/PageLayout";
import {
  ErrorPanel,
  SectionTitle,
  SkeletonBlock,
} from "../../components/doctor/Panel";

import { getAppointments } from "../../services/appointmentService";
import useQueuePolling from "../../hooks/useQueuePolling";
import usePageVisible from "../../hooks/usePageVisible";
import { formatDisplayDate, toLocalDateString } from "../../utils/dateUtils";
import { formatTimeSlot } from "../../utils/timeUtils";

const DASH = "\u2014";

/**
 * DOC - Doctor's Today Schedule / Consultation Queue.
 *
 * Server-authoritative order, scoped to the selected local date.
 * The queue refreshes approximately every 2 seconds while the tab is visible.
 *
 * This page is read-only. Starting a consultation opens DOC-04, where
 * notes, prescriptions and consultation completion are handled.
 */
const TodaySchedulePage = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const pageVisible = usePageVisible();

  const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState(() => toLocalDateString());
  const [records, setRecords] = useState([]);
  const [queueStatus, setQueueStatus] = useState("idle");

  const requestIdRef = useRef(0);
  const wasHiddenRef = useRef(false);

  const loading = queueStatus === "loading";
  const firstLoad = loading && records.length === 0;
  const failed = queueStatus === "failed";

  /**
   * Refresh appointments manually or through polling.
   * Request IDs prevent older requests from overwriting newer results.
   */
  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    const requestId = ++requestIdRef.current;

    setQueueStatus("loading");

    try {
      const appointments = await getAppointments({
        date: selectedDate,
      });

      if (requestId !== requestIdRef.current) {
        return;
      }

      setRecords(appointments);
      setQueueStatus("succeeded");
    } catch {
      if (requestId !== requestIdRef.current) {
        return;
      }

      setQueueStatus("failed");
    }
  }, [selectedDate]);

  /**
   * Initial fetch and re-fetch whenever the selected date changes.
   *
   * The request is started inside the effect rather than by calling
   * refresh(), avoiding the set-state-in-effect lint warning.
   */
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    const requestId = ++requestIdRef.current;

    const loadSchedule = async () => {
      setQueueStatus("loading");

      try {
        const appointments = await getAppointments({
          date: selectedDate,
        });

        if (cancelled || requestId !== requestIdRef.current) {
          return;
        }

        setRecords(appointments);
        setQueueStatus("succeeded");
      } catch {
        if (cancelled || requestId !== requestIdRef.current) {
          return;
        }

        setQueueStatus("failed");
      }
    };

    loadSchedule();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [selectedDate]);

  /**
   * Poll only while the tab is visible.
   */
  useQueuePolling({
    enabled: pageVisible,
    onTick: refresh,
  });

  /**
   * Refresh immediately when the user returns to the tab.
   */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pageVisible) {
      wasHiddenRef.current = true;
      return;
    }

    if (wasHiddenRef.current) {
      wasHiddenRef.current = false;
      refresh();
    }
  }, [pageVisible, refresh]);

  const handleDateChange = useCallback((event) => {
    setSelectedDate(event.target.value || toLocalDateString());
  }, []);

  const columns = useMemo(
    () => [
      {
        key: "queuePosition",
        header: "#",
        render: (row) => row.appointmentId ?? DASH,
      },
      {
        key: "patientFullName",
        header: "Patient",
        render: (row) => (
          <span className="font-medium text-neutral-900">
            {row.patientFullName ?? DASH}
          </span>
        ),
      },
      {
        key: "timeSlot",
        header: "Time",
        render: (row) => formatTimeSlot(row.timeSlot),
      },
      {
        key: "status",
        header: "Status",
        render: (row) => <StatusBadge status={row.status} />,
      },
      {
        key: "actions",
        header: "Actions",
        render: (row) =>
          row.status === "CANCELLED" ? (
            DASH
          ) : (
            <Button
              size="sm"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={() =>
                navigate(`/doctor/consultation/${row.appointmentId}`)
              }
            >
              {row.status === "COMPLETED"
                ? "View Consultation"
                : "Start Consultation"}
            </Button>
          ),
      },
    ],
    [navigate],
  );

  return (
    <PageShell>
      <PageHeader
        title={"Today\u2019s schedule"}
        description="Review appointments for the selected date and open the consultation screen to treat patients and complete appointments."
        meta={
          <>
            <p className="font-medium text-neutral-800">
              {formatDisplayDate(selectedDate)}
            </p>

            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-emerald-700">
              <span
                aria-hidden="true"
                className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 motion-safe:animate-pulse"
              />
              {pageVisible ? "Live" : "Paused"}
            </span>
          </>
        }
        actions={
          <>
            <DatePicker
              id="schedule-date"
              name="scheduleDate"
              label="Date"
              value={selectedDate}
              onChange={handleDateChange}
              className="min-w-40"
            />

            <Button
              variant="outline"
              onClick={refresh}
              disabled={firstLoad}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2"
            >
              <Icon name="refresh" className="h-4 w-4" />
              Refresh
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      />

      <section aria-labelledby="schedule-queue-heading">
        <SectionTitle id="schedule-queue-heading">
          Appointment queue
        </SectionTitle>

        {firstLoad ? (
          <SkeletonBlock label="Loading appointment queue" rows={5} />
        ) : failed ? (
          <ErrorPanel
            title="Unable to load the schedule"
            message="Please check your connection and try again."
            actionLabel="Retry"
            onAction={refresh}
            actionLoading={loading}
          />
        ) : (
          <Table
            columns={columns}
            data={records}
            getRowKey={(row) => row.appointmentId}
            emptyTitle="No appointments scheduled for the selected date."
            emptyMessage="Pick another date or refresh to check again."
            label="Appointment queue for the selected date"
          />
        )}
      </section>
    </PageShell>
  );
};

export default TodaySchedulePage;
