import { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import Button from "../../components/Button";
import ErrorMessage from "../../components/ErrorMessage";
import Input from "../../components/Input";
import Icon from "../../components/Icon";
import { forgotPassword } from "../../services/authService";

const EMAIL_REGEX = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

const ForgotPasswordPage = () => {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);

  const validateEmail = (val) => {
    if (!val || !val.trim()) {
      return "Email is required.";
    }
    if (!EMAIL_REGEX.test(val.trim())) {
      return "Please enter a valid email address.";
    }
    return "";
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSubmitted(true);
    setError("");

    const validationMsg = validateEmail(email);
    if (validationMsg) {
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await forgotPassword(email.trim());
      setSuccess(true);
    } catch (err) {
      const status = err?.response?.status;
      if (status === 429) {
        setError("Too many attempts, please try again shortly.");
      } else {
        setError("Something went wrong. Please try again shortly.");
      }
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="relative flex min-h-screen w-full items-center justify-center overflow-hidden bg-slate-50 px-4 py-8 lg:px-8">
      {/* Background decoration - optimized with reduced blur complexity for better paint times */}
      <div
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="pointer-events-none absolute -left-20 -top-20 h-64 w-64 rounded-full bg-blue-100/40 blur-2xl"
      />
      <div
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="pointer-events-none absolute -bottom-20 right-0 h-64 w-64 rounded-full bg-indigo-100/40 blur-2xl"
      />

      <div className="relative w-full max-w-md">
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 bg-white p-6 shadow-xl shadow-slate-200/50 sm:p-8">
          {/* Header logo / branding */}
          <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
            <Link
              to="/login"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 transition hover:text-blue-600 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-600 rounded-md"
            >
              <Icon name="arrow-left" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>Back to sign in</span>
            </Link>

            <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-md shadow-blue-600/20">
              <Icon name="stethoscope" className="h-4 w-4" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-6">
            <div className="mb-2.5 flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 ring-1 ring-blue-100">
              <Icon name="key-round" className="h-5 w-5" />
            </div>

            <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-slate-900 sm:text-2xl">
              Forgot password?
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-500">
              Enter your registered email address and we will send you
              instructions to reset your password.
            </p>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <ErrorMessage
              className="mb-4 text-xs"
              message={error}
              role="alert"
            />
          )}

          {success ? (
            <div
              role="alert"
              aria-live="polite"
              className="rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-4 text-emerald-900"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <span className="flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600">
                  <Icon name="circle-check" className="h-4 w-4" />
                </span>
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-emerald-950">
                    Check your email
                  </h2>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-emerald-800">
                    If an account exists for this email, a reset link has been
                    sent.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-5 border-t border-emerald-200/60 pt-4">
                <Link
                  to="/login"
                  className="group flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-md shadow-emerald-600/20 transition hover:bg-emerald-700 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-4 focus-visible:ring-emerald-600/25"
                >
                  <Icon name="arrow-left" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  <span>Return to login</span>
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
              <div>
                <Input
                  id="reset-email"
                  name="email"
                  type="email"
                  label="Registered Email"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  validate={validateEmail}
                  autoComplete="email"
                  required
                  disabled={submitting}
                  submitted={submitted}
                  placeholder="doctor@onecare.com"
                  leftIcon={
                    <Icon name="mail" className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />
                  }
                />
              </div>

              <Button
                type="submit"
                className="group flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-md shadow-blue-600/10 transition duration-200 hover:bg-blue-700 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-4 focus-visible:ring-blue-600/25 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
                loading={submitting}
                disabled={submitting}
              >
                <span>Send reset instructions</span>
                <Icon
                  name="arrow-right"
                  className="h-4 w-4 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1"
                />
              </Button>

              <div className="pt-2 text-center">
                <Link
                  to="/login"
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 hover:text-blue-600 hover:underline focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-600 rounded"
                >
                  Remember your password?{" "}
                  <span className="font-semibold text-blue-600">Sign in</span>
                </Link>
              </div>
            </form>
          )}

          <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 text-center text-[10px] leading-relaxed text-slate-400">
            <Icon name="shield" className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
            <span>
              Secure password recovery for OneCare authorized personnel.
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
};

export default ForgotPasswordPage;

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  useNavigate,
  useLocation,
  useSearchParams,
  Link,
} from "react-router-dom";
import Button from "../../components/Button";
import ErrorMessage from "../../components/ErrorMessage";
import Input from "../../components/Input";
import Icon from "../../components/Icon";
import { login } from "../../services/authService";

const LoginPage = () => {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const sessionExpired = searchParams.get("reason") === "session-expired";
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const [passwordResetSuccess] = useState(
    () => location.state?.passwordResetSuccess || "",
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!passwordResetSuccess) return;
    navigate(`${location.pathname}${location.search}${location.hash}`, {
      replace: true,
      state: null,
    });
  }, [
    location.hash,
    location.pathname,
    location.search,
    navigate,
    passwordResetSuccess,
  ]);

  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSubmitted(true);
    setError("");

    if (!username.trim() || !password) return;

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const result = await login({
        username: username.trim(),
        password,
      });

      const { redirectUrl } = result.data;
      const requestedPath = location.state?.from;

      const target =
        typeof requestedPath === "string" && requestedPath.startsWith("/")
          ? requestedPath
          : redirectUrl;

      if (
        typeof target !== "string" ||
        !target.startsWith("/") ||
        target.startsWith("//")
      ) {
        setError(
          "Unable to determine your destination. Please contact support.",
        );
        return;
      }

      navigate(target, { replace: true });
    } catch (err) {
      const status = err?.response?.status;
      const message = err?.response?.data?.message;

      if (status === 429) {
        setError("Too many attempts, try again shortly");
      } else if (status === 403 || status === 423) {
        setError(
          message ||
            "Account locked. Wait for timed unlock, contact Super Admin, or use Forgot Password.",
        );
      } else if (status === 401) {
        setError("Invalid username or password.");
      } else {
        setError("Something went wrong. Please try again.");
      }
      setPassword("");
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="relative flex h-screen w-full items-center justify-center overflow-hidden bg-slate-50 px-4 py-4 lg:px-8">
      {/* Background blobs - optimized for lighter paint overhead */}
      <div
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="pointer-events-none absolute -left-20 -top-20 h-64 w-64 rounded-full bg-blue-100/40 blur-2xl"
      />
      <div
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="pointer-events-none absolute -bottom-20 right-0 h-64 w-64 rounded-full bg-indigo-100/40 blur-2xl"
      />

      {/* Main card container optimized for non-scrollable viewport fit */}
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-5xl my-auto">
        <div className="grid w-full overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 bg-white shadow-xl shadow-slate-200/50 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_0.92fr]">
          {/* Left Branding Section */}
          <section
            aria-hidden="true"
            className="hidden relative flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-950 via-blue-950 to-indigo-950 p-6 text-white lg:flex xl:p-8"
          >
            <div
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="absolute -right-24 top-20 h-80 w-80 rounded-full border border-white/10"
            />
            <div
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="absolute -right-12 top-32 h-56 w-56 rounded-full border border-white/10"
            />
            <div
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="absolute -bottom-24 -left-20 h-72 w-72 rounded-full bg-blue-500/10 blur-2xl"
            />

            <div className="relative z-10">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-white/10 shadow-lg backdrop-blur">
                  <Icon name="stethoscope" className="h-4 w-4 text-blue-300" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-extrabold tracking-tight">
                    OneCare
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[10px] font-medium tracking-[0.18em] text-blue-200">
                    FAMILY CLINIC
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-6 max-w-md">
                <div className="mb-3 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-blue-400/20 bg-blue-400/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-200">
                  <Icon name="activity" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  Connected care. Simplified.
                </div>

                <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold leading-tight tracking-tight xl:text-3xl text-white">
                  Better care
                  <br />
                  starts with
                  <span className="text-blue-300"> better systems.</span>
                </h1>

                <p className="mt-3 max-w-sm text-xs leading-5 text-slate-300">
                  Your central workspace for clinical operations, patient care,
                  and healthcare management â€” all in one place.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-5 space-y-2.5">
                {[
                  "A unified clinical workspace",
                  "Role-based access to your tools",
                  "Streamlined healthcare workflows",
                ].map((item) => (
                  <div key={item} className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <span className="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-blue-400/15">
                      <Icon
                        name="circle-check"
                        className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-blue-300"
                      />
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-200">{item}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="relative z-10 mt-6 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-white/10 pt-3">
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                Healthcare Management System
              </p>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-blue-200">
                <Icon name="shield" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                Secure workspace
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* Right Form Section */}
          <section className="flex min-w-0 flex-col justify-center px-5 py-6 sm:px-8 sm:py-8 lg:px-8 xl:px-10">
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-3 lg:hidden">
              <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20">
                <Icon name="stethoscope" className="h-4 w-4" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-extrabold tracking-tight">
                  OneCare Family Clinic
                </p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                  Healthcare Management System
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-md">
              <div className="mb-4">
                <div className="mb-2.5 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 ring-1 ring-blue-100">
                  <Icon name="heart-pulse" className="h-4 w-4" />
                </div>

                <p className="mb-1 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-600">
                  Welcome back
                </p>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-slate-900 xl:text-2xl">
                  Sign in to OneCare
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-normal text-slate-500">
                  Enter your professional credentials to access your workspace.
                </p>
              </div>

              {sessionExpired && (
                <p
                  role="alert"
                  aria-live="polite"
                  className="mb-3 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-2.5 text-xs text-red-800"
                >
                  Your session has expired. Please sign in again.
                </p>
              )}

              {passwordResetSuccess && (
                <p
                  role="status"
                  aria-live="polite"
                  className="mb-3 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-2.5 text-xs text-emerald-900"
                >
                  {passwordResetSuccess}
                </p>
              )}

              {error && (
                <ErrorMessage
                  className="mb-3 text-xs"
                  message={error}
                  role="alert"
                />
              )}

              <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-3.5" noValidate>
                <div>
                  <Input
                    id="professional-id"
                    name="username"
                    type="text"
                    label="Username"
                    value={username}
                    onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)}
                    autoComplete="username"
                    required
                    disabled={submitting}
                    submitted={submitted}
                    placeholder="Enter your professional ID"
                    leftIcon={
                      <Icon name="user" className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />
                    }
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <Link
                      to="/forgot-password"
                      className="text-xs font-semibold text-blue-600 transition hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-600 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 rounded"
                    >
                      Forgot password?
                    </Link>
                  </div>

                  <div className="group relative">
                    <Input
                      id="professional-password"
                      name="password"
                      type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                      label="Password"
                      value={password}
                      onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                      autoComplete="current-password"
                      required
                      disabled={submitting}
                      submitted={submitted}
                      placeholder="Enter your password"
                      leftIcon={
                        <Icon name="lock" className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />

                      rightIcon={
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setShowPassword((v) => !v)}
                          aria-label={

                          }
                          aria-pressed={showPassword}
                          className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 flex h-8 w-8 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-600"
                        >
                          <Icon
                            name={showPassword ? "eye-off" : "eye"}
                            className="h-4 w-4"
                          />
                        </button>
                      }
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <Button
                  type="submit"
                  className="group flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/15 transition duration-200 hover:bg-blue-700 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-4 focus-visible:ring-blue-600/25 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
                  loading={submitting}
                  disabled={submitting}
                >
                  <span>Sign in securely</span>
                  <Icon
                    name="arrow-right"
                    className="h-4 w-4 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1"
                  />
                </Button>
              </form>

              <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border-t border-slate-100 pt-3 text-center text-[10px] leading-relaxed text-slate-400">
                <Icon name="shield" className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                <span>
                  Authorized access only. Protect patient information and sign
                  out when finished.
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\pages\public\ResetPasswordPage.jsx

import { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import {
  Link,
  useLocation,
  useNavigate,
  useSearchParams,
} from "react-router-dom";
import Button from "../../components/Button";
import ErrorMessage from "../../components/ErrorMessage";
import Input from "../../components/Input";
import Icon from "../../components/Icon";
import { resetPassword } from "../../services/authService";

const ResetPasswordPage = () => {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();

  const [token, setToken] = useState(() => searchParams.get("token") || "");
  const tokenFromUrl = token !== "";
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [showConfirmPassword, setShowConfirmPassword] = useState(false);

  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [invalidLink, setInvalidLink] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!searchParams.has("token")) return;
    const nextParams = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);
    nextParams.delete("token");
    navigate(
      `${location.pathname}${nextParams.size ? `?${nextParams}` : ""}${location.hash}`,
      { replace: true },
    );
  }, [location.hash, location.pathname, navigate, searchParams]);

  const passwordCriteria = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      minLength: newPassword.length >= 8 && newPassword.length <= 64,
      hasLetter: /[a-zA-Z]/.test(newPassword),
      hasNumberOrSymbol: /[\d\W]/.test(newPassword),
    };
  }, [newPassword]);

  const validatePassword = (pwd) => {
    if (!pwd) return "New password is required.";
    if (pwd.length < 8) return "Password must be at least 8 characters.";
    if (pwd.length > 64) return "Password cannot exceed 64 characters.";
    return "";
  };

  const validateConfirmPassword = (val) => {
    if (!val) return "Please confirm your new password.";
    if (val !== newPassword) return "Passwords do not match.";
    return "";
  };

  const validateToken = (t) => {
    if (!t || !t.trim()) return "Reset token is required.";
    return "";
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSubmitted(true);
    setError("");

    const tokenErr = validateToken(token);
    const pwdErr = validatePassword(newPassword);
    const confirmErr = validateConfirmPassword(confirmPassword);

    if (tokenErr || pwdErr || confirmErr) {
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await resetPassword(token, newPassword);
      navigate("/login", {
        replace: true,
        state: {
          passwordResetSuccess:
            "Your password has been reset. You can now sign in.",
        },
      });
    } catch (err) {
      const status = err?.response?.status;
      const backendMessage = err?.response?.data?.message;

      if (status === 400 && backendMessage === "Invalid or expired token") {
        setInvalidLink(true);
      } else if (status === 429) {
        setError("Too many attempts. Please try again later.");
      } else {
        setError("Something went wrong. Please try again.");
      }
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="relative flex min-h-screen w-full items-center justify-center overflow-hidden bg-slate-50 px-4 py-8 lg:px-8">
      {/* Background decoration - optimized for lighter paint overhead */}
      <div
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="pointer-events-none absolute -left-20 -top-20 h-64 w-64 rounded-full bg-blue-100/40 blur-2xl"
      />
      <div
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="pointer-events-none absolute -bottom-20 right-0 h-64 w-64 rounded-full bg-indigo-100/40 blur-2xl"
      />

      <div className="relative w-full max-w-md">
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200/80 bg-white p-6 shadow-xl shadow-slate-200/50 sm:p-8">
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
            <Link
              to="/login"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 transition hover:text-blue-600 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-600 rounded-md"
            >
              <Icon name="arrow-left" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>Back to sign in</span>
            </Link>

            <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-md shadow-blue-600/20">
              <Icon name="stethoscope" className="h-4 w-4" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-6">
            <div className="mb-2.5 flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 ring-1 ring-blue-100">
              <Icon name="lock" className="h-5 w-5" />
            </div>

            <h1 className="text-xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-slate-900 sm:text-2xl">
              Reset your password
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-500">
              Choose a strong, unique password between 8 and 64 characters to
              secure your account.
            </p>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <ErrorMessage
              className="mb-4 text-xs"
              message={error}
              role="alert"
            />
          )}

          {invalidLink ? (
            <section
              aria-labelledby="invalid-link-heading"
              role="alert"
              className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-4 text-amber-950"
            >
              <h2 id="invalid-link-heading" className="text-sm font-bold">
                This reset link is invalid
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed">
                The link may have expired or already been used. Request a new
                reset link to continue.
              </p>
              <div className="mt-4 flex justify-between text-xs font-semibold">
                <Link
                  to="/forgot-password"
                  className="text-blue-700 hover:underline"
                >
                  Request a new link
                </Link>
                <Link to="/login" className="text-blue-700 hover:underline">
                  Go to login
                </Link>
              </div>
            </section>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
              {!tokenFromUrl && (
                <div>
                  <Input
                    id="reset-token"
                    name="token"
                    type="text"
                    label="Reset Token"
                    value={token}
                    onChange={(e) => setToken(e.target.value)}
                    validate={validateToken}
                    required
                    disabled={submitting}
                    submitted={submitted}
                    placeholder="Paste your reset token"
                    hint="The token provided in your reset email"
                    leftIcon={
                      <Icon
                        name="key-round"
                        className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-400"
                      />
                    }
                  />
                </div>
              )}

              <div>
                <Input
                  id="new-password"
                  name="newPassword"
                  type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                  label="New Password"
                  value={newPassword}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                  validate={validatePassword}
                  autoComplete="new-password"
                  required
                  disabled={submitting}
                  submitted={submitted}
                  placeholder="Enter new password (8-64 characters)"
                  leftIcon={
                    <Icon name="lock" className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />
                  }
                  rightIcon={
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setShowPassword((v) => !v)}
                      aria-label={
                        showPassword ? "Hide password" : "Show password"
                      }
                      aria-pressed={showPassword}
                      className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 flex h-8 w-8 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-600"
                    >
                      <Icon
                        name={showPassword ? "eye-off" : "eye"}
                        className="h-4 w-4"
                      />
                    </button>
                  }
                />

                <div className="mt-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 p-2.5 text-[11px] text-slate-600 border border-slate-100">
                  <p className="font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Password requirements:
                  </p>
                  <ul className="space-y-1">
                    <li className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <Icon
                        name={
                          passwordCriteria.minLength
                            ? "circle-check"
                            : "circle-help"
                        }
                        className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 ${
                          passwordCriteria.minLength
                            ? "text-emerald-600"
                            : "text-slate-400"
                        }`}
                      />
                      <span
                        className={
                          passwordCriteria.minLength
                            ? "text-slate-900 font-medium"
                            : ""
                        }
                      >
                        Between 8 and 64 characters
                      </span>
                    </li>
                  </ul>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <Input
                  id="confirm-password"
                  name="confirmPassword"
                  type={showConfirmPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                  label="Confirm New Password"
                  value={confirmPassword}
                  onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                  validate={validateConfirmPassword}
                  autoComplete="new-password"
                  required
                  disabled={submitting}
                  submitted={submitted}
                  placeholder="Re-enter your new password"
                  leftIcon={
                    <Icon name="lock" className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />
                  }
                  rightIcon={
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setShowConfirmPassword((v) => !v)}
                      aria-label={
                        showConfirmPassword
                          ? "Hide confirm password"
                          : "Show confirm password"
                      }
                      aria-pressed={showConfirmPassword}
                      className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 flex h-8 w-8 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-600"
                    >
                      <Icon
                        name={showConfirmPassword ? "eye-off" : "eye"}
                        className="h-4 w-4"
                      />
                    </button>
                  }
                />
              </div>

              <Button
                type="submit"
                className="group flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/10 transition duration-200 hover:bg-blue-700 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-4 focus-visible:ring-blue-600/25 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
                loading={submitting}
                disabled={submitting}
              >
                <span>Reset password</span>
                <Icon
                  name="arrow-right"
                  className="h-4 w-4 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1"
                />
              </Button>
            </form>
          )}

          <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 text-center text-[10px] leading-relaxed text-slate-400">
            <Icon name="shield" className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
            <span>
              Authorized access only. OneCare Healthcare Management System.
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
};

export default ResetPasswordPage;

            {" "}

          Wide tables scroll inside their own container (AC3). On a 360 px

                {step}

              <span

                style={{ width: spacing[step] }}

              <span className="font-mono text-xs text-neutral-600">
                {spacing[step]}
              </span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-4">
          {RADIUS_SAMPLES.map((sample) => (
            <div key={sample.label} className="text-center">
              <div
                className={`mb-2 h-16 w-16 border-2 border-primary-500 bg-primary-100 ${sample.className}`}
              />
              <span className="font-mono text-xs text-neutral-600">
                {sample.label}
              </span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-4 font-mono text-xs text-neutral-600">
          borderRadius token values:{" "}
          {Object.entries(borderRadius)
            .map(([key, value]) => `${key}=${value}`)
            .join(", ")}
        </p>
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="fonts-heading">
        <h2 id="fonts-heading" className={SECTION_TITLE}>
          Font stacks
        </h2>
        <ul className="space-y-2 text-base text-neutral-700">
          {Object.entries(fontFamily).map(([key, stack]) => (
            <li key={key}>
              <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-800">{key}:</span>{" "}
              <span className="font-mono text-sm text-neutral-600">
                {stack.join(", ")}
              </span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ThemeReferencePage;

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, memo } from "react";
import Button from "../../components/Button";
import ConfirmDialog from "../../components/ConfirmDialog";
import ErrorMessage from "../../components/ErrorMessage";
import Icon from "../../components/Icon";
import SearchBar from "../../components/SearchBar";
import Select from "../../components/Select";
import StatCard from "../../components/StatCard";
import Table from "../../components/Table";
import Toast from "../../components/Toast";
import {
  PERMISSION_CODES,
  PERMISSION_MATRIX,
} from "../../constants/permissions";
import { ROLES } from "../../constants/roles";
import {
  getRolePermissions,
  setRolePermissions,
} from "../../services/rolePermissionService";

const ALL_CATEGORIES = "ALL";
const UNCATEGORIZED = "Uncategorized";
const ROLE_ORDER = Object.values(ROLES);
const IMMUTABLE_ROLE = ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN;
const SUPER_ADMIN_READONLY_TITLE =
  "BR005: SUPER_ADMIN permissions are system-locked and immutable.";

const roleLabel = (role) => String(role ?? "").replace(/_/g, " ");

/**
 * Sub-component for scope cell rendering to avoid unneeded re-renders.
 */
const ScopeInfoCell = memo(({ row }) => (
  <div className="max-w-md whitespace-normal py-1">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
      <span className="rounded-md bg-warning-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-warning-800 border border-warning-200">
        {row.category}
      </span>
      <span className="font-mono text-xs text-neutral-500">{row.code}</span>
      {row.privileged && (
        <span className="rounded-md bg-danger-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-danger-700 border border-danger-200">
          Privileged
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
    <p className="mt-1 font-semibold text-neutral-900 text-sm">{row.name}</p>
    <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-neutral-600 line-clamp-2 hover:line-clamp-none">
      {row.description}
    </p>
  </div>
));
ScopeInfoCell.displayName = "ScopeInfoCell";

/**
 * Individual Matrix Cell Component with localized state & memoization.
 */
const MatrixCell = memo(
  ({
    row,
    role,
    isGranted,
    isSaving,
    totalRoleGrants,
    onToggle,
  }) => {
    const isSuperAdmin = role === IMMUTABLE_ROLE;
    const isLastGranted = isGranted && totalRoleGrants <= 1; // BR006
    const isPrivilegedForNonAdmin = Boolean(row.privileged) && !isSuperAdmin; // BR004
    const isBlocked = isLastGranted || isPrivilegedForNonAdmin;

    const blockReason = isLastGranted
      ? "BR006: A role's permission set cannot be empty"
      : isPrivilegedForNonAdmin
        ? "BR004: Only SUPER_ADMIN may hold this privileged scope"
        : null;

    if (isSuperAdmin) {
      return (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center">
          <span
            className="inline-flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-neutral-50"
            title={SUPER_ADMIN_READONLY_TITLE}
            aria-label={`SUPER_ADMIN ${isGranted ? "Authorized" : "Denied"} (Immutable)`}
          >
            <Icon
              name={isGranted ? "circle-check" : "circle-x"}
              className={`h-5 w-5 ${isGranted ? "text-success-600" : "text-neutral-300"
                }`}
            />
          </span>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onToggle(row.code, role, row)}
          disabled={isSaving || isBlocked}
          aria-pressed={isGranted}
          aria-label={`${isGranted ? "Revoke" : "Grant"} ${row.name} for ${roleLabel(
            role
          )}`}
          title={
            isBlocked
              ? blockReason
              : `${isGranted ? "Authorized" : "Denied"} â€” click to toggle`
          }
          className={`relative inline-flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg transition-all duration-150 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary-500 ${isBlocked
              ? "cursor-not-allowed opacity-40 bg-neutral-50"
              : isGranted
                ? "bg-success-50 hover:bg-success-100 text-success-700"
                : "bg-neutral-50 hover:bg-neutral-100 text-neutral-400"
            }`}
        >
          {isSaving ? (
            <Icon name="loader" className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-primary-600" />
          ) : (
            <Icon
              name={isGranted ? "circle-check" : "circle-x"}
              className={`h-5 w-5 ${isGranted ? "text-success-600" : "text-neutral-400"
                }`}
            />
          )}
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }
);
MatrixCell.displayName = "MatrixCell";

const AccessControlPage = () => {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("loading"); // loading | ready | failed
  const [grantsByRole, setGrantsByRole] = useState({});
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState(null);
  const [savingKey, setSavingKey] = useState(null); // tracking cell key: `${role}:${code}`

  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [debouncedSearch, setDebouncedSearch] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(ALL_CATEGORIES);

  const [toast, setToast] = useState(null);
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState(null);
  const searchInputRef = useRef(null);

  // Debounce search query to keep matrix filtering smooth
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setDebouncedSearch(searchQuery), 150);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [searchQuery]);

  // Keyboard shortcut Ctrl/Cmd + F for search focus
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e) => {
      if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key === "f") {
        e.preventDefault();
        searchInputRef.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  }, []);

  // Fetch all permissions in parallel
  const fetchPermissions = useCallback(
    () =>
      Promise.all(
        ROLE_ORDER.map(async (role) => {
          const { permissions } = await getRolePermissions(role);
          return [role, permissions];
        })
      ),
    []
  );

  const loadPermissions = useCallback(async () => {
    setStatus("loading");
    setLoadError(null);
    try {
      const results = await fetchPermissions();
      setGrantsByRole(Object.fromEntries(results));
      setStatus("ready");
    } catch (error) {
      setLoadError(error?.userMessage ?? "Failed to load permissions matrix.");
      setStatus("failed");
    }
  }, [fetchPermissions]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetchPermissions()
      .then((results) => {
        if (!active) return;
        setGrantsByRole(Object.fromEntries(results));
        setStatus("ready");
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        if (!active) return;
        setLoadError(error?.userMessage ?? "Failed to load permissions matrix.");
        setStatus("failed");
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [fetchPermissions]);

  // Fast Set lookups per role: Map<role, Set<code/permission>>
  const grantsByRoleSets = useMemo(() => {
    const map = {};
    for (const [role, permissions] of Object.entries(grantsByRole)) {
      map[role] = new Set(permissions);
    }
    return map;
  }, [grantsByRole]);

  // Catalog metadata merged with dynamic extra backend permissions
  const catalog = useMemo(() => {
    const grantedCodes = Object.values(grantsByRole).flat();
    const extras = [...new Set(grantedCodes)]
      .filter((code) => !PERMISSION_CODES.includes(code))
      .sort();

    return [
      ...PERMISSION_MATRIX,
      ...extras.map((code) => ({
        code,
        category: UNCATEGORIZED,
        name: code,
        description:
          "Granted through the backend API but absent from local catalog metadata.",
      })),
    ];
  }, [grantsByRole]);

  const categoryOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const seen = new Set();
    for (const entry of catalog) {
      if (entry.category) seen.add(entry.category);
    }
    return [
      { value: ALL_CATEGORIES, label: "All Categories" },
      ...Array.from(seen).map((val) => ({ value: val, label: val })),
    ];
  }, [catalog]);

  const filteredPermissions = useMemo(() => {
    const query = debouncedSearch.trim().toLowerCase();
    return catalog.filter((entry) => {
      const matchesCategory =
        category === ALL_CATEGORIES || entry.category === category;
      const matchesText =
        !query ||
        entry.code.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        entry.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        entry.description.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        entry.category.toLowerCase().includes(query);
      return matchesCategory && matchesText;
    });
  }, [catalog, category, debouncedSearch]);

  const totalGrantedEdges = useMemo(
    () =>
      Object.values(grantsByRole).reduce(
        (sum, list) => sum + (list?.length || 0),
        0
      ),
    [grantsByRole]
  );

  /** Optimistic update handler with failure fallback */
  const applyRolePermissions = useCallback(async (
    role,
    nextPermissions,
    entry,
    wasGranted
  ) => {
    const key = `${role}:${entry.code}`;
    setSavingKey(key);

    // Backup state for rollback on error
    const previousPermissions = grantsByRole[role] ?? [];

    // Optimistically apply state change
    setGrantsByRole((prev) => ({ ...prev, [role]: nextPermissions }));

    try {
      const updated = await setRolePermissions(role, nextPermissions);
      setGrantsByRole((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        [role]: updated.permissions,
      }));
      setToast({
        type: "success",
        message: `${roleLabel(role)} updated: ${entry.name} ${wasGranted ? "revoked" : "granted"
          }.`,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      // Revert optimistic update
      setGrantsByRole((prev) => ({ ...prev, [role]: previousPermissions }));
      setToast({
        type: "error",
        message:
          error?.userMessage ??
          error?.message ??
          "Failed to update permission set on server.",
      });
    } finally {
      setSavingKey(null);
    }
  }, [grantsByRole]);

  const requestToggle = useCallback(
    (code, role, entry) => {
      const currentList = grantsByRole[role] ?? [];
      const isGranted = currentList.includes(code);
      const nextList = isGranted
        ? currentList.filter((c) => c !== code)
        : [...currentList, code];

      setConfirm({
        title: isGranted ? "Revoke Security Scope?" : "Grant Security Scope?",
        message: `Are you sure you want to ${isGranted ? "revoke" : "grant"
          } "${entry.name}" (${code}) for [${roleLabel(
            role
          )}]? Changes take effect immediately.`,
        confirmLabel: isGranted ? "Confirm Revocation" : "Authorize Scope",
        variant: isGranted ? "danger" : "primary",
        run: () => applyRolePermissions(role, nextList, entry, isGranted),
      });
    },
    [applyRolePermissions, grantsByRole]
  );

  const permissionColumns = useMemo(
    () => [
      {
        key: "scope",
        header: "Permission Scope",
        render: (row) => <ScopeInfoCell row={row} />,
      },
      ...ROLE_ORDER.map((role) => ({
        key: role,
        header: roleLabel(role),
        headerClass: "text-center",
        render: (row) => {
          const roleSet = grantsByRoleSets[role];
          const isGranted = roleSet ? roleSet.has(row.code) : false;
          const totalGrants = grantsByRole[role]?.length || 0;
          const isSaving = savingKey === `${role}:${row.code}`;

          return (
            <MatrixCell
              row={row}
              role={role}
              isGranted={isGranted}
              isSaving={isSaving}
              totalRoleGrants={totalGrants}
              onToggle={requestToggle}
            />
          );
        },
      })),
    ],
    [grantsByRoleSets, grantsByRole, savingKey, requestToggle]
  );

  const matrixPanel = (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Search & Domain Controls */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-4 shadow-xs sm:p-5">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <div className="relative flex-1 max-w-md">
            <SearchBar
              ref={searchInputRef}
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={setSearchQuery}
              placeholder="Search scope, code, or description... (âŒ˜F)"
              searchLabel="Search permission scopes"
              className="w-full"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <Select
              id="category-filter"
              name="category-filter"
              label="Domain"
              options={categoryOptions}
              value={category}
              onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value || ALL_CATEGORIES)}
              className="w-full sm:w-56"
            />
            {(searchQuery || category !== ALL_CATEGORIES) && (
              <Button
                variant="tertiary"
                onClick={() => {
                  setSearchQuery("");
                  setCategory(ALL_CATEGORIES);
                }}
                className="mt-6 sm:mt-0 text-xs text-neutral-500 hover:text-neutral-800"
              >
                Clear Filters
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Matrix Main Section */}
      <section aria-labelledby="permission-matrix-heading">
        {status === "ready" && (
          <div className="mb-3 flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <div>
              <h2
                id="permission-matrix-heading"
                className="text-base font-semibold text-neutral-900"
              >
                Granular Permission Boundaries
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500">
                Click any cell indicator to toggle role access. Changes persist directly to the security backend.
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs text-neutral-600">
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-medium">
                <Icon name="circle-check" className="h-4 w-4 text-success-600" />
                Authorized
              </span>
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-medium">
                <Icon name="circle-x" className="h-4 w-4 text-neutral-400" />
                Denied
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {status === "loading" ? (
          <div
            role="status"
            aria-label="Loading matrix"
            className="space-y-3 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xs animate-pulse"
          >
            {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="flex gap-4">
                <div className="h-6 w-1/3 rounded bg-neutral-100" />
                <div className="h-6 w-1/6 rounded bg-neutral-100" />
                <div className="h-6 w-1/6 rounded bg-neutral-100" />
                <div className="h-6 w-1/6 rounded bg-neutral-100" />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : status === "failed" ? (
          <div className="space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xs text-center">
            <ErrorMessage
              title="Unable to load permission entitlements"
              message={loadError}
            />
            <Button
              onClick={loadPermissions}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 mt-2"
            >
              <Icon name="refresh" className="h-4 w-4" />
              Retry Connection
            </Button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white shadow-xs">
            <Table
              columns={permissionColumns}
              data={filteredPermissions}
              getRowKey={(row) => row.code}
              emptyTitle="No matching permissions found"
              emptyMessage={
                searchQuery || category !== ALL_CATEGORIES
                  ? "No permission scopes match your search or domain query."
                  : "The permission matrix catalog is empty."
              }
              label="Role entitlement matrix"
            />
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-6 p-4 sm:p-6">
      {/* Header Summary Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3">
        <StatCard
          label="Role Profiles"
          value={`${ROLE_ORDER.length} Roles`}
          chip="RBAC"
          icon="key-round"
          iconClass="bg-warning-50 text-warning-700"
          chipClass="bg-warning-100 text-warning-900"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Active Scopes"
          value={`${catalog.length} Catalog Scopes`}
          chip="Enforced"
          icon="shield"
          iconClass="bg-primary-50 text-primary-700"
          chipClass="bg-primary-100 text-primary-800"
        />
        <StatCard
          label="Granted Entitlements"
          value={String(totalGrantedEdges)}
          chip={`of ${catalog.length * ROLE_ORDER.length} total max`}
          icon="circle-check"
          iconClass="bg-success-50 text-success-700"
          chipClass="bg-success-100 text-success-800"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Role Entitlement Matrix */}
      {matrixPanel}

      {/* Confirmation Modal */}
      <ConfirmDialog
        isOpen={Boolean(confirm)}
        onClose={() => setConfirm(null)}
        onConfirm={() => {
          confirm?.run?.();
          setConfirm(null);
        }}
        title={confirm?.title ?? ""}
        message={confirm?.message ?? ""}
        confirmLabel={confirm?.confirmLabel}
        variant={confirm?.variant ?? "danger"}
      />

      {/* Dynamic Toast Feedback */}
      {toast && (
        <Toast
          message={toast.message}
          type={toast.type}
          onClose={() => setToast(null)}
          duration={5000}
          className="fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 max-w-sm shadow-xl animate-bounce-short"
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default AccessControlPage;

import {
  memo,
  useCallback,
  useDeferredValue,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from "react";

import Button from "../../components/Button";
import ErrorMessage from "../../components/ErrorMessage";
import Icon from "../../components/Icon";
import Pagination from "../../components/Pagination";
import SearchBar from "../../components/SearchBar";
import Select from "../../components/Select";
import StatCard from "../../components/StatCard";
import Table from "../../components/Table";
import { DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE, getAuditLogs } from "../../services/auditService";

const ALL_ACTIONS = "ALL";

/** Principal actor details renderer (memoized). */
const PrincipalActorCell = memo(({ username, ipAddress }) => (
  <div className="min-w-0 py-1">
    <span className="block truncate font-medium text-neutral-900">
      {username || "â€”"}
    </span>
    {ipAddress && (
      <span className="block truncate font-mono text-xs text-neutral-500">
        {ipAddress}
      </span>
    )}
  </div>
));
PrincipalActorCell.displayName = "PrincipalActorCell";

/**
 * Super-admin audit trail (/api/audit-logs, Spring `Page` contract).
 *
 * Pagination is server-driven (?page=&size=, 0-based page). The backend
 * exposes no filtering parameters, so the search box and the action
 * dropdown filter the rows of the loaded page client-side â€” the hint
 * under the filter bar says exactly that. Timestamps render as the
 * server sends them (no zone offset on the wire, so none is claimed).
 */
const AuditLogsPage = () => {
  /** Server page: { content, page, size, totalElements, totalPages, â€¦ } */
  const [pageData, setPageData] = useState(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("loading");
  const [reloadKey, setReloadKey] = useState(0);
  /** 1-based UI page number (Spring pages are 0-based on the wire). */
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);

  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const deferredQuery = useDeferredValue(query);
  const [actionFilter, setActionFilter] = useState(ALL_ACTIONS);

  const searchInputRef = useRef(null);

  // Ctrl/Cmd + F focuses the search input.
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (event) => {
      if ((event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey) && event.key === "f") {
        event.preventDefault();
        searchInputRef.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  }, []);

  // Fetch one server page (unmount-safe).
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    setStatus("loading");

    getAuditLogs({ page: page - 1, size: DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE })
      .then((result) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setPageData(result);
        setStatus("ready");
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setStatus("failed");
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [page, reloadKey]);

  const retry = useCallback(() => {
    setStatus("loading");
    setReloadKey((key) => key + 1);
  }, []);

  const handleQueryChange = useCallback((value) => {
    setQuery(value);
  }, []);

  const logs = pageData?.content ?? [];
  const normalizedQuery = deferredQuery.trim().toLowerCase();

  // Client-side filtering over the loaded page only.
  const filteredLogs = useMemo(
    () =>
      logs.filter((log) => {
        const matchesAction =
          actionFilter === ALL_ACTIONS || log.actionType === actionFilter;
        const matchesSearch =
          !normalizedQuery ||
          [
            log.username,
            log.actionType,
            log.entityType,
            log.entityId,
            log.description,
            log.ipAddress,
            log.logId,
          ].some((field) =>
            String(field ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(normalizedQuery),
          );
        return matchesAction && matchesSearch;
      }),
    [logs, actionFilter, normalizedQuery],
  );

  // Action options come from the rows actually loaded on this page.
  const actionOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const actions = [
      ...new Set(logs.map((log) => log.actionType).filter(Boolean)),
    ].sort();
    return [
      { value: ALL_ACTIONS, label: "All Actions" },
      ...actions.map((action) => ({ value: action, label: action })),
    ];
  }, [logs]);

  // Unique principals on the loaded page.
  const uniqueUsers = useMemo(
    () => new Set(logs.map((log) => log.username).filter(Boolean)).size,
    [logs],
  );

  const totalPages = pageData?.totalPages ?? 1;
  const totalElements = pageData?.totalElements ?? 0;
  const isFiltering = Boolean(normalizedQuery) || actionFilter !== ALL_ACTIONS;

  const columns = useMemo(
    () => [
      {
        key: "logId",
        header: "Log ID / Time",
        render: (row) => (
          <div className="min-w-0 whitespace-normal">
            <span className="block font-mono text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-900">
              #{row.logId}
            </span>
            <span className="mt-0.5 block text-xs text-neutral-500">
              {row.timestamp.replace("T", " ")}
            </span>
          </div>
        ),
      },
      {
        key: "username",
        header: "Principal Actor",
        render: (row) => (
          <PrincipalActorCell
            username={row.username}
            ipAddress={row.ipAddress}
          />
        ),
      },
      {
        key: "actionType",
        header: "Action",
        render: (row) => (
          <span className="font-mono text-xs font-semibold text-primary-700">
            {row.actionType || "â€”"}
          </span>
        ),
      },
      {
        key: "entity",
        header: "Entity",
        render: (row) => (
          <div className="min-w-0 whitespace-normal">
            <span className="block text-xs font-medium text-neutral-800">
              {row.entityType || "â€”"}
            </span>
            {row.entityId && (
              <span className="mt-0.5 block font-mono text-xs text-neutral-500">
                #{row.entityId}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        ),
      },
      {
        key: "description",
        header: "Description",
        render: (row) => (
          <span className="block max-w-md whitespace-normal text-xs leading-relaxed text-neutral-600">
            {row.description || "â€”"}
          </span>
        ),
      },
    ],
    [],
  );

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-6 p-4 sm:p-6">
      {/* Summary counters (server totals + current-page facts) */}
      {status === "ready" && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
          <StatCard
            label="Total Trail Events"
            value={`${totalElements} Logged`}
            chip="All pages"
            icon="activity"
            iconClass="bg-primary-50 text-primary-700"
            chipClass="bg-primary-100 text-primary-800"
          />
          <StatCard
            label="This Page"
            value={`${logs.length} Entries`}
            chip={`Page ${page} of ${totalPages}`}
            icon="inbox"
            iconClass="bg-secondary-50 text-secondary-700"
            chipClass="bg-secondary-100 text-secondary-800"
          />
          <StatCard
            label="Principal Actors"
            value={`${uniqueUsers} Users`}
            chip="On this page"
            icon="users"
            iconClass="bg-teal-50 text-teal-700"
            chipClass="bg-teal-100 text-teal-800"
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Filter bar â€” search & action filter cover the loaded page only */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-4 shadow-xs sm:p-5">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <SearchBar
            ref={searchInputRef}
            value={query}
            onChange={handleQueryChange}
            placeholder="Search user, action, entity, description or IP... (âŒ˜F)"
            searchLabel="Search audit logs"
            className="w-full sm:max-w-xl"
          />
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <Select
              id="action-filter"
              name="action-filter"
              label="Action"
              options={actionOptions}
              value={actionFilter}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setActionFilter(event.target.value || ALL_ACTIONS);
              }}
              className="w-full sm:w-48"
            />
            {isFiltering && (
              <Button
                variant="tertiary"
                onClick={() => {
                  setQuery("");
                  setActionFilter(ALL_ACTIONS);
                }}
                className="mt-6 text-xs text-neutral-500 hover:text-neutral-800 sm:mt-0"
              >
                Clear Filters
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
        {isFiltering && (
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-neutral-500">
            Search and filters apply to page {page} only â€” clear them to see
            every row on this page.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Ledger */}
      {status === "loading" ? (
        <div
          role="status"
          aria-label="Loading audit trail"
          className="animate-pulse space-y-3 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xs"
        >
          {Array.from({ length: 8 }).map((_, index) => (
            <div key={index} className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className="h-4 w-1/6 rounded bg-neutral-100" />
              <div className="h-4 w-1/4 rounded bg-neutral-100" />
              <div className="h-4 w-1/4 rounded bg-neutral-100" />
              <div className="h-4 w-1/6 rounded bg-neutral-100" />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : status === "failed" ? (
        <div className="space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-6 text-center shadow-xs">
          <ErrorMessage
            title="Unable to load the audit trail"
            message="The audit-log service did not answer. Check the API connection and retry."
          />
          <Button
            onClick={retry}
            className="mt-2 inline-flex items-center gap-2"
          >
            <Icon name="refresh" className="h-4 w-4" />
            Retry
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <section aria-labelledby="audit-trail-heading" className="space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <h2
              id="audit-trail-heading"
              className="text-base font-semibold text-neutral-900"
            >
              Event Ledger
            </h2>
            <span className="text-sm text-neutral-500">
              {filteredLogs.length === logs.length
                ? `${logs.length} of ${totalElements} events`
                : `${filteredLogs.length} of ${logs.length} on this page Â· ${totalElements} total`}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white shadow-xs">
            <Table
              columns={columns}
              data={filteredLogs}
              getRowKey={(row) => row.logId}
              emptyTitle="No audit entries found"
              emptyMessage={
                isFiltering
                  ? "No entries on this page match the current search or action filter."
                  : "The audit trail has not recorded any events yet."
              }
              label="Audit trail event ledger"
            />
          </div>

          {totalPages > 1 && (
            <Pagination
              className="mt-4"
              currentPage={page}
              totalPages={totalPages}
              onPageChange={setPage}
            />
          )}
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default AuditLogsPage;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\pages\superAdmin\ClinicConfigPage.jsx
