import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Button from "../../components/Button";
import ErrorMessage from "../../components/ErrorMessage";
import Icon from "../../components/Icon";
import Input from "../../components/Input";
import Toast from "../../components/Toast";
import {
  DAY_LABELS,
  WORKING_DAYS,
  isValidTime,
} from "../../constants/schedule";
import {
  extractConflictSlots,
  getDoctorSchedule,
  setDoctorSchedule,
} from "../../services/doctorScheduleService";
import { getUsers } from "../../services/userService";

const createEmptyWeek = () =>
  WORKING_DAYS.map((dayOfWeek) => ({
    dayOfWeek,
    timeSlots: [],
  }));

const normalizeWeek = (days = []) =>
  WORKING_DAYS.map((dayOfWeek) => ({
    dayOfWeek,
    timeSlots: (
      days.find((day) => day.dayOfWeek === dayOfWeek)?.timeSlots ?? []
    ).map((slot) => ({ start: slot.start, end: slot.end })),
  }));

const getSlotCount = (days) =>
  days.reduce((total, day) => total + day.timeSlots.length, 0);

const ClinicConfigPage = () => {
  const [toast, setToast] = useState(null);
  const [doctors, setDoctors] = useState([]);
  const [doctorsStatus, setDoctorsStatus] = useState("loading");
  const [doctorsReload, setDoctorsReload] = useState(0);
  const [schedulesMap, setSchedulesMap] = useState({});

  const [editingDoctorId, setEditingDoctorId] = useState(null);
  const [editDays, setEditDays] = useState(createEmptyWeek);
  const [savingSchedule, setSavingSchedule] = useState(false);
  const [scheduleFormError, setScheduleFormError] = useState("");
  const [scheduleSaveError, setScheduleSaveError] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    const loadDoctors = async () => {
      setDoctorsStatus("loading");

      try {
        const users = await getUsers();
        if (cancelled) return;

        const fetchedDoctors = users.filter((user) => user.role === "DOCTOR");
        setDoctors(fetchedDoctors);
        setDoctorsStatus("ready");

        const initialMap = {};
        fetchedDoctors.forEach((doctor) => {
          initialMap[doctor.userId] = {
            status: "loading",
            days: createEmptyWeek(),
            loadError: null,
          };
        });
        setSchedulesMap(initialMap);

        await Promise.all(
          fetchedDoctors.map(async (doctor) => {
            try {
              const schedule = await getDoctorSchedule(doctor.userId);
              if (cancelled) return;

              setSchedulesMap((previous) => ({
                ...previous,
                [doctor.userId]: {
                  status: "ready",
                  days: normalizeWeek(schedule.days),
                  loadError: null,
                },
              }));
            } catch (error) {
              if (cancelled) return;

              setSchedulesMap((previous) => ({
                ...previous,
                [doctor.userId]: {
                  status: "failed",
                  days: createEmptyWeek(),
                  loadError: error?.userMessage ?? "Could not load schedule.",
                },
              }));
            }
          }),
        );
      } catch {
        if (!cancelled) setDoctorsStatus("failed");
      }
    };

    loadDoctors();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [doctorsReload]);

  const handleStartEdit = (doctor) => {
    const schedule = schedulesMap[doctor.userId];
    setEditingDoctorId(doctor.userId);
    setEditDays(normalizeWeek(schedule?.days));
    setScheduleFormError("");
    setScheduleSaveError(null);
  };

  const handleCancelEdit = () => {
    setEditingDoctorId(null);
    setScheduleFormError("");
    setScheduleSaveError(null);
    setEditDays(createEmptyWeek());
  };

  const updateDaySlots = (dayOfWeek, updater) => {
    setEditDays((current) =>
      current.map((day) =>
        day.dayOfWeek === dayOfWeek
          ? { ...day, timeSlots: updater(day.timeSlots) }
          : day,
      ),
    );
  };

  const changeSlotTime = (dayOfWeek, slotIndex, field, value) => {
    updateDaySlots(dayOfWeek, (slots) =>
      slots.map((slot, index) =>
        index === slotIndex ? { ...slot, [field]: value } : slot,
      ),
    );
  };

  const addTimeSlot = (dayOfWeek) => {
    updateDaySlots(dayOfWeek, (slots) => [
      ...slots,
      { start: "", end: "" },
    ]);
  };

  const removeTimeSlot = (dayOfWeek, slotIndex) => {
    updateDaySlots(dayOfWeek, (slots) =>
      slots.filter((_, index) => index !== slotIndex),
    );
  };

  const handleScheduleSave = async (event, doctorId) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setScheduleFormError("");
    setScheduleSaveError(null);

    if (getSlotCount(editDays) === 0) {
      setScheduleFormError(
        "Add at least one time slot. A schedule must contain at least one working period.",
      );
      return;
    }

    for (const day of editDays) {
      const slots = day.timeSlots;

      if (
        slots.some(
          (slot) => !isValidTime(slot.start) || !isValidTime(slot.end),
        )
      ) {
        setScheduleFormError(
          `${DAY_LABELS[day.dayOfWeek] ?? day.dayOfWeek}: every time slot needs valid HH:mm start and end times.`,
        );
        return;
      }

      if (slots.some((slot) => slot.end <= slot.start)) {
        setScheduleFormError(
          `${DAY_LABELS[day.dayOfWeek] ?? day.dayOfWeek}: each time slot must end after it starts.`,
        );
        return;
      }

      const sortedSlots = [...slots].sort((a, b) =>
        a.start.localeCompare(b.start),
      );
      for (let index = 1; index < sortedSlots.length; index += 1) {
        if (sortedSlots[index].start < sortedSlots[index - 1].end) {
          setScheduleFormError(
            `${DAY_LABELS[day.dayOfWeek] ?? day.dayOfWeek}: time slots cannot overlap.`,
          );
          return;
        }
      }
    }

    setSavingSchedule(true);
    try {
      const updated = await setDoctorSchedule(doctorId, { days: editDays });

      setSchedulesMap((previous) => ({
        ...previous,
        [doctorId]: {
          status: "ready",
          days: normalizeWeek(updated.days),
          loadError: null,
        },
      }));

      const doctor = doctors.find((item) => item.userId === doctorId);
      setToast({
        type: "success",
        message: `Schedule saved for ${doctor?.fullName ?? "the doctor"}.`,
      });
      setEditingDoctorId(null);
    } catch (error) {
      setScheduleSaveError({
        message:
          error?.userMessage ??
          error?.message ??
          "Could not save the schedule. No changes were saved.",
        conflicts: extractConflictSlots(error),
      });
    } finally {
      setSavingSchedule(false);
    }
  };

  const renderWeeklySchedule = (schedule) => {
    const days = normalizeWeek(schedule.days);

    if (getSlotCount(days) === 0) {
      return (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-neutral-300 bg-white p-4">
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-neutral-700">
            No individual schedule configured
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-neutral-500">
            Configure each weekday below. Days without time slots are treated
            as days off.
          </p>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        {days.map(({ dayOfWeek, timeSlots }) => {
          const isWorkingDay = timeSlots.length > 0;

          return (
            <div
              key={dayOfWeek}
              className={`rounded-xl border p-3 ${
                isWorkingDay
                  ? "border-primary-200 bg-primary-50"
                  : "border-neutral-200 bg-white"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-800">
                  {DAY_LABELS[dayOfWeek] ?? dayOfWeek}
                </span>
                <span
                  className={`rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${
                    isWorkingDay
                      ? "bg-primary-100 text-primary-800"
                      : "bg-neutral-100 text-neutral-500"
                  }`}
                >
                  {isWorkingDay ? "Working" : "Day off"}
                </span>
              </div>

              {isWorkingDay ? (
                <div className="mt-3 space-y-2">
                  {timeSlots.map((slot, index) => (
                    <div
                      key={`${slot.start}-${slot.end}-${index}`}
                      className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-primary-100 bg-white px-2.5 py-2 text-xs text-neutral-700"
                    >
                      <Icon
                        name="clock"
                        className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-primary-700"
                      />
                      <span>
                        {slot.start} â€“ {slot.end}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-neutral-500">Not available</p>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-6">
      <section
        aria-labelledby="doctor-schedule-heading"
        className="space-y-6 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xs"
      >
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-neutral-200 pb-4">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <Icon
              name="calendar-clock"
              className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-primary-700"
            />
            <div>
              <h2
                id="doctor-schedule-heading"
                className="text-base font-semibold text-neutral-900"
              >
                Doctor working schedules
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-neutral-600">
                View and edit each doctor's availability independently for
                every weekday.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full border border-warning-200 bg-warning-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-warning-900">
            SCHEDULE_MANAGE
          </span>
        </div>

        {doctorsStatus === "failed" ? (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <ErrorMessage
              title="Unable to load doctors"
              message="The staff directory could not be fetched, so no schedules can be displayed."
            />
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={() => setDoctorsReload((key) => key + 1)}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2"
            >
              <Icon name="refresh" className="h-4 w-4" />
              Retry
            </Button>
          </div>
        ) : doctorsStatus === "loading" ? (
          <div
            role="status"
            aria-label="Loading doctors and schedules"
            aria-busy="true"
            className="motion-safe:animate-pulse space-y-3 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 p-6"
          >
            {Array.from({ length: 3 }, (_, index) => (
              <div key={index} className="h-12 rounded bg-neutral-100" />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : doctors.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600">No doctors provisioned.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-6">
            {doctors.map((doctor) => {
              const schedule = schedulesMap[doctor.userId] ?? {
                status: "loading",
                days: createEmptyWeek(),
              };
              const isEditing = editingDoctorId === doctor.userId;

              return (
                <article
                  key={doctor.userId}
                  className="space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50/50 p-5"
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-neutral-200 pb-3">
                    <div>
                      <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-900">
                        {doctor.fullName}
                        <span className="ml-1 font-normal text-neutral-500">
                          (@{doctor.username})
                        </span>
                      </h3>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-neutral-500">
                        Doctor ID: {doctor.userId}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    {!isEditing && schedule.status === "ready" && (
                      <Button
                        type="button"
                        variant="outline"
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => handleStartEdit(doctor)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5"
                      >
                        <Icon name="edit" className="h-4 w-4" />
                        Edit schedule
                      </Button>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {schedule.status === "loading" ? (
                    <div
                      role="status"
                      aria-label={`Loading schedule for ${doctor.fullName}`}
                      className="motion-safe:animate-pulse grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4"
                    >
                      {Array.from({ length: 4 }, (_, index) => (
                        <div
                          key={index}
                          className="h-24 rounded-xl bg-neutral-100"
                        />
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  ) : schedule.status === "failed" ? (
                    <ErrorMessage
                      title="Unable to load schedule"
                      message={schedule.loadError ?? "Please try again."}
                    />
                  ) : !isEditing ? (
                    renderWeeklySchedule(schedule)
                  ) : (
                    <form
                      onSubmit={(event) =>
                        handleScheduleSave(event, doctor.userId)
                      }
                      className="space-y-5 pt-1"
                    >
                      <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600">
                        Add one or more slots to a working day. Leave a day's
                        slots empty to mark it as a day off.
                      </p>

                      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
                        {editDays.map(({ dayOfWeek, timeSlots }) => (
                          <fieldset
                            key={dayOfWeek}
                            className="min-w-0 space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-3"
                          >
                            <legend className="px-1 text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-800">
                              {DAY_LABELS[dayOfWeek] ?? dayOfWeek}
                            </legend>

                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                              <span className="text-xs text-neutral-500">
                                {timeSlots.length
                                  ? `${timeSlots.length} slot(s)`
                                  : "Day off"}
                              </span>
                              <Button
                                type="button"
                                variant="outline"
                                size="sm"
                                onClick={() => addTimeSlot(dayOfWeek)}
                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                              >
                                <Icon name="plus" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                Add slot
                              </Button>
                            </div>

                            {timeSlots.length === 0 ? (
                              <p className="rounded-lg bg-neutral-50 p-3 text-xs text-neutral-500">
                                No slots configured. This day will be off.
                              </p>
                            ) : (
                              <div className="space-y-3">
                                {timeSlots.map((slot, index) => (
                                  <div
                                    key={`${dayOfWeek}-${index}`}
                                    className="space-y-2 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 p-2"
                                  >
                                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                                      <Input
                                        id={`slot-start-${doctor.userId}-${dayOfWeek}-${index}`}
                                        name={`slot-start-${dayOfWeek}-${index}`}
                                        label={`Slot ${index + 1} start`}
                                        type="time"
                                        value={slot.start}
                                        onChange={(event) =>
                                          changeSlotTime(
                                            dayOfWeek,
                                            index,
                                            "start",
                                            event.target.value,
                                          )
                                        }
                                      />
                                      <Input
                                        id={`slot-end-${doctor.userId}-${dayOfWeek}-${index}`}
                                        name={`slot-end-${dayOfWeek}-${index}`}
                                        label={`Slot ${index + 1} end`}
                                        type="time"
                                        value={slot.end}
                                        onChange={(event) =>
                                          changeSlotTime(
                                            dayOfWeek,
                                            index,
                                            "end",
                                            event.target.value,
                                          )
                                        }
                                      />
                                    </div>
                                    <Button
                                      type="button"
                                      variant="ghost"
                                      size="sm"
                                      onClick={() =>
                                        removeTimeSlot(dayOfWeek, index)
                                      }
                                      aria-label={`Remove ${DAY_LABELS[dayOfWeek] ?? dayOfWeek} slot ${index + 1}`}
                                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                                    >
                                      <Icon
                                        name="circle-x"
                                        className="h-4 w-4 text-danger-600"
                                      />
                                      Remove slot
                                    </Button>
                                  </div>
                                ))}
                              </div>
                            )}
                          </fieldset>
                        ))}
                      </div>

                      {scheduleFormError && (
                        <ErrorMessage
                          title="Check the schedule"
                          message={scheduleFormError}
                        />
                      )}

                      {scheduleSaveError && (
                        <ErrorMessage
                          title={
                            scheduleSaveError.conflicts?.length
                              ? "Time slot conflict"
                              : "Unable to save the schedule"
                          }
                          message={`${scheduleSaveError.message}${
                            scheduleSaveError.conflicts?.length
                              ? ` Overlapping slots: ${scheduleSaveError.conflicts
                                  .map((slot) => `${slot.start}â€“${slot.end}`)
                                  .join(", ")}. No changes were saved.`
                              : ""
                          }`}
                        />
                      )}

                      <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-neutral-200 pt-3">
                        <Button
                          type="button"
                          variant="outline"
                          size="sm"
                          onClick={handleCancelEdit}
                          disabled={savingSchedule}
                        >
                          Cancel
                        </Button>
                        <Button
                          type="submit"
                          size="sm"
                          loading={savingSchedule}
                          disabled={savingSchedule}
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5"
                        >
                          <Icon name="save" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                          Save changes
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    </form>
                  )}
                </article>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {toast && (
        <Toast
          message={toast.message}
          type={toast.type}
          onClose={() => setToast(null)}
          duration={6000}
          className="fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 max-w-sm shadow-lg"
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default ClinicConfigPage;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\pages\superAdmin\SystemMaintenancePage.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Button from "../../components/Button";
import ConfirmDialog from "../../components/ConfirmDialog";
import ErrorMessage from "../../components/ErrorMessage";
import Icon from "../../components/Icon";
import StatCard from "../../components/StatCard";
import StatusBadge from "../../components/StatusBadge";
import Table from "../../components/Table";
import Toast from "../../components/Toast";
import {
  createBackup,
  downloadBackup,
  formatBytes,
  listBackups,
  restoreBackup,
} from "../../services/systemBackupService";

/**
 * Super-admin system maintenance.
 *
 * Backup & restore is wired to the real FR010/UC5 API
 * (/api/system/backups + /api/system/restore, Super Admin only):
 * backups run and list for real, downloads stream the encrypted file,
 * and restores send the mandatory confirm=true. Everything else on
 * this screen â€” the operational task cards, service health and stats â€”
 * remains a session-local fixture: "Executing" a task simulates its
 * run in the browser and is appended to the journal with a SIMULATED
 * status, and the copy on the page says so. Real backup/restore runs
 * are appended to the journal as completed entries.
 */

const formatUtc = (date) =>
  `${date.toISOString().replace("T", " ").slice(0, 16)} UTC`;

const SystemMaintenancePage = () => {
  const [journal, setJournal] = useState();
  const [runningTask, setRunningTask] = useState(null);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState(null);
  /** { title, message, confirmLabel, variant, run } | null */
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState(null);

  // â€” Backup & restore (FR010/UC5) â€” live API â€”â€”â€”â€”â€”â€”â€”â€”â€”â€”â€”â€”â€”â€”â€”â€”
  const [backups, setBackups] = useState([]);
  const [backupsStatus, setBackupsStatus] = useState("loading");
  const [backupsReload, setBackupsReload] = useState(0);
  const [creatingBackup, setCreatingBackup] = useState(false);
  const [downloadingId, setDownloadingId] = useState(null);

  const [restoreFile, setRestoreFile] = useState(null);
  const [restoring, setRestoring] = useState(false);
  /** Per-table restored row counts from the last successful restore. */
  const [restoreResult, setRestoreResult] = useState(null);
  const [restoreError, setRestoreError] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    setBackupsStatus("loading");
    listBackups()
      .then((records) => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setBackups(records);
        setBackupsStatus("ready");
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setBackupsStatus("failed");
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [backupsReload]);


  const handleCreateBackup = async () => {
    setCreatingBackup(true);
    try {
      const record = await createBackup();
      setBackups((current) => [record, ...current]);
      setToast({
        type: "success",
        message: `Backup #${record.backupId} created â€” ${record.filename}.`,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      setToast({
        type: "error",
        message:
          error?.userMessage ?? "Could not create the backup. Please try again.",
      });
    } finally {
      setCreatingBackup(false);
    }
  };


  const performRestore = async ({ backupId, file, label }) => {
    setRestoring(true);
    setRestoreError(null);
    setRestoreResult(null);
    try {
      const counts = await restoreBackup({ backupId, file, confirm: true });
      setRestoreResult(counts);
      setRestoreFile(null);
      setToast({
        type: "success",
        message:
          "Restore completed â€” all exported tables were replaced in a single transaction.",
      });
    } catch (error) {
      setRestoreError(
        error?.userMessage ??
          error?.message ??
          "Could not complete the restore. Nothing was changed.",
      );
    } finally {
      setRestoring(false);
    }
  };


  const requestRestoreFromFile = () => {
    if (!restoreFile) {
      setRestoreError("Choose an encrypted .backup file to restore first.");
      return;
    }
    setConfirm({
      title: "Restore from uploaded file? (destructive)",
      message: `Restore ${restoreFile.name}? Every exported table is replaced inside a single database transaction. The backend verifies the file checksum first and rejects corrupted payloads. A SYSTEM_RESTORE audit entry is written on completion.`,
      confirmLabel: "Confirm restore (overwrites data)",
      variant: "danger",
      run: () =>
        performRestore({ file: restoreFile, label: restoreFile.name }),
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-6">
      {/* Backup & restore â€” FR010/UC5 live API */}
      <section
        aria-labelledby="backup-heading"
        className="rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xs"
      >
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-neutral-200 pb-3">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <h2
              id="backup-heading"
              className="flex items-center gap-2 text-base font-semibold text-neutral-900"
            >
              <Icon name="hard-drive" className="h-4 w-4 text-secondary-700" />
              Backup & Restore
            </h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600">
              Full data exports, timestamped and encrypted AES-256-GCM.
              Backups, downloads and restores below run against the real
              /api/system endpoints â€” Super Admin only.
            </p>
          </div>
          <Button
            onClick={handleCreateBackup}
            loading={creatingBackup}
            disabled={Boolean(confirm)}
            className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2"
          >
            <Icon name="database" className="h-4 w-4" />
            {creatingBackup ? "Creating backup..." : "Run backup now"}
          </Button>
        </div>

        {/* Restore result */}
        {restoreResult ? (
          <div
            role="status"
            className="mt-4 flex items-start gap-2.5 rounded-2xl border border-success-200 bg-success-50 p-3.5 text-sm text-success-900"
          >
            <Icon
              name="circle-check"
              className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-success-700"
            />
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="font-semibold">Restore completed successfully</p>
              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-relaxed opacity-90">
                Restored row counts per table:{" "}
                {Object.entries(restoreResult)
                  .map(([table, count]) => `${table} ${count}`)
                  .join(" Â· ")}
                . All exported tables were replaced inside one transaction and
                a SYSTEM_RESTORE audit entry was written (AC4). Operational
                target NFRR-005: restore completes within 4 hours.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {restoreError ? (
          <div className="mt-4">
            <ErrorMessage title="Restore refused" message={restoreError} />
          </div>
        ) : null}

        {/* Stored backups */}
        <div className="mt-4">
          {backupsStatus === "loading" ? (
            <div
              role="status"
              aria-label="Loading stored backups"
              className="space-y-2 animate-pulse"
            >
              {[0, 1, 2].map((row) => (
                <div key={row} className="h-10 rounded-xl bg-neutral-100" />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : backupsStatus === "failed" ? (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <ErrorMessage
                title="Could not load stored backups"
                message="The backup service did not answer. Check the API connection and retry."
              />
              <div className="text-center">
                <Button
                  size="sm"
                  variant="outline"
                  onClick={() => setBackupsReload((count) => count + 1)}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-2"
                >
                  <Icon name="refresh" className="h-4 w-4" />
                  Retry
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <Table
              data={backups}
              getRowKey={(backup) => backup.backupId}
              emptyTitle="No backups stored."
              emptyMessage="Run a backup now to create the first encrypted snapshot."
              label="Stored backups"
            />
          )}
        </div>

      </section>


      {/* Sensitive-operation confirmation */}
      <ConfirmDialog
        isOpen={Boolean(confirm)}
        onClose={() => setConfirm(null)}
        onConfirm={() => {
          confirm?.run?.();
          setConfirm(null);
        }}
        title={confirm?.title ?? ""}
        message={confirm?.message ?? ""}
        confirmLabel={confirm?.confirmLabel}
        variant={confirm?.variant ?? "primary"}
      />

      {toast && (
        <Toast
          message={toast.message}
          type={toast.type}
          onClose={() => setToast(null)}
          duration={6000}
          className="fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 max-w-sm shadow-lg"
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default SystemMaintenancePage;

import {
  useCallback,
  useDeferredValue,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
  memo,
} from "react";

import Button from "../../components/Button";
import ConfirmDialog from "../../components/ConfirmDialog";
import ErrorMessage from "../../components/ErrorMessage";
import Icon from "../../components/Icon";
import Input from "../../components/Input";
import Modal from "../../components/Modal";
import Pagination from "../../components/Pagination";
import SearchBar from "../../components/SearchBar";
import Select from "../../components/Select";
import StatusBadge from "../../components/StatusBadge";
import Table from "../../components/Table";
import Toast from "../../components/Toast";
import { ROLES } from "../../constants/roles";
import {
  assignUserRole,
  createUser,
  deactivateUser,
  getUsers,
  resetUserPassword,
  unlockUser,
  updateUserEmail,
} from "../../services/userService";

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

const STATUS_ACTIVE = "ACTIVE";
const STATUS_INACTIVE = "INACTIVE";
const STATUS_LOCKED = "LOCKED";
const ALL_FILTER = "ALL";

const roleLabel = (role) => String(role ?? "").replace(/_/g, " ");

const ROLE_BADGE_CLASSES = {
  [ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN]: "border-warning-200 bg-warning-100 text-warning-900",
  [ROLES.ADMIN]: "border-primary-200 bg-primary-100 text-primary-800",
  [ROLES.DOCTOR]: "border-teal-200 bg-teal-100 text-teal-800",
  [ROLES.PHARMACIST]:
    "border-secondary-200 bg-secondary-100 text-secondary-800",
};

const EMPTY_FORM = {
  fullName: "",
  username: "",
  email: "",
  role: ROLES.DOCTOR,
};

const EMAIL_PATTERN = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

const validateEmail = (value) =>
  !EMAIL_PATTERN.test(String(value ?? "").trim())
    ? "Enter a valid email address."
    : "";

/**
 * Sub-component for rendering employee profile cell (Memoized for performance)
 */
const EmployeeCell = memo(({ fullName, username, email }) => {
  const initial = String(fullName ?? "?")
    .charAt(0)
    .toUpperCase();

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 py-1">
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary-100 text-sm font-semibold text-primary-800 border border-primary-200 shadow-xs"
      >
        {initial}
      </span>
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <span className="block font-medium text-neutral-900 truncate">
          {fullName}
        </span>
        <span className="block font-mono text-xs text-neutral-500 truncate">
          {username} {email ? `â€¢ ${email}` : ""}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
});
EmployeeCell.displayName = "EmployeeCell";

/**
 * Sub-component for account status badges (Memoized)
 */
const UserStatusBadge = memo(({ status }) => {
  if (status === STATUS_LOCKED) {
    return (
      <span
        title="Account locked due to consecutive failed login attempts."
        className="inline-flex cursor-help"
      >
        <StatusBadge status="warning">Locked</StatusBadge>
      </span>
    );
  }
  if (status === STATUS_INACTIVE) {
    return <StatusBadge status="danger">Inactive</StatusBadge>;
  }
  if (status === STATUS_ACTIVE) {
    return <StatusBadge status="success">Active</StatusBadge>;
  }
  return <StatusBadge status="neutral">{status || "Unknown"}</StatusBadge>;
});
UserStatusBadge.displayName = "UserStatusBadge";

/**
 * Action buttons per table row (Memoized)
 */
const UserTableRowActions = memo(
  ({ row, onEdit, onUnlock, onDeactivate }) => {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-2">
        <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => onEdit(row)}>
          Edit
        </Button>

        {row.status === STATUS_LOCKED && (
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="warning"
            onClick={() => onUnlock(row)}
          >
            Unlock
          </Button>
        )}

        {row.status !== STATUS_INACTIVE && (
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="danger"
            onClick={() => onDeactivate(row)}
          >
            Deactivate
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }
);
UserTableRowActions.displayName = "UserTableRowActions";

const UserManagementPage = () => {
  const [users, setUsers] = useState([]);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("loading");
  const [reloadKey, setReloadKey] = useState(0);

  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const deferredQuery = useDeferredValue(query);
  const [roleFilter, setRoleFilter] = useState(ALL_FILTER);
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState(ALL_FILTER);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);

  const [isCreateOpen, setIsCreateOpen] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [submitAttempted, setSubmitAttempted] = useState(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [submitError, setSubmitError] = useState("");

  const [resettingUserId, setResettingUserId] = useState(null);

  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState(null);
  const [confirmBusy, setConfirmBusy] = useState(false);

  const [isEditOpen, setIsEditOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editTarget, setEditTarget] = useState(null);
  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState({ email: "", role: ROLES.DOCTOR });
  const [editAttempted, setEditAttempted] = useState(false);
  const [editSubmitting, setEditSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [editError, setEditError] = useState("");

  const [toast, setToast] = useState(null);
  const searchInputRef = useRef(null);

  // Keyboard shortcut Ctrl/Cmd + F for quick search navigation
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e) => {
      if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key === "f") {
        e.preventDefault();
        searchInputRef.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  }, []);

  // Fetch users with query race condition handling
  useEffect(() => {
    let isSubscribed = true;

    getUsers()
      .then((data) => {
        if (!isSubscribed) return;
        setUsers(data || []);
        setStatus("ready");
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (isSubscribed) setStatus("failed");
      });

    return () => {
      isSubscribed = false;
    };
  }, [reloadKey]);

  const retry = useCallback(() => {
    setStatus("loading");
    setReloadKey((k) => k + 1);
  }, []);

  const handleQueryChange = useCallback((value) => {
    setQuery(value);
    setPage(1);
  }, []);

  const openCreate = useCallback(() => {
    setForm(EMPTY_FORM);
    setSubmitAttempted(false);
    setSubmitError("");
    setIsCreateOpen(true);
  }, []);

  const closeCreate = useCallback(() => {
    if (submitting) return;
    setIsCreateOpen(false);
  }, [submitting]);

  const handleFormChange = useCallback((event) => {
    const { name, value } = event.target;
    setForm((current) => ({ ...current, [name]: value }));
  }, []);

  const handleCreateSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSubmitAttempted(true);
    setSubmitError("");

    if (
      !form.fullName.trim() ||
      !form.username.trim() ||
      !form.email.trim() ||
      !EMAIL_PATTERN.test(form.email.trim())
    ) {
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const newUser = await createUser(form);
      setIsCreateOpen(false);
      setForm(EMPTY_FORM);
      setSubmitAttempted(false);

      // Optimistically append created user locally
      if (newUser && newUser.userId) {
        setUsers((prev) => [newUser, ...prev]);
      } else {
        setReloadKey((k) => k + 1);
      }

      setToast({
        type: "success",
        message: `Account for ${form.username.trim()} provisioned successfully. Instructions sent to email.`,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      setSubmitError(
        error?.userMessage ?? "Unable to create user account. Please try again."
      );
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const patchUserLocally = useCallback((userId, patch) => {
    setUsers((current) =>
      current.map((user) =>
        user.userId === userId ? { ...user, ...patch } : user
      )
    );
  }, []);

  const handleConfirm = async () => {
    if (!confirm?.run) return;
    setConfirmBusy(true);
    try {
      await confirm.run();
    } finally {
      setConfirmBusy(false);
      setConfirm(null);
    }
  };

  const requestDeactivate = useCallback(
    (user) => {
      setConfirm({
        title: "Deactivate account?",
        message: `Are you sure you want to deactivate ${user.fullName} (${user.username})? Access will be revoked immediately while maintaining compliance audit logs.`,
        confirmLabel: "Deactivate Account",
        variant: "danger",
        run: async () => {
          try {
            await deactivateUser(user.userId);
            patchUserLocally(user.userId, {
              status: STATUS_INACTIVE,
              lockedUntil: null,
            });
            setToast({
              type: "success",
              message: `${user.fullName} has been deactivated.`,
            });
          } catch (error) {
            setToast({
              type: "error",
              message:
                error?.userMessage ??
                "Unable to deactivate the account. Please try again.",
            });
          }
        },
      });
    },
    [patchUserLocally]
  );

  const requestUnlock = useCallback(
    (user) => {
      setConfirm({
        title: "Clear sign-in lockout?",
        message: `Clear authentication lockout for ${user.fullName}? The account will regain standard access privileges immediately.`,
        confirmLabel: "Unlock Account",
        variant: "primary",
        run: async () => {
          try {
            await unlockUser(user.userId);
            patchUserLocally(user.userId, {
              status: STATUS_ACTIVE,
              lockedUntil: null,
            });
            setToast({
              type: "success",
              message: `Lockout cleared for ${user.fullName}.`,
            });
          } catch (error) {
            setToast({
              type: "error",
              message:
                error?.userMessage ??
                "Unable to clear the lockout. Please try again.",
            });
          }
        },
      });
    },
    [patchUserLocally]
  );

  const openEdit = useCallback((user) => {
    setEditTarget(user);
    setEditForm({ email: user.email, role: user.role });
    setEditAttempted(false);
    setEditError("");
    setIsEditOpen(true);
  }, []);

  const closeEdit = useCallback(() => {
    if (editSubmitting) return;
    setIsEditOpen(false);
  }, [editSubmitting]);

  const handleEditChange = useCallback((event) => {
    const { name, value } = event.target;
    setEditForm((current) => ({ ...current, [name]: value }));
  }, []);

  const handleEditSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setEditAttempted(true);
    setEditError("");

    const email = editForm.email.trim();
    if (!email || !EMAIL_PATTERN.test(email)) return;

    const emailChanged = email !== editTarget.email;
    const roleChanged = editForm.role !== editTarget.role;
    if (!emailChanged && !roleChanged) {
      setIsEditOpen(false);
      return;
    }

    setEditSubmitting(true);
    try {
      if (emailChanged) {
        await updateUserEmail(editTarget.userId, email);
        patchUserLocally(editTarget.userId, { email });
      }
      if (roleChanged) {
        await assignUserRole(editTarget.userId, editForm.role);
        patchUserLocally(editTarget.userId, { role: editForm.role });
      }
      setIsEditOpen(false);
      setToast({
        type: "success",
        message: `Account settings for ${editTarget.username} updated.`,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      setEditError(
        error?.userMessage ?? "Unable to update account details. Please try again."
      );
    } finally {
      setEditSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleResetKey = useCallback(async (user) => {
    setResettingUserId(user.userId);
    try {
      await resetUserPassword(user.userId);
      setToast({
        type: "success",
        message: `Password reset dispatched to ${user.email || user.username}.`,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      setToast({
        type: "error",
        message:
          error?.userMessage ??
          "Unable to initiate password reset sequence.",
      });
    } finally {
      setResettingUserId(null);
    }
  }, []);

  // Filter pipeline optimized using deferred searching
  const filteredUsers = useMemo(() => {
    const normalizedQuery = deferredQuery.trim().toLowerCase();

    return users.filter((user) => {
      const matchesSearch =
        !normalizedQuery ||
        [user.fullName, user.username, user.email].some((field) =>
          String(field ?? "")
            .toLowerCase()
            .includes(normalizedQuery)
        );

      const matchesRole =
        roleFilter === ALL_FILTER || user.role === roleFilter;

      const matchesStatus =
        statusFilter === ALL_FILTER || user.status === statusFilter;

      return matchesSearch && matchesRole && matchesStatus;
    });
  }, [users, deferredQuery, roleFilter, statusFilter]);

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filteredUsers.length / PAGE_SIZE));
  const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages);
  const startIndex = (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;

  const visibleUsers = useMemo(
    () => filteredUsers.slice(startIndex, startIndex + PAGE_SIZE),
    [filteredUsers, startIndex]
  );

  const columns = useMemo(
    () => [
      {
        key: "fullName",
        header: "Employee",
        render: (row) => (
          <EmployeeCell
            fullName={row.fullName}
            username={row.username}
            email={row.email}
          />
        ),
      },
      {
        key: "role",
        header: "Security Role",
        render: (row) => (
          <span
            className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${
              ROLE_BADGE_CLASSES[row.role] ??
              "border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-100 text-neutral-800"
            }`}
          >
            {roleLabel(row.role) || "â€”"}
          </span>
        ),
      },
      {
        key: "status",
        header: "Status",
        render: (row) => <UserStatusBadge status={row.status} />,
      },
      {
        key: "actions",
        header: "Actions",
        headerClass: "text-right",
        render: (row) => (
          <UserTableRowActions
            row={row}
            isResetting={resettingUserId === row.userId}
            onEdit={openEdit}
            onResetKey={handleResetKey}
            onUnlock={requestUnlock}
            onDeactivate={requestDeactivate}
          />
        ),
      },
    ],
    [
      resettingUserId,
      openEdit,
      handleResetKey,
      requestUnlock,
      requestDeactivate,
    ]
  );

  const roleOptions = useMemo(
    () => [
      { value: ALL_FILTER, label: "All Roles" },
      ...Object.values(ROLES).map((role) => ({
        value: role,
        label: roleLabel(role),
      })),
    ],
    []
  );

  const statusOptions = useMemo(
    () => [
      { value: ALL_FILTER, label: "All Statuses" },
      { value: STATUS_ACTIVE, label: "Active" },
      { value: STATUS_LOCKED, label: "Locked" },
      { value: STATUS_INACTIVE, label: "Inactive" },
    ],
    []
  );

  const isFiltered =
    query || roleFilter !== ALL_FILTER || statusFilter !== ALL_FILTER;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-6 p-4 sm:p-6">
      {/* Controls Bar: Search, Role/Status Filters & Actions */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-4 shadow-xs sm:p-5">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
          <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
            <SearchBar
              ref={searchInputRef}
              value={query}
              onChange={handleQueryChange}
              placeholder="Search by name, username, email... (âŒ˜F)"
              className="w-full sm:max-w-xs"
            />

            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <Select
                id="role-filter"
                name="roleFilter"
                options={roleOptions}
                value={roleFilter}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setRoleFilter(e.target.value);
                  setPage(1);
                }}
                className="w-36"
              />

              <Select
                id="status-filter"
                name="statusFilter"
                options={statusOptions}
                value={statusFilter}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setStatusFilter(e.target.value);
                  setPage(1);
                }}
                className="w-36"
              />

              {isFiltered && (
                <Button
                  variant="tertiary"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setQuery("");
                    setRoleFilter(ALL_FILTER);
                    setStatusFilter(ALL_FILTER);
                    setPage(1);
                  }}
                  className="text-xs text-neutral-500 hover:text-neutral-800"
                >
                  Clear Filters
                </Button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <Button
            onClick={openCreate}
            className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-2"
          >
            <Icon name="plus" className="h-4 w-4" />
            Provision New User
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Staff Table Section */}
      {status === "loading" ? (
        <div
          role="status"
          aria-label="Loading staff accounts"
          className="space-y-3 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xs animate-pulse"
        >
          {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, idx) => (
            <div key={idx} className="flex gap-4 items-center">
              <div className="h-9 w-9 rounded-full bg-neutral-100 shrink-0" />
              <div className="h-4 w-1/3 rounded bg-neutral-100" />
              <div className="h-4 w-1/6 rounded bg-neutral-100" />
              <div className="h-4 w-1/6 rounded bg-neutral-100" />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : status === "failed" ? (
        <div className="space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xs text-center">
          <ErrorMessage
            title="Unable to load staff directory"
            message="We encountered an issue connecting to the identity provider. Please check your connection."
          />
          <Button onClick={retry} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 mt-2">
            <Icon name="refresh" className="h-4 w-4" />
            Retry Connection
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <section aria-labelledby="staff-directory-heading" className="space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <h2
              id="staff-directory-heading"
              className="text-base font-semibold text-neutral-900"
            >
              Staff Directory ({filteredUsers.length})
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white shadow-xs">
            <Table
              columns={columns}
              data={visibleUsers}
              getRowKey={(row) => row.userId}
              emptyTitle="No accounts match criteria"
              emptyMessage={
                isFiltered
                  ? "No staff accounts match the specified filter conditions. Try adjusting your search query."
                  : "No staff accounts exist yet. Provision a new account to populate the directory."
              }
              label="Staff Directory Table"
            />
          </div>

          {totalPages > 1 && (
            <Pagination
              className="mt-4"
              currentPage={currentPage}
              totalPages={totalPages}
              onPageChange={setPage}
            />
          )}
        </section>
      )}

      {/* Deactivate/Unlock Confirmation Modal */}
      <ConfirmDialog
        isOpen={Boolean(confirm)}
        onClose={() => {
          if (!confirmBusy) setConfirm(null);
        }}
        onConfirm={handleConfirm}
        title={confirm?.title ?? ""}
        message={confirm?.message ?? ""}
        confirmLabel={confirm?.confirmLabel}
        variant={confirm?.variant ?? "danger"}
        loading={confirmBusy}
      />

      {/* Provision User Modal */}
      <Modal
        isOpen={isCreateOpen}
        onClose={closeCreate}
        title="Provision New User"
      >
        <form onSubmit={handleCreateSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          {submitError && (
            <ErrorMessage
              title="Creation Failed"
              message={submitError}
            />
          )}

          <Input
            id="fullName"
            name="fullName"
            label="Full Legal Name"
            value={form.fullName}
            onChange={handleFormChange}
            required
            submitted={submitAttempted}
            placeholder="e.g. Dr. Arthur Pendelton"
          />

          <Input
            id="username"
            name="username"
            label="Staff ID / Username"
            value={form.username}
            onChange={handleFormChange}
            required
            submitted={submitAttempted}
            placeholder="e.g. PENDELTON-3312"
          />

          <Input
            id="email"
            name="email"
            label="Clinic Email Address"
            type="email"
            value={form.email}
            onChange={handleFormChange}
            required
            validate={validateEmail}
            submitted={submitAttempted}
            placeholder="staff@onecare.clinic"
            hint="An onboarding password activation key will be dispatched to this email address."
          />

          <Select
            id="role"
            name="role"
            label="Role Clearance"
            options={Object.values(ROLES).map((role) => ({
              value: role,
              label: roleLabel(role),
            }))}
            value={form.role}
            onChange={handleFormChange}
            required
            submitted={submitAttempted}
          />

          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-neutral-100 pt-4 mt-6">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={closeCreate}
              disabled={submitting}
            >
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button type="submit" loading={submitting}>
              Provision Account
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </Modal>

      {/* Edit User Modal */}
      <Modal
        isOpen={isEditOpen}
        onClose={closeEdit}
        title={
          editTarget
            ? `Edit Account â€” ${editTarget.username}`
            : "Edit Account"
        }
      >
        <form onSubmit={handleEditSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          {editError && (
            <ErrorMessage
              title="Update Failed"
              message={editError}
            />
          )}

          <Input
            id="edit-email"
            name="email"
            label="Clinic Email Address"
            type="email"
            value={editForm.email}
            onChange={handleEditChange}
            required
            validate={validateEmail}
            submitted={editAttempted}
            placeholder="staff@onecare.clinic"
          />

          <Select
            id="edit-role"
            name="role"
            label="Role Clearance"
            options={Object.values(ROLES).map((role) => ({
              value: role,
              label: roleLabel(role),
            }))}
            value={editForm.role}
            onChange={handleEditChange}
            required
            submitted={editAttempted}
          />

          <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 bg-neutral-50 p-2.5 rounded-lg border border-neutral-100">
            Note: Role revisions take effect immediately on subsequent authentication updates or token refreshes.
          </p>

          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-neutral-100 pt-4 mt-6">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={closeEdit}
              disabled={editSubmitting}
            >
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button type="submit" loading={editSubmitting}>
              Save Changes
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </Modal>

      {/* Global Toast Component */}
      {toast && (
        <Toast
          message={toast.message}
          type={toast.type}
          onClose={() => setToast(null)}
          duration={5000}
          className="fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 max-w-sm shadow-xl"
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default UserManagementPage;

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\routes\RequireAuth.jsx
========================================
import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { useSelector } from "react-redux";

import { selectIsAuthenticated, selectRole } from "../features/auth/authSlice";
import { dashboardPathForRole } from "../constants/roles";

/**
 * CFG-03 â€” role-based route guard (SDS Â§2.4.8, Table 9).
 * A UX layer only; the backend still authorises every request (403),
 * per Coding Standards Â§21.
 *
 * Check order:
 * 1. Unauthenticated           â†’ /login                     (AC3)
 * 2. Role not in allowedRoles[] â†’ the user's own dashboard   (AC2, AC4)
 * 3. Otherwise render the route                             (AC1)
 *
 * Usage (pathless layout route, so allowedRoles is per route/group):
 *   <Route element={<RequireAuth allowedRoles={ROUTE_ACCESS.admin} />}>
 *     <Route path="/admin" element={<AdminLayout />}>...</Route>
 *   </Route>
 */
const RequireAuth = ({ allowedRoles }) => {
  const isAuthenticated = useSelector(selectIsAuthenticated);
  const role = useSelector(selectRole);
  const location = useLocation();

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return (
      <Navigate
        to="/login"
        replace
        state={{ from: location.pathname + location.search }}
      />
    );
  }

  const roleAllowed =
    !Array.isArray(allowedRoles) ||
    allowedRoles.length === 0 ||
    allowedRoles.includes(role);

  if (!roleAllowed) {
    return <Navigate to={dashboardPathForRole(role)} replace />;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
};

export default RequireAuth;


========================================
FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\services\apiClient.js
========================================
import axios from "axios";
import { API_BASE_URL } from "./config";
import { store } from "../app/store";
import {
  clearSession,
  selectAccessToken,
  selectExpiresAt,
  selectRefreshToken,
  setSession,
} from "../features/auth/authSlice";

/**
 * CFG-02 â€” central Axios client (API Standards Â§49â€“50).
 *
 * - Request interceptor: attaches the Bearer token in one place only (Â§38).
 * - Response interceptor: 401 â†’ single shared refresh then transparent retry;
 *   401 from the refresh endpoint (or a second 401) ends the session exactly
 *   once and sends the user to /login (no retry loop).
 * - 403 / 429 / 500: the standard {success, message, data} envelope is surfaced
 *   on the rejected error; the user is never logged out for these (Â§14â€“15, Â§44).
 *
 * Auth state lives in the Redux store (src/features/auth/authSlice.js), read
 * through selectors and written through dispatch â€” never in component-local
 * copies.
 * Never log tokens, passwords or request bodies.
 */

export const REFRESH_URL = "/auth/refresh";
export const LOGIN_PATH = "/login";
export const SESSION_EXPIRED_EVENT = "onecare:session-expired";
export const SESSION_EXPIRED_REASON = "session-expired";
export const DEFAULT_EXPIRES_IN_SECONDS = 1800;
export const REFRESH_LEEWAY_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;

const NETWORK_MESSAGE = "Unable to reach the server. Please try again.";
const GENERIC_MESSAGE = "Something went wrong. Please try again.";

const apiClient = axios.create({
  baseURL: API_BASE_URL,
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
});

const defaultNavigate = (to) => {
  if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.location) {
    window.location.assign(to);
  }
};

let navigate = defaultNavigate;
let sessionExpired = false;
let refreshPromise = null;
const stopCallbacks = new Set();

/** Test/app seam: swap the redirect implementation (router navigate, spy, ...). */
export const setNavigator = (fn) => {
  navigate = typeof fn === "function" ? fn : defaultNavigate;
};

/**
 * Clear auth state and send the user to /login once.
 * Idempotent: concurrent failures share a single redirect.
 */
export const handleSessionExpired = () => {
  if (sessionExpired) return;
  sessionExpired = true;
  store.dispatch(clearSession());
  if (
    typeof window !== "undefined" &&
    typeof window.dispatchEvent === "function"
  ) {
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event(SESSION_EXPIRED_EVENT));
  }
  navigate(`${LOGIN_PATH}?reason=${SESSION_EXPIRED_REASON}`);
};

/** Call after a successful login so the next expiry can redirect again. */
export const resetAuthState = () => {
  sessionExpired = false;
};

/** Normalise an Axios error onto the standard envelope, without assuming more. */
export const normalizeError = (error) => {
  const response = error.response;
  const body = response?.data;
  const envelope =
    body && typeof body === "object" && !Array.isArray(body) ? body : null;

  error.status = response?.status ?? null;
  if (envelope && "success" in envelope) error.success = envelope.success;
  if (envelope && "data" in envelope) error.data = envelope.data;
  if (typeof envelope?.message === "string") error.message = envelope.message;

  if (typeof error.userMessage !== "string") {
    if (typeof envelope?.message === "string") {
      error.userMessage = envelope.message;
    } else if (!response) {
      error.userMessage = NETWORK_MESSAGE;
    } else {
      error.userMessage = GENERIC_MESSAGE;
    }
  }
  return error;
};

/**
 * Heuristic for `expiresIn`: anything above this is milliseconds, not seconds
 * (~11.5 days expressed in seconds). Backends disagree on the unit, and a
 * millisecond value read as seconds puts expiry decades out â€” which is exactly
 * what silently stops the proactive scheduler from ever firing.
 */
const EXPIRES_UNIT_THRESHOLD = 1_000_000;

/** Normalise a relative expiry to a positive number of seconds, or null. */
export const normalizeExpiresInSeconds = (value) => {
  if (value == null) return null;
  const numeric = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(numeric) || numeric <= 0) return null;
  if (numeric > EXPIRES_UNIT_THRESHOLD) {
    return Math.max(1, Math.round(numeric / 1000));
  }
  return Math.round(numeric);
};

/** Convert an absolute expiry (epoch or ISO string) into seconds from now. */
const absoluteExpiryToSeconds = (value) => {
  const numeric = Number(value);
  let at;

  if (Number.isFinite(numeric) && String(value).trim() !== "") {
    at = numeric > EXPIRES_UNIT_THRESHOLD ? numeric : numeric * 1000;
  } else {
    at = Date.parse(String(value));
  }

  if (!Number.isFinite(at)) return null;
  const seconds = (at - Date.now()) / 1000;
  return seconds > 0 ? Math.ceil(seconds) : null;
};

export const extractTokens = (payload) => {
  const data = payload?.data ?? payload ?? {};

  // Backends spell these differently and disagree on the unit. Accept the
  // camelCase and snake_case forms plus an absolute expiry, and normalise to
  // seconds before it reaches the store.
  const rawExpiresIn =
    data.expiresIn ?? data.expires_in ?? data.expiresInSeconds ?? null;
  const rawExpiresAt = data.expiresAt ?? data.expires_at ?? null;

  const expiresIn =
    normalizeExpiresInSeconds(rawExpiresIn) ??
    (rawExpiresAt != null ? absoluteExpiryToSeconds(rawExpiresAt) : null);

  const session = {
    accessToken: data.accessToken ?? data.access_token ?? null,
    refreshToken: data.refreshToken ?? data.refresh_token ?? null,
    expiresIn: expiresIn ?? DEFAULT_EXPIRES_IN_SECONDS,
    role: data.role ?? null,
  };
  if (data.username !== undefined && data.username !== null) {
    session.user = data.username;
  }
  return session;
};

const withAuthHeader = (headers, token) => {
  const value = `Bearer ${token}`;
  if (!headers) return { Authorization: value };
  if (typeof headers.set === "function") {
    headers.set("Authorization", value);
    return headers;
  }
  headers.Authorization = value;
  return headers;
};

const cloneHeaders = (headers) => {
  if (!headers) return {};
  if (typeof headers.toJSON === "function") return { ...headers.toJSON() };
  return { ...headers };
};

const isRefreshRequest = (config) => {
  if (!config) return false;
  if (config._isRefresh) return true;
  const url = config.url ?? "";
  if (url === REFRESH_URL || url.endsWith(REFRESH_URL)) return true;
  return `${config.baseURL ?? ""}${url}`.includes("/auth/refresh");
};

const performRefresh = async () => {
  const refreshToken = selectRefreshToken(store.getState());
  if (!refreshToken) {
    throw new Error("No refresh token available");
  }
  const response = await apiClient.post(
    REFRESH_URL,
    { refreshToken },
    { skipAuth: true, _isRefresh: true },
  );
  const tokens = extractTokens(response.data);
  if (!tokens.accessToken) {
    throw new Error("Refresh response did not include an access token");
  }
  store.dispatch(setSession(tokens));
  sessionExpired = false;
  return tokens.accessToken;
};

/**
 * Single-flight refresh: concurrent 401s share one refresh call.
 * A failure rejects every waiter; no caller retries.
 */
export const refreshSession = () => {
  if (refreshPromise) return refreshPromise;
  refreshPromise = performRefresh().finally(() => {
    refreshPromise = null;
  });
  return refreshPromise;
};

apiClient.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
  if (config.skipAuth) return config;
  const token = selectAccessToken(store.getState());
  if (token) config.headers = withAuthHeader(config.headers, token);
  return config;
});

apiClient.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  async (error) => {
    const config = error.config;
    const status = error.response?.status;

    if (!config) return Promise.reject(normalizeError(error));

    if (status === 401) {
      if (isRefreshRequest(config) || config._retry) {
        handleSessionExpired();
        return Promise.reject(normalizeError(error));
      }
      if (config.skipAuth) {
        // Public endpoint (e.g. wrong credentials on login): surface the
        // envelope, do not refresh and do not end the session.
        return Promise.reject(normalizeError(error));
      }
      config._retry = true;
      try {
        await refreshSession();
        // Fresh headers so the request interceptor attaches the new token
        // without mutating the failed attempt's config.
        return await apiClient({
          ...config,
          headers: cloneHeaders(config.headers),
        });
      } catch {
        handleSessionExpired();
        return Promise.reject(normalizeError(error));
      }
    }

    return Promise.reject(normalizeError(error));
  },
);

/**
 * Proactive refresh (SDS Â§2.5.1): fires ~5 minutes before expiry (expiresIn
 * 1800 s) and only while the session may be extended.
 *
 * `canExtendSession` decides that gate and defaults to `isUserActive`, which
 * is inactive-safe: with no callback supplied nothing is ever refreshed.
 * CFG-05 passes a visibility predicate instead, so an idle-but-visible user is
 * kept signed in while a background tab is not.
 *
 * A failed refresh is reported through `onRefreshError` (and logged when
 * `debug` is on) and then retried on the next tick while the token is still
 * inside the leeway window â€” it never ends the session by itself; the next
 * real call receives a 401 and runs the full refresh / session-expired flow.
 *
 * Returns a stop function.
 */
export const startTokenRefreshScheduler = ({
  isUserActive = () => false,
  canExtendSession = null,
  checkIntervalMs = 60 * 1000,
  now = () => Date.now(),
  leewayMs = REFRESH_LEEWAY_MS,
  debug = false,
  onRefreshError = null,
} = {}) => {
  const extendSession = canExtendSession ?? isUserActive;

  const log = (...args) => {
    if (debug && typeof console !== "undefined") {
      console.warn("[onecare] token-refresh:", ...args);
    }
  };

  const tick = () => {
    if (refreshPromise) return;

    const expiresAt = selectExpiresAt(store.getState());
    if (!expiresAt) {
      // Nothing to schedule against â€” surface it instead of letting the
      // session die quietly at expiry.
      log("no expiresAt in the store; the token will never be refreshed.");
      return;
    }

    const remainingMs = expiresAt - now();
    if (remainingMs > leewayMs) return;

    if (!extendSession()) return;

    log(`refreshing with ${Math.round(remainingMs / 1000)}s left`);

    refreshSession().catch((error) => {
      log(
        "proactive refresh failed; retrying on the next tick",
        error?.message,
      );
      onRefreshError?.(error);
    });
  };

  const intervalId = setInterval(tick, checkIntervalMs);
  const stop = () => {
    clearInterval(intervalId);
    stopCallbacks.delete(stop);
  };
  stopCallbacks.add(stop);
  return stop;
};

/** Test seam: reset module state (flag, in-flight refresh, schedulers, navigator). */
export const __resetAuthState = () => {
  sessionExpired = false;
  refreshPromise = null;
  stopCallbacks.forEach((stop) => stop());
  stopCallbacks.clear();
  navigate = defaultNavigate;
};

export default apiClient;

import apiClient from "./apiClient";

export const adaptAppointmentsResponse = (payload) => {
  if (payload?.success !== true || !Array.isArray(payload.data)) {
    throw new Error("Unexpected appointments response format");
  }

  return payload.data.filter(
    (record) =>
      record &&
      (typeof record.appointmentId === "string" ||
        typeof record.appointmentId === "number"),
  );
};

export const getAppointments = async ({ date, signal } = {}) => {
  const response = await apiClient.get("/appointments", {
    params: date ? { date } : undefined,
    signal,
  });
  return adaptAppointmentsResponse(response.data);
};

/** Fetch one appointment, including its clinical details. */
export const getAppointmentById = async (appointmentId, { signal } = {}) => {
  const response = await apiClient.get(`/appointments/${appointmentId}`, {
    signal,
  });
  const payload = response.data;
  if (
    payload?.success !== true ||
    !payload.data ||
    String(payload.data.appointmentId) !== String(appointmentId)
  ) {
    throw new Error("Unexpected appointment response format");
  }
  return payload.data;
};

export const findAppointment = getAppointmentById;

/*
 * Clinical-notes endpoints below come from the backend branch
 * `fix/44/align-clinical-notes` (clinical notes moved from the prescription
 * onto the appointment entity). That branch is NOT merged into develop yet â€”
 * until it lands, `saveClinicalNotes` is a silent no-op against a develop
 * backend (the unknown `clinicalNotes` key is ignored) and the two read
 * endpoints answer 404. The UI treats read failures as "no notes available".
 */

/** `AppointmentClinicalNotesResponse` for GET /appointments/{id}/clinical-notes. */
export const adaptClinicalNotesResponse = (payload) => {
  const data = payload?.data;
  if (
    payload?.success !== true ||
    !data ||
    (typeof data.appointmentId !== "string" &&
      typeof data.appointmentId !== "number")
  ) {
    throw new Error("Unexpected clinical notes response format");
  }
  return {
    appointmentId: data.appointmentId,
    patientId: data.patientId ?? null,
    doctorId: data.doctorId ?? null,
    clinicalNotes:
      typeof data.clinicalNotes === "string" ? data.clinicalNotes : "",
  };
};

export const getClinicalNotes = async (appointmentId, { signal } = {}) => {
  const response = await apiClient.get(
    `/appointments/${appointmentId}/clinical-notes`,
    { signal },
  );
  return adaptClinicalNotesResponse(response.data);
};

/**
 * Save the appointment's clinical notes (doctor-only, max 2000 chars;
 * the backend rejects saves on CANCELLED appointments with HTTP 400).
 * `PUT /api/appointments/{id}` treats every field as optional â€” sending
 * only `clinicalNotes` leaves date/reason/doctor untouched.
 */
export const saveClinicalNotes = async (
  appointmentId,
  clinicalNotes,
  { signal } = {},
) => {
  const response = await apiClient.put(
    `/appointments/${appointmentId}`,
    { clinicalNotes },
    { signal },
  );
  return response.data;
};

/** `PatientHistoryResponse`: previous appointments (with notes) + prescriptions. */
export const adaptPatientHistoryResponse = (payload) => {
  const data = payload?.data;
  if (
    payload?.success !== true ||
    !data ||
    (!data.patient && data.patientId === undefined)
  ) {
    throw new Error("Unexpected patient history response format");
  }
  return {
    patient: data.patient ?? null,
    patientId: data.patient?.patientId ?? data.patientId,
    appointments: Array.isArray(data.appointments) ? data.appointments : [],
    prescriptions: Array.isArray(data.prescriptions) ? data.prescriptions : [],
  };
};

  const response = await apiClient.get(`/patients/${patientId}/history`, {
    signal,
  });

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\src\services\auditService.js
========================================
import apiClient from "./apiClient";

/**
 * Super-admin audit trail (immutable security log).
 *
 * GET /audit-logs â€” Spring Data `Page` envelope:
 *   { success, message, data: { content: AuditLog[], totalElements,
 *     totalPages, number, size, first, last, pageable, sort, ... } }
 *
 * Wire shape (data.content[]):
 *   { logId, userId, username, actionType, entityType, entityId,
 *     description, ipAddress, timestamp }
 *   // timestamp: "2026-10-20T10:54:46" (no zone offset on the wire)
 *
 * Pagination follows Spring's convention â€” `?page=` (0-based) and
 * `?size=` â€” confirmed by the pageable/size/number fields the server
 * echoes back. No server-side filtering parameters are documented, so
 * the page filters the loaded rows client-side and labels it.
 */
export const DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 20;

const asTrimmedString = (value) =>
  typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";

const asNullableString = (value) =>
  value === null || value === undefined || value === "" ? null : String(value);

/** Normalise one wire record into the shape the UI renders. */
export const adaptAuditLog = (raw) => ({
  logId: Number(raw.logId),
  userId:
    raw.userId === null || raw.userId === undefined ? null : Number(raw.userId),
  username: asTrimmedString(raw.username),
  actionType: asTrimmedString(raw.actionType).toUpperCase(),
  entityType: asTrimmedString(raw.entityType).toUpperCase(),
  entityId: asNullableString(raw.entityId),
  description: asTrimmedString(raw.description),
  ipAddress: asTrimmedString(raw.ipAddress),
  timestamp: asTrimmedString(raw.timestamp),
});

/** A row is usable when it carries its server-assigned log id. */
const hasIdentity = (raw) =>
  Boolean(raw) && raw.logId !== null && raw.logId !== undefined;

/** Page response â†’ { content, page, size, totalElements, totalPages, â€¦ }. */
export const adaptAuditLogsPageResponse = (payload) => {
  const data = payload?.data;
  if (payload?.success !== true || !data || !Array.isArray(data.content)) {
    throw new Error("Unexpected audit logs response format");
  }
  const content = data.content.filter(hasIdentity).map(adaptAuditLog);
  const rawPage = Number(data.number);
  const rawSize = Number(data.size);
  const rawTotalElements = Number(data.totalElements);
  const rawTotalPages = Number(data.totalPages);
  const page = Number.isFinite(rawPage) && rawPage >= 0 ? rawPage : 0;
  const size =
    Number.isFinite(rawSize) && rawSize > 0
      ? rawSize
      : content.length || DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE;
  const totalElements =
    Number.isFinite(rawTotalElements) && rawTotalElements >= 0
      ? rawTotalElements
      : content.length;
  const totalPages =
    Number.isFinite(rawTotalPages) && rawTotalPages > 0 ? rawTotalPages : 1;
  return {
    content,
    page,
    size,
    totalElements,
    totalPages,
    first: typeof data.first === "boolean" ? data.first : page === 0,
    last:
      typeof data.last === "boolean" ? data.last : page >= totalPages - 1,
  };
};

/**
 * Fetch one page of the trail. `page` is 0-based (Spring convention);
 * the backend applies its own default sort (newest first in practice).
 */
export const getAuditLogs = async ({
  page = 0,
  size = DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE,
  signal,
} = {}) => {
  // API_BASE_URL already includes the `/api` prefix.
  const response = await apiClient.get("/audit-logs", {
    params: { page, size },
    signal,
  });
  return adaptAuditLogsPageResponse(response.data);
};

export default {
  DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE,
  getAuditLogs,
  adaptAuditLog,
  adaptAuditLogsPageResponse,
};

import apiClient, {
  extractTokens,
  refreshSession,
  resetAuthState,
} from "./apiClient";
import { store } from "../app/store";
import {
  clearSession,
  selectIsAuthenticated,
  setSession,
} from "../features/auth/authSlice";

export const login = async (credentials) => {
  const response = await apiClient.post("/auth/login", credentials, {
    skipAuth: true,
  });
  const tokens = extractTokens(response.data);
  if (!tokens.accessToken) {
    throw new Error("Login response did not include an access token");
  }
  store.dispatch(setSession(tokens));
  resetAuthState();
  return response.data;
};

export const refresh = () => refreshSession();

export const logout = async () => {
  try {
    await apiClient.post("/auth/logout");
  } catch {
    /* best effort â€” local state is cleared regardless */
  } finally {
    store.dispatch(clearSession());
    resetAuthState();
  }
};

export const getCurrentUser = () => apiClient.get("/auth/me");

export const forgotPassword = async (email) => {
  const response = await apiClient.post(
    "/auth/forgot-password",
    { email },
    { skipAuth: true },
  );
  return response.data;
};

export const resetPassword = async (token, newPassword) => {
  const response = await apiClient.post(
    "/auth/reset-password",
    { token, newPassword },
    { skipAuth: true },
  );
  return response.data;
};

export const isAuthenticated = () => selectIsAuthenticated(store.getState());

const authService = {
  login,
  refresh,
  logout,
  getCurrentUser,
  forgotPassword,
  resetPassword,
  isAuthenticated,
};

export default authService;
