FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\constants\permissions.test.js
========================================
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { ROLES } from "../../src/constants/roles";
import {
  PERMISSION_CATEGORIES,
  PERMISSION_CODES,
  PERMISSION_MATRIX,
  hasPermission,
} from "../../src/constants/permissions";

/**
 * The matrix is a static mirror of the backend policy classes
 * `com.onecare.backend.security.Permission` / `RolePermission`. These
 * tests pin the mirror: counts, naming and the exact per-role grants, so
 * a drift against the Java source fails here first.
 */
describe("permission matrix â€” CFG-03 backend mirror", () => {
  it("mirrors every Permission constant exactly once", () => {
    expect(PERMISSION_MATRIX).toHaveLength(39);
    expect(new Set(PERMISSION_CODES).size).toBe(PERMISSION_CODES.length);
    expect(PERMISSION_CODES).toHaveLength(39);

    for (const entry of PERMISSION_MATRIX) {
      // Java constant naming: UPPER_SNAKE_CASE.
      expect(entry.code).toMatch(/^[A-Z]+(_[A-Z]+)+$/);
      expect(PERMISSION_CATEGORIES).toContain(entry.category);
      expect(entry.name.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(entry.description.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });

  it("groups scopes into the four display domains in order", () => {
    expect(PERMISSION_CATEGORIES).toEqual([
      "Clinical EMR",
      "Pharmacy & Dispensary",
      "Financial & Billing",
      "Security & Infrastructure",
    ]);

    const used = PERMISSION_MATRIX.map((entry) => entry.category);
    expect(used).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(PERMISSION_CATEGORIES));
  });

  it("grants only known roles, without duplicates", () => {
    const knownRoles = Object.values(ROLES);

    for (const entry of PERMISSION_MATRIX) {
      expect(new Set(entry.granted).size).toBe(entry.granted.length);
      for (const role of entry.granted) {
        expect(knownRoles).toContain(role);
      }
    }
  });

  it("reproduces the RolePermission grants for every role", () => {
    const grantsFor = (role) =>
      PERMISSION_MATRIX.filter((entry) => entry.granted.includes(role))
        .map((entry) => entry.code)
        .sort();

    expect(grantsFor(ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN)).toHaveLength(30);
    expect(grantsFor(ROLES.ADMIN)).toHaveLength(23);
    expect(grantsFor(ROLES.DOCTOR)).toHaveLength(14);
    expect(grantsFor(ROLES.PHARMACIST)).toHaveLength(14);

    // 39 scopes Ã— 4 roles = 156 cells, 81 of them granted by default.
    const grantedCells = PERMISSION_MATRIX.reduce(
      (sum, entry) => sum + entry.granted.length,
      0,
    );
    expect(PERMISSION_MATRIX.length * Object.keys(ROLES).length).toBe(156);
    expect(grantedCells).toBe(81);
  });

  it("keeps the sharp edges of the Java policy intact", () => {
    const rolesWith = (code) => {
      const entry = PERMISSION_MATRIX.find((item) => item.code === code);
      expect(entry).toBeDefined();
      return [...entry.granted].sort();
    };

    // Destructive / identity-clearance scopes stay SUPER_ADMIN-only.
    expect(rolesWith("USER_DELETE")).toEqual([ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN]);
    expect(rolesWith("USER_ROLE_ASSIGN")).toEqual([ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN]);
    expect(rolesWith("PATIENT_DELETE")).toEqual([ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN]);

    // Clinical notes are invisible to pure admin roles (Java: only
    // DOCTOR holds WRITE, DOCTOR + PHARMACIST hold READ).
    expect(rolesWith("APPOINTMENT_WRITE_CLINICAL_NOTES")).toEqual([
      ROLES.DOCTOR,
    ]);
    expect(rolesWith("APPOINTMENT_READ_CLINICAL_NOTES")).toEqual([
      ROLES.DOCTOR,
      ROLES.PHARMACIST,
    ]);

    // Prescription authority is split by workflow stage.
    expect(rolesWith("PRESCRIPTION_CREATE")).toEqual([ROLES.DOCTOR]);
    expect(rolesWith("PRESCRIPTION_DISPENSE")).toEqual([ROLES.PHARMACIST]);
    expect(rolesWith("PRESCRIPTION_READ_ALL")).toEqual(
      [...Object.values(ROLES)].sort(),
    );

    // ADMIN creates but cannot delete staff accounts.
    expect(rolesWith("USER_CREATE")).toEqual(
      [ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN, ROLES.ADMIN].sort(),
    );

    // Governance scopes shipped with the role permissions API (FR003):
    // only SUPER_ADMIN may read or rewrite role permission sets, and
    // SYSTEM_BACKUP is privileged (backend rule BR004).
    expect(rolesWith("SYSTEM_BACKUP")).toEqual([ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN]);
    expect(rolesWith("ROLE_PERMISSION_READ")).toEqual([ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN]);
    expect(rolesWith("ROLE_PERMISSION_UPDATE")).toEqual([ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN]);
    // Clinic configuration: only SUPER_ADMIN writes doctor schedules
    // (FR009/UC4 requires SCHEDULE_MANAGE).
    expect(rolesWith("SCHEDULE_MANAGE")).toEqual([ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN]);
    expect(
      PERMISSION_MATRIX.find((entry) => entry.code === "SYSTEM_BACKUP")
        .privileged,
    ).toBe(true);
  });

  it("surfaces scopes defined but granted to no role yet", () => {
    const withNoGrants = PERMISSION_MATRIX.filter(
      (entry) => entry.granted.length === 0,
    ).map((entry) => entry.code);

    expect(withNoGrants.sort()).toEqual([
      "AUDIT_READ",
      "REPORT_GENERATE",
      "USER_READ_OWN",
    ]);
  });

  it("hasPermission reads a granted list", () => {
    expect(hasPermission([ROLES.DOCTOR], ROLES.DOCTOR)).toBe(true);
    expect(hasPermission([ROLES.ADMIN], ROLES.DOCTOR)).toBe(false);
    expect(hasPermission(null, ROLES.DOCTOR)).toBe(false);
    expect(hasPermission("not-a-list", ROLES.DOCTOR)).toBe(false);
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\features\auth\authSlice.test.js
========================================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from "vitest";
import { store } from "../../../src/app/store";
import authReducer, {
  clearSession,
  selectAccessToken,
  selectExpiresAt,
  selectIsAuthenticated,
  selectRefreshToken,
  selectRole,
  setSession,
} from "../../../src/features/auth/authSlice";

const state = () => store.getState();

const seed = (payload) => store.dispatch(setSession(payload));

beforeEach(() => {
  store.dispatch(clearSession());
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe("authSlice (Redux, in-memory session)", () => {
  it("stores tokens with an expiry timestamp", () => {
    const before = Date.now();
    seed({ accessToken: "a", refreshToken: "r", expiresIn: 1800 });
    const after = Date.now();

    expect(selectAccessToken(state())).toBe("a");
    expect(selectRefreshToken(state())).toBe("r");
    const expiresAt = selectExpiresAt(state());
    expect(expiresAt).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(before + 1800 * 1000);
    expect(expiresAt).toBeLessThanOrEqual(after + 1800 * 1000);
    expect(selectIsAuthenticated(state())).toBe(true);
  });

  it("keeps a previous refresh token when none is supplied", () => {
    seed({ accessToken: "a1", refreshToken: "r1", expiresIn: 60 });
    seed({ accessToken: "a2" });

    expect(selectAccessToken(state())).toBe("a2");
    expect(selectRefreshToken(state())).toBe("r1");
  });

  it("ignores an empty access token", () => {
    seed({ accessToken: "", refreshToken: "r", expiresIn: 60 });

    expect(selectAccessToken(state())).toBeNull();
    expect(selectRefreshToken(state())).toBeNull();
    expect(selectIsAuthenticated(state())).toBe(false);
  });

  it("clears the whole session", () => {
    seed({
      accessToken: "a",
      refreshToken: "r",
      expiresIn: 60,
      role: "DOCTOR",
    });
    store.dispatch(clearSession());

    expect(selectAccessToken(state())).toBeNull();
    expect(selectRefreshToken(state())).toBeNull();
    expect(selectExpiresAt(state())).toBeNull();
    expect(selectRole(state())).toBeNull();
    expect(selectIsAuthenticated(state())).toBe(false);
  });

  it("stores role and redirectUrl from the login payload", () => {
    seed({
      accessToken: "a",
      refreshToken: "r",
      expiresIn: 1800,
      role: "SUPER_ADMIN",
      redirectUrl: "/super-admin/dashboard",
    });

    expect(selectRole(state())).toBe("SUPER_ADMIN");
    expect(state().auth.redirectUrl).toBe("/super-admin/dashboard");
  });

  it("treats a non-positive or invalid expiresIn as no expiry", () => {
    seed({ accessToken: "a", refreshToken: "r", expiresIn: 0 });
    expect(selectExpiresAt(state())).toBeNull();

    seed({ accessToken: "a", refreshToken: "r", expiresIn: "soon" });
    expect(selectExpiresAt(state())).toBeNull();
  });

  it("reduces through the auth slice key", () => {
    expect(state()).toHaveProperty("auth");
    expect(authReducer(undefined, { type: "@@INIT" })).toMatchObject({
      accessToken: null,
      refreshToken: null,
      expiresAt: null,
    });
  });

  it("updates the state correctly for persistence", () => {
    seed({
      accessToken: "a",
      refreshToken: "r",
      expiresIn: 1800,
      role: "ADMIN",
    });

    expect(selectAccessToken(state())).toBe("a");
    expect(selectRole(state())).toBe("ADMIN");
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\features\auth\sessionManager.test.jsx
========================================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from "vitest";

import {
  render,
  screen,
  fireEvent,
  act,
  cleanup,
} from "@testing-library/react";

import { Provider } from "react-redux";
import { MemoryRouter, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";

import { store } from "../../../src/app/store";
import { setSession, clearSession } from "../../../src/features/auth/authSlice";

vi.mock("../../../src/services/authService", () => ({
  logout: vi.fn(),
}));

vi.mock("../../../src/services/apiClient", () => ({
  startTokenRefreshScheduler: vi.fn(() => vi.fn()),
  SESSION_EXPIRED_EVENT: "session-expired",
}));

import { logout } from "../../../src/services/authService";
import { startTokenRefreshScheduler } from "../../../src/services/apiClient";
import { isTabVisible } from "../../../src/features/auth/sessionActivity";
import SessionManager from "../../../src/features/auth/SessionManager.jsx";

const LocationDisplay = () => {
  const location = useLocation();

  return (
    <div data-testid="location">
      {location.pathname}
      {location.search}
    </div>
  );
};

const renderSessionManager = () =>
  render(
    <Provider store={store}>
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={["/dashboard"]}>
        <SessionManager />
        <LocationDisplay />
      </MemoryRouter>
    </Provider>,
  );

const advanceTime = async (milliseconds) => {
  await act(async () => {
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(milliseconds);
  });
};

describe("SessionManager â€” CFG-05", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    store.dispatch(clearSession());

    store.dispatch(
      setSession({
        accessToken: "test-access-token",
        refreshToken: "test-refresh-token",
        expiresIn: 3600,
        role: "DOCTOR",
      }),
    );

    logout.mockReset();
    logout.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    cleanup();
    store.dispatch(clearSession());
    vi.clearAllTimers();
    vi.useRealTimers();
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  it("AC1: logs out after 30 minutes and redirects to login", async () => {
    renderSessionManager();

    await advanceTime(30 * 60 * 1000);

    expect(logout).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(store.getState().auth.accessToken).toBeNull();

    expect(screen.getByTestId("location").textContent).toBe(
      "/login?reason=session-expired",
    );
  });

  it("AC2: clears the session even when logout API fails", async () => {
    logout.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error("Logout API failed"));

    renderSessionManager();

    await advanceTime(30 * 60 * 1000);

    expect(store.getState().auth.accessToken).toBeNull();
    expect(store.getState().auth.refreshToken).toBeNull();

    expect(screen.getByTestId("location").textContent).toBe(
      "/login?reason=session-expired",
    );
  });

  it("AC3: shows a warning after 28 minutes and resets after activity", async () => {
    renderSessionManager();

    await advanceTime(28 * 60 * 1000);

    expect(
      screen.getByRole("dialog", {
        name: /session expiring soon/i,
      }),
    ).toBeTruthy();

    fireEvent.keyDown(window, { key: "a" });

    expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).toBeNull();

    await advanceTime(28 * 60 * 1000);

    expect(
      screen.getByRole("dialog", {
        name: /session expiring soon/i,
      }),
    ).toBeTruthy();

    expect(logout).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("allows the user to stay signed in when the warning appears", async () => {
    renderSessionManager();

    await advanceTime(28 * 60 * 1000);

    fireEvent.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", {
        name: /stay signed in/i,
      }),
    );

    expect(screen.queryByRole("dialog")).toBeNull();

    expect(store.getState().auth.accessToken).toBe("test-access-token");

    await advanceTime(28 * 60 * 1000);

    expect(
      screen.getByRole("dialog", {
        name: /session expiring soon/i,
      }),
    ).toBeTruthy();

    expect(logout).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("gates the proactive token refresh on tab visibility", () => {
    renderSessionManager();

    expect(startTokenRefreshScheduler).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({ canExtendSession: isTabVisible }),
    );
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\features\queue\queueSlice.test.js
========================================
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { setupStore } from "../../../src/app/store";
import {
  fetchQueue,
  fetchTodayQueue,
  selectQueueError,
  selectQueueRecords,
  selectQueueRequestDate,
  selectQueueStatus,
} from "../../../src/features/queue/queueSlice";
import { toLocalDateString } from "../../../src/utils/dateUtils";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../../msw/handlers";

const authState = {
  auth: {
    accessToken: "slice-token",
    role: "DOCTOR",
    authStatus: "authenticated",
  },
};

const queueState = (store) => ({
  records: selectQueueRecords(store.getState()),
  status: selectQueueStatus(store.getState()),
  error: selectQueueError(store.getState()),
  requestDate: selectQueueRequestDate(store.getState()),
});

afterEach(resetMswState);

describe("queueSlice â€” date-aware doctor queue", () => {
  it("fetches today's queue when no date is given and keeps server order", async () => {
    mswState.queueResponses = [
      [
        { appointmentId: 2, status: "SCHEDULED" },
        { appointmentId: 1, status: "COMPLETED" },
      ],
    ];
    const store = setupStore(authState);
    await store.dispatch(fetchTodayQueue());

    const state = queueState(store);
    expect(state.status).toBe("succeeded");
    expect(state.requestDate).toBeNull();
    // Server-authoritative order â€” no client-side re-sorting.
    expect(state.records.map((record) => record.appointmentId)).toEqual([2, 1]);
  });

  it("records the requested date on the state", async () => {
    mswState.queueResponses = [[]];
    const store = setupStore(authState);
    await store.dispatch(fetchQueue({ date: "2020-05-05" }));
    expect(queueState(store).requestDate).toBe("2020-05-05");
  });

  it("keeps previous rows visible while a refresh is pending (non-destructive)", async () => {
    mswState.queueResponses = [
      [
        {
          appointmentId: 5,
          status: "SCHEDULED",
          appointmentDate: "2020-05-05",
        },
      ],
    ];
    const store = setupStore(authState);
    await store.dispatch(fetchQueue({ date: "2020-05-05" }));
    expect(queueState(store).records).toHaveLength(1);

    mswState.queueErrorMode = "delay";
    const pending = store.dispatch(fetchQueue({ date: "2020-05-05" }));
    // Rows stay on screen during the refresh instead of blanking out.
    expect(queueState(store).records).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(queueState(store).status).toBe("loading");

    mswState.queueErrorMode = null;
    mswState.queueResponses = [[]];
    await pending;
    expect(queueState(store).records).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("drops a stale response when the date changed while in flight", async () => {
    const store = setupStore(authState);

    mswState.queueErrorMode = "delay";
    const slow = store.dispatch(fetchQueue({ date: "2020-05-05" }));
    // Let the slow request actually start its delay before flipping the mode,
    // otherwise the handler would miss "delay" and race the faster request.
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 20));

    mswState.queueErrorMode = null;
    mswState.queueResponses = [
      [
        {
          appointmentId: 9,
          status: "SCHEDULED",
          appointmentDate: "2020-05-06",
        },
      ],
    ];
    await store.dispatch(fetchQueue({ date: "2020-05-06" }));
    expect(
      queueState(store).records.map((record) => record.appointmentId),
    ).toEqual([9]);

    // The old date's response arrives later and must be discarded.
    mswState.queueResponses = [];
    await slow;
    const state = queueState(store);
    expect(state.records.map((record) => record.appointmentId)).toEqual([9]);
    expect(state.status).toBe("succeeded");
    expect(state.requestDate).toBe("2020-05-06");
  });

  it("blocks a duplicate in-flight request for the same date only", async () => {
    const store = setupStore(authState);
    mswState.queueErrorMode = "delay";
    const first = store.dispatch(fetchQueue({ date: "2020-05-05" }));

    // Same date â†’ condition blocks the duplicate before any network call.
    const duplicate = store.dispatch(fetchQueue({ date: "2020-05-05" }));
    duplicate?.catch?.(() => {});

    // A different date may still start.
    const otherDate = store.dispatch(fetchQueue({ date: "2020-05-06" }));

    mswState.queueErrorMode = null;
    await Promise.allSettled([first, duplicate, otherDate]);
    // Only the first and the different-date request reached the network.
    expect(mswState.queueHits).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("surfaces fetch failures as a retryable failed state", async () => {
    mswState.queueErrorMode = "server-error";
    const store = setupStore(authState);
    await store.dispatch(fetchQueue({ date: toLocalDateString() }));

    const state = queueState(store);
    expect(state.status).toBe("failed");
    expect(state.error).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\hooks\useQueuePolling.test.jsx
========================================
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { act, renderHook } from "@testing-library/react";

import useQueuePolling, {
  QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS,
} from "../../src/hooks/useQueuePolling";

describe("useQueuePolling", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it("exports a ~2s configurable cadence", () => {
    expect(QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS).toBe(2000);
  });

  it("ticks at the configured interval while enabled", () => {
    const onTick = vi.fn();
    renderHook(() => useQueuePolling({ enabled: true, onTick }));

    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS));
    expect(onTick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);

    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS * 2));
    expect(onTick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
  });

  it("does not tick while disabled and stops after unmount", () => {
    const onTick = vi.fn();
    const { rerender, unmount } = renderHook(
      ({ enabled }) => useQueuePolling({ enabled, onTick }),
      { initialProps: { enabled: false } },
    );

    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS * 3));
    expect(onTick).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    rerender({ enabled: true });
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS));
    expect(onTick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);

    unmount();
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS * 5));
    expect(onTick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("honours an injected interval for tests", () => {
    const onTick = vi.fn();
    renderHook(() => useQueuePolling({ onTick, intervalMs: 50 }));

    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(150));
    expect(onTick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
  });

  it("skips ticks while the tab is hidden", () => {
    const onTick = vi.fn();
    renderHook(() => useQueuePolling({ onTick, intervalMs: 50 }));

    const hidden = vi.spyOn(document, "hidden", "get").mockReturnValue(true);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(100));
    expect(onTick).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    hidden.mockReturnValue(false);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(100));
    expect(onTick).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    hidden.mockRestore();
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\layouts\layoutAccessibility.integration.test.jsx
========================================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { Provider } from "react-redux";
import { MemoryRouter } from "react-router-dom";

import { store } from "../../src/app/store";
import { clearSession, setSession } from "../../src/features/auth/authSlice";
import { ROLES } from "../../src/constants/roles";
import AppRoutes from "../../src/routes/AppRoutes";
import PublicLayout from "../../src/layouts/PublicLayout";

/**
 * CFG-07 AC4 â€” keyboard-only use: every interactive element in the shared
 * layouts is reachable by Tab in logical (DOM) order.
 *
 * jsdom verifies reachability and order; the visible focus ring and the
 * 360 px no-overflow guarantees are verified in the real-browser
 * (Playwright) suite, since jsdom performs no layout.
 */
const renderAppAt = (initialPath) =>
  render(
    <Provider store={store}>
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[initialPath]}>
        <AppRoutes />
      </MemoryRouter>
    </Provider>,
  );

const tabUntil = async (user, match, maxStops = 25) => {
  const stops = [];
  for (let index = 0; index < maxStops; index += 1) {
    await user.tab();
    const active = document.activeElement;
    const description = {
      tag: active?.tagName,
      name:
        active?.getAttribute("aria-label") ?? active?.textContent?.trim() ?? "",
      href: active?.getAttribute("href") ?? null,
    };
    stops.push(description);
    if (match(description, active)) return { reached: true, stops };
  }
  return { reached: false, stops };
};

beforeEach(() => {
  store.dispatch(clearSession());
});

describe("StaffLayout â€” CFG-07 AC4 keyboard tab order (integration)", () => {
  it("reaches every chrome control in logical order with no traps", async () => {
    store.dispatch(
      setSession({
        accessToken: "a",
        role: ROLES.DOCTOR,
        user: "dr.house",
      }),
    );
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderAppAt("/doctor/dashboard");

    // Top bar first: Home button â†’ language select â†’ logout.
    const home = await tabUntil(user, (s) => s.name === "Home");
    expect(home.reached).toBe(true);

    const logout = await tabUntil(user, (s) => s.name === "Log out");
    expect(logout.reached).toBe(true);

    // Then the sidebar links, then the breadcrumb link â€” all reachable.
    const dashboard = await tabUntil(
      user,
      (s) => s.tag === "A" && s.href?.endsWith("/doctor/dashboard"),
    );
    expect(dashboard.reached).toBe(true);

    const queue = await tabUntil(user, (s) => s.tag === "A");
    expect(queue.reached).toBe(true);
  });

  it("never skips backwards or lands on hidden elements while tabbing", async () => {
    store.dispatch(setSession({ accessToken: "a", role: ROLES.DOCTOR }));
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderAppAt("/doctor/dashboard");

    // Tab through one full cycle; each stop must be a visible, focusable
    // control. The cycle ends when focus wraps back to the first stop.
    await user.tab();
    const first = document.activeElement;
    expect(first.tagName).toMatch(/BUTTON|SELECT|A|INPUT/);

    for (let index = 0; index < 15; index += 1) {
      await user.tab();
      if (
        document.activeElement === first ||
        document.activeElement === document.body
      ) {
        break;
      }
      expect(document.activeElement.tagName).toMatch(/BUTTON|SELECT|A|INPUT/);
      expect(document.activeElement).not.toBeDisabled();
    }
  });
});

describe("PublicLayout â€” CFG-07 AC4 keyboard tab order (integration)", () => {
  it("reaches the header nav links and the login link in order", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={["/home"]}>
        <PublicLayout />
      </MemoryRouter>,
    );

    const logo = await tabUntil(
      user,
      (s) => s.tag === "A" && s.href === "/home",
    );
    expect(logo.reached).toBe(true);
  });

  it("exposes the shared focus-visible ring declared in the design system", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={["/home"]}>
        <PublicLayout />
      </MemoryRouter>,
    );

    await user.tab();
    const active = document.activeElement;
    expect(active.tagName).toBe("A");
    // The global :focus-visible rule in src/index.css supplies the ring;
    // jsdom cannot compute it, so assert the stylesheet contract instead.
    const css = await import("node:fs").then((fs) =>
      fs.readFileSync("src/index.css", "utf8"),
    );
    expect(css).toMatch(/:focus-visible[^}]*ring-2/s);
  });
});

describe("Shared layouts â€” CFG-07 AC1/AC3 structural guards (jsdom)", () => {
  it("staff main can shrink below content width so overflow stays contained", () => {
    store.dispatch(setSession({ accessToken: "a", role: ROLES.DOCTOR }));
    const { container } = renderAppAt("/doctor/dashboard");

    const main = container.querySelector("main");
    expect(main).not.toBeNull();
    expect(main.className).toContain("min-w-0");
  });

  it("sidebar strip scrolls inside its own nav at narrow widths", () => {
    store.dispatch(setSession({ accessToken: "a", role: ROLES.DOCTOR }));
    const { container } = renderAppAt("/doctor/dashboard");

    const list = container.querySelector(
      'nav[aria-label="Main navigation"] ul',
    );
    expect(list.className).toContain("overflow-x-auto");
  });

  it("styleguide wide table lives in a scrollable region, not the page", () => {
    renderAppAt("/styleguide");

    const region = screen.getByRole("region", {
      name: "Example appointment list",
    });
    expect(region.className).toContain("overflow-x-auto");
    expect(region).toHaveAttribute("tabindex", "0");
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { Provider } from "react-redux";

    <Provider store={store}>
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[initialPath]}>

      user: "dr.house",

    renderAppAt("/doctor/dashboard");

    expect(
      screen.getByRole("heading", { name: /today.s appointments/i }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /log out/i }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("dr.house")).toBeInTheDocument();

    const sidebar = screen.getByRole("navigation", {
      name: "Main navigation",
    });
    expect(
      within(sidebar).getByRole("link", { name: "Todayâ€™s schedule" }),
    ).toHaveAttribute("href", "/doctor/today-schedule");

    const crumbs = screen.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Breadcrumb" });
    expect(within(crumbs).getByText("Dashboard")).toHaveAttribute(
      "aria-current",
      "page",
    );
  });

  it("renders the shared /queue screen inside the staff chrome", () => {
    seed({ accessToken: "a", role: ROLES.ADMIN });

    renderAppAt("/queue");

    expect(
      screen.getByRole("heading", { name: "QueuePage" }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Breadcrumb" }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /log out/i }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("gives the pharmacist only its own menu entries", () => {
    seed({ accessToken: "a", role: ROLES.PHARMACIST });

    renderAppAt("/pharmacist/dashboard");

    expect(
      screen.getByRole("heading", { name: "PharmacistDashboardPage" }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.queryByRole("link", { name: "Queue" }),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

  auditLogsHits: [],
  auditLogsErrorMode: null,
  rolePermissions: defaultRolePermissions(),
  rolePermissionReadHits: [],
  rolePermissionWriteHits: [],
  rolePermissionsErrorMode: null,
  doctorSchedules: defaultDoctorSchedules(),
  doctorScheduleHits: [],
  doctorScheduleWriteHits: [],
  doctorScheduleErrorMode: null,
  systemBackups: defaultSystemBackups(),
  systemBackupHits: [],
  systemBackupCreateHits: [],
  systemBackupDownloadHits: [],
  systemBackupsErrorMode: null,
  systemRestoreHits: [],
  systemRestoreErrorMode: null,
};

export const resetMswState = () => {
  mswState.tokenValid = true;
  mswState.refreshValid = true;
  mswState.refreshCount = 0;
  mswState.protectedHits = [];
  mswState.refreshTokens = [];
  mswState.loginValid = true;
  mswState.loginRole = "ADMIN";
  mswState.loginErrorMode = null;
  mswState.forgotPasswordHits = [];
  mswState.forgotPasswordErrorMode = null;
  mswState.resetPasswordHits = [];
  mswState.resetPasswordErrorMode = null;
  mswState.queueHits = [];
  mswState.queueErrorMode = null;
  mswState.queueResponses = [];
  mswState.queueCompleteHits = [];
  mswState.queueCompleteErrorMode = null;
  mswState.patientsHits = [];
  mswState.patientsResponses = [];
  mswState.patientsErrorMode = null;
  mswState.patientErrorMode = null;
  mswState.appointmentsHits = [];
  mswState.appointmentsResponses = [];
  mswState.appointmentsErrorMode = null;
  mswState.appointmentByIdResponses = {};
  mswState.appointmentByIdHits = [];
  mswState.appointmentByIdErrorMode = null;
  mswState.appointmentUpdateHits = [];
  mswState.appointmentUpdateErrorMode = null;
  mswState.clinicalNotesHits = [];
  mswState.clinicalNotesResponses = [];
  mswState.clinicalNotesErrorMode = null;
  mswState.historyHits = [];
  mswState.historyResponses = [];
  mswState.historyErrorMode = null;
  mswState.prescriptionCreateHits = [];
  mswState.prescriptionCreateResponses = [];
  mswState.prescriptionCreateErrorMode = null;
  mswState.usersHits = [];
  mswState.usersResponses = [];
  mswState.usersErrorMode = null;
  mswState.userCreateHits = [];
  mswState.userCreateErrorMode = null;
  mswState.userEmailUpdateHits = [];
  mswState.userRoleUpdateHits = [];
  mswState.userUnlockHits = [];
  mswState.userDeactivateHits = [];
  mswState.userResetHits = [];
  mswState.userResetErrorMode = null;
  mswState.auditLogs = defaultAuditLogs();
  mswState.auditLogsHits = [];
  mswState.auditLogsErrorMode = null;

  mswState.systemBackupCreateHits = [];
  mswState.systemBackupDownloadHits = [];
  mswState.systemBackupsErrorMode = null;
  mswState.systemRestoreHits = [];
  mswState.systemRestoreErrorMode = null;
};

const ok = (data, message = "ok") =>
  HttpResponse.json({ success: true, message, data });

const fail = (status, message) =>
  HttpResponse.json({ success: false, message, data: null }, { status });

const protectedReply = ({ request, params }) => {
  mswState.protectedHits.push({
    id: params.id ?? params.path,
    authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
  });
  if (!mswState.tokenValid) return fail(401, "Access token expired");
  return ok({ id: params.id ?? params.path, name: "Jane Doe" });
};

export const handlers = [
  http.post("*/auth/login", async ({ request }) => {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));

    if (mswState.loginErrorMode === "rate-limit") {
      return fail(429, "Too many requests. Please slow down.");
    }

    if (mswState.loginErrorMode === "lockout") {
      return fail(
        423,
        "Account locked. Wait for timed unlock, contact Super Admin, or use Forgot Password.",
      );
    }

    if (!mswState.loginValid) {
      return fail(401, "Invalid credentials");
    }

    return ok({
      accessToken: "login-access",
      refreshToken: "login-refresh",
      expiresIn: 1800,
      role: mswState.loginRole,
      username: body?.username ?? "test-user",
      redirectUrl: ROLE_DASHBOARDS[mswState.loginRole],
      user: {
        name: "Test User",
        email: body?.username ?? "test@onecare.test",
      },
    });
  }),
  http.post("*/auth/forgot-password", async ({ request }) => {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    mswState.forgotPasswordHits.push(body);

    if (mswState.forgotPasswordErrorMode === "rate-limit") {
      return fail(429, "Too many requests. Please slow down.");
    }
    if (mswState.forgotPasswordErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.forgotPasswordErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }

    return ok(null, "Password reset instructions sent");
  }),
  http.post("*/auth/reset-password", async ({ request }) => {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    mswState.resetPasswordHits.push(body);

    if (mswState.resetPasswordErrorMode === "rate-limit") {
      return fail(429, "Too many requests. Please slow down.");
    }
    if (mswState.resetPasswordErrorMode === "invalid-token") {
      return fail(400, "Invalid or expired token");
    }
    if (mswState.resetPasswordErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }

    return ok(null, "Password has been reset successfully");
  }),
  http.get("*/queue/today", async ({ request }) => {
    mswState.queueHits.push({
      url: request.url,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.queueErrorMode === "network-error")
      return HttpResponse.error();
    if (mswState.queueErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.queueErrorMode === "delay") {
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
    }
    const data = mswState.queueResponses.shift() ?? [];
    return ok(data);
  }),
  // DOC-04 Complete action â€” verified backend contract:
  // PATCH /api/queue/{appointmentId}/status with { status: "COMPLETED" }.
  http.patch("*/queue/:id/status", async ({ request, params }) => {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    mswState.queueCompleteHits.push({
      id: params.id,
      body,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.queueCompleteErrorMode === "conflict") {
      return fail(409, "Appointment status changed");
    }
    // The backend reports conflicting transitions as 400
    // (InvalidAppointmentStatusException), not 409.
    if (mswState.queueCompleteErrorMode === "bad-request") {
      return fail(400, "Invalid appointment status transition");
    }
    if (mswState.queueCompleteErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.queueCompleteErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    return ok({
      appointmentId: Number(params.id),
      status: body?.status ?? "COMPLETED",
    });
  }),
  // Patient directory (doctor holds PATIENT_READ) used only to label rows.
  http.get("*/patients", ({ request }) => {
    mswState.patientsHits.push({
      url: request.url,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.patientsErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    if (mswState.patientsErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    return ok(mswState.patientsResponses.shift() ?? []);
  }),
  // Single patient (doctor holds PATIENT_READ). Verified `PatientResponse`
  // shape is `patientId`/`fullName`; the legacy `id`/`name` fields are kept
  // for the apiClient integration probe that reads `data.name`.
  http.get("*/patients/:id", ({ request, params }) => {
    mswState.protectedHits.push({
      id: params.id,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (!mswState.tokenValid) return fail(401, "Access token expired");
    // Optional failure modes so callers can test best-effort enrichment
    // without tripping the 401 refresh flow.
    if (mswState.patientErrorMode === "not-found") {
      return fail(404, "Patient not found");
    }
    if (mswState.patientErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.patientErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    return ok({
      id: params.id,
      name: "Jane Doe",
      patientId: Number(params.id),
      fullName: "Jane Doe",
    });
  }),
  // `GET /api/appointments` returns the doctor's list; a single appointment
  // is read through the dedicated `GET /api/appointments/{id}` below.
  http.get("*/appointments", async ({ request }) => {
    mswState.appointmentsHits.push({
      url: request.url,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.appointmentsErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    if (mswState.appointmentsErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.appointmentsErrorMode === "delay") {
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
    }
    return ok(mswState.appointmentsResponses.shift() ?? []);
  }),
  // Clinical notes live on the appointment (unmerged backend branch
  // `fix/44/align-clinical-notes`): PUT accepts only { clinicalNotes }.
  http.put("*/appointments/:id", async ({ request, params }) => {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    mswState.appointmentUpdateHits.push({
      id: params.id,
      body,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.appointmentUpdateErrorMode === "cancelled") {
      return fail(
        400,
        "Clinical notes cannot be added to a cancelled appointment",
      );
    }
    if (mswState.appointmentUpdateErrorMode === "forbidden") {
      return fail(403, "Access denied");
    }
    if (mswState.appointmentUpdateErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.appointmentUpdateErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    return ok({
      appointmentId: Number(params.id),
      status: "SCHEDULED",
      clinicalNotes: body?.clinicalNotes ?? null,
    });
  }),
  http.get("*/appointments/:id/clinical-notes", async ({ params }) => {
    mswState.clinicalNotesHits.push({ id: params.id });
    if (mswState.clinicalNotesErrorMode === "not-found") {
      return fail(404, "Appointment not found");
    }
    if (mswState.clinicalNotesErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.clinicalNotesErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    return ok(
      mswState.clinicalNotesResponses.shift() ?? {
        appointmentId: Number(params.id),
        patientId: null,
        doctorId: null,
        clinicalNotes: "",
      },
    );
  }),
  // `GET /appointments/{id}` â€” the consultation screen resolves a single
  // appointment directly. Seeded per-id through `appointmentByIdResponses`;
  // unseeded ids answer with a minimal SCHEDULED record so callers that only
  // need the id keep working.
  http.get("*/appointments/:id", async ({ request, params }) => {
    mswState.appointmentByIdHits.push({
      id: params.id,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.appointmentByIdErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    if (mswState.appointmentByIdErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.appointmentByIdErrorMode === "not-found") {
      return fail(404, "Appointment not found");
    }
    const stored = mswState.appointmentByIdResponses[params.id];
    if (stored) return ok(stored);
    return ok({
      appointmentId: Number(params.id),
      patientId: null,
      doctorId: null,
      appointmentDate: null,
      timeSlot: null,
      status: "SCHEDULED",
      clinicalNotes: "",
    });
  }),
  http.get("*/patients/:patientId/history", async ({ params }) => {
    mswState.historyHits.push({ patientId: params.patientId });
    if (mswState.historyErrorMode === "not-found") {
      return fail(404, "Patient not found");
    }
    if (mswState.historyErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.historyErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    return ok(
      mswState.historyResponses.shift() ?? {
        patient: {
          patientId: Number(params.patientId),
          fullName: `Patient ${params.patientId}`,
        },
        patientId: Number(params.patientId),
        appointments: [],
        prescriptions: [],
      },
    );
  }),
  http.post("*/prescriptions", async ({ request }) => {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    mswState.prescriptionCreateHits.push({
      body,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.prescriptionCreateErrorMode === "validation") {
      return fail(400, "Invalid prescription");
    }
    if (mswState.prescriptionCreateErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.prescriptionCreateErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    return ok(
      mswState.prescriptionCreateResponses.shift() ?? {
        prescriptionId: 9001,
        patientId: body?.patientId ?? null,
        doctorId: 3,
        appointmentId: body?.appointmentId ?? null,
        date: "2026-10-14",
        status: "ISSUED",
        items: body?.items ?? [],
        createdAt: "2026-10-14T09:00:00",
      },
    );
  }),
  // Super-admin user management (access governance): list, provision,
  // update email/role, unlock the failed-login lockout, admin reset and
  // deactivate. Account state (isActive + lockedUntil) is backend-
  // managed â€” the mutation handlers echo the accepted change only.
  http.get("*/users", ({ request }) => {
    mswState.usersHits.push({
      url: request.url,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.usersErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    if (mswState.usersErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    return ok(mswState.usersResponses.shift() ?? []);
  }),
  http.post("*/users", async ({ request }) => {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    mswState.userCreateHits.push({
      body,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.userCreateErrorMode === "validation") {
      return fail(400, "Username already exists");
    }
    if (mswState.userCreateErrorMode === "forbidden") {
      return fail(403, "Access denied");
    }
    if (mswState.userCreateErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    return ok({
      userId: 9001,
      ...body,
      isActive: true,
    });
  }),
  http.put("*/users/:id/role", async ({ request, params }) => {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    mswState.userRoleUpdateHits.push({
      id: params.id,
      body,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (!Object.values(ROLES).includes(body?.role)) {
      return fail(400, "Unknown role");
    }
    return ok({ userId: Number(params.id), role: body.role });
  }),
  http.put("*/users/:id/unlock", ({ request, params }) => {
    mswState.userUnlockHits.push({
      id: params.id,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    return ok({ userId: Number(params.id), isActive: true, lockedUntil: null });
  }),
  http.put("*/users/:id", async ({ request, params }) => {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    mswState.userEmailUpdateHits.push({
      id: params.id,
      body,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (typeof body?.email !== "string" || !body.email.trim()) {
      return fail(400, "Email is required");
    }
    return ok({ userId: Number(params.id), email: body.email });
  }),
  http.delete("*/users/:id", ({ request, params }) => {
    mswState.userDeactivateHits.push({
      id: params.id,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    return ok({ userId: Number(params.id), isActive: false });
  }),
  http.post("*/users/:id/reset-password", ({ request, params }) => {
    mswState.userResetHits.push({
      id: params.id,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.userResetErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    return ok(null, "Password reset instructions sent");
  }),
  // FR010/UC5 â€” system backup & restore (Super Admin, AC1/AC4). The
  // mock stores backup metadata only; downloads stream fake encrypted
  // bytes and restore mirrors the confirm-first / checksum rules.
  http.get("*/system/backups", ({ request }) => {
    mswState.systemBackupHits.push({
      url: request.url,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.systemBackupsErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    if (mswState.systemBackupsErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.systemBackupsErrorMode === "forbidden") {
      return fail(403, "Access denied");
    }
    return ok(
      [...mswState.systemBackups].sort((a, b) => b.backupId - a.backupId),
      "Backups retrieved successfully",
    );
  }),
  http.post("*/system/backups", ({ request }) => {
    mswState.systemBackupCreateHits.push({
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.systemBackupsErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.systemBackupsErrorMode === "forbidden") {
      return fail(403, "Access denied");
    }
    const lastId = mswState.systemBackups.reduce(
      (max, backup) => Math.max(max, backup.backupId),
      0,
    );
    const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
    const compactStamp = createdAt.replace(/[-:]/g, "").replace(/\..+$/, "");
    const record = {
      backupId: lastId + 1,
      createdAt,
      filename: `onecare-backup-${compactStamp}.backup`,
      sizeBytes: 45000 + (lastId + 1) * 1137,
    };
    mswState.systemBackups = [record, ...mswState.systemBackups];
    return HttpResponse.json(
      {
        success: true,
        message: "Backup created successfully",
        data: record,
      },
      { status: 201 },
    );
  }),
  http.get("*/system/backups/:backupId/download", ({ request, params }) => {
    mswState.systemBackupDownloadHits.push({
      id: params.backupId,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.systemBackupsErrorMode === "forbidden") {
      return fail(403, "Access denied");
    }
    const backup = mswState.systemBackups.find(
      (item) => String(item.backupId) === String(params.backupId),
    );
    if (!backup) {
      return fail(404, "Backup not found");
    }
    return new HttpResponse(
      `MOCK-ENCRYPTED-BACKUP ${backup.filename} ${backup.sizeBytes}`,
      {
        status: 200,
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/octet-stream",
          "Content-Disposition": `attachment; filename="${backup.filename}"`,
        },
      },
    );
  }),
  http.post("*/system/restore", async ({ request }) => {
    const form = await request.formData().catch(() => null);
    const uploaded = form?.get("file");
    mswState.systemRestoreHits.push({
      backupId: form?.get("backupId") ?? null,
      fileName:
        uploaded && typeof uploaded === "object" ? uploaded.name : null,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.systemRestoreErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    if (mswState.systemRestoreErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.systemRestoreErrorMode === "forbidden") {
      return fail(403, "Access denied");
    }
    if (!form) {
      return fail(400, "Multipart form data required");
    }
    if (form.get("confirm") !== "true") {
      return fail(400, "Explicit confirmation required");
    }
    const backupId = form.get("backupId");
    const hasFile = Boolean(uploaded && typeof uploaded === "object");
    if (!backupId && !hasFile) {
      return fail(
        400,
        "Provide a backupId or an uploaded backup file to restore",
      );
    }
    if (backupId) {
      const known = mswState.systemBackups.some(
        (item) => String(item.backupId) === String(backupId),
      );
      if (!known) return fail(404, "Unknown backupId");
    }
    if (mswState.systemRestoreErrorMode === "corrupt") {
      return fail(400, "Checksum mismatch â€” the backup payload is corrupted");
    }
    return ok(
      { users: 12, patients: 340, appointments: 1250 },
      "Restore completed successfully",
    );
  }),
  // Audit trail â€” Spring `Page` contract (GET /api/audit-logs). Newest
  // first; `?page=` (0-based) and `?size=` slice the seeded trail and
  // the envelope mirrors the real pageable/total* metadata.
  http.get("*/audit-logs", ({ request }) => {
    mswState.auditLogsHits.push({
      url: request.url,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.auditLogsErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    if (mswState.auditLogsErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const requestedPage = Number(url.searchParams.get("page") ?? 0);
    const requestedSize = Number(url.searchParams.get("size") ?? 20);
    const page =
      Number.isFinite(requestedPage) && requestedPage >= 0
        ? requestedPage
        : 0;
    const size =
      Number.isFinite(requestedSize) && requestedSize > 0
        ? requestedSize
        : 20;
    const sorted = [...mswState.auditLogs].sort((a, b) => b.logId - a.logId);
    const totalElements = sorted.length;
    const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(totalElements / size));
    const content = sorted.slice(page * size, page * size + size);
    const pageSort = { empty: false, unsorted: false, sorted: true };
    return ok(
      {
        content,
        pageable: {
          pageNumber: page,
          pageSize: size,
          sort: pageSort,
          offset: page * size,
          unpaged: false,
          paged: true,
        },
        last: page >= totalPages - 1,
        totalPages,
        totalElements,
        size,
        number: page,
        sort: pageSort,
        numberOfElements: content.length,
        first: page === 0,
        empty: content.length === 0,
      },
      "Audit logs retrieved successfully",
    );
  }),
  // FR003/UC3 â€” configurable role permissions (super-admin governance).
  // PUT replaces the full set atomically: every policy rule must pass or
  // the stored set stays untouched (AC5). The mock enforces BR004-006
  // like the backend; the error mode lets tests force any single rule.
  http.get("*/roles/:role/permissions", ({ request, params }) => {
    mswState.rolePermissionReadHits.push({
      role: params.role,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.rolePermissionsErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    if (mswState.rolePermissionsErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (
      !(params.role in mswState.rolePermissions) ||
      mswState.rolePermissionsErrorMode === "not-found"
    ) {
      return fail(404, "Unknown role");
    }
    return ok(
      {
        role: params.role,
        permissions: [...mswState.rolePermissions[params.role]],
      },
      "Permissions retrieved successfully",
    );
  }),
  http.put("*/roles/:role/permissions", async ({ request, params }) => {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const permissions = Array.isArray(body?.permissions)
      ? body.permissions
      : null;
    mswState.rolePermissionWriteHits.push({
      role: params.role,
      permissions,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.rolePermissionsErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    if (mswState.rolePermissionsErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.rolePermissionsErrorMode === "forbidden") {
      return fail(403, "Access denied");
    }
    if (!(params.role in mswState.rolePermissions)) {
      return fail(404, "Unknown role");
    }
    if (!permissions) {
      return fail(400, "Request body must include a permissions array");
    }
    const unknown = permissions.filter(
      (code) => !PERMISSION_CODES.includes(code),
    );
    if (unknown.length > 0) {
      return fail(400, `Unknown permission(s): ${unknown.join(", ")}`);
    }
    if (mswState.rolePermissionsErrorMode === "br004") {
      return fail(
        422,
        "BR004: SYSTEM_BACKUP is a privileged permission that only the SUPER_ADMIN role may hold",
      );
    }
    if (mswState.rolePermissionsErrorMode === "br005") {
      return fail(
        422,
        "BR005: the SUPER_ADMIN role's own permission set cannot be modified",
      );
    }
    if (mswState.rolePermissionsErrorMode === "br006") {
      return fail(422, "BR006: a role's permission set cannot be empty");
    }
    if (params.role === ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN) {
      return fail(
        422,
        "BR005: the SUPER_ADMIN role's own permission set cannot be modified",
      );
    }
    if (permissions.length === 0) {
      return fail(422, "BR006: a role's permission set cannot be empty");
    }
    if (permissions.includes("SYSTEM_BACKUP")) {
      return fail(
        422,
        "BR004: SYSTEM_BACKUP is a privileged permission that only the SUPER_ADMIN role may hold",
      );
    }
    mswState.rolePermissions[params.role] = [...permissions];
    return ok(
      { role: params.role, permissions: [...permissions] },
      "Permissions updated successfully",
    );
  }),
  // FR009/UC4 â€” doctor working schedules (booking availability). PUT
  // replaces the whole schedule and mirrors the backend's atomic rules:
  // empty sets, malformed HH:mm, end â‰¤ start, or overlapping slots
  // (400 "Time slot conflict" carrying data.conflictingSlots).
  http.get("*/doctors/:doctorId/schedule", ({ request, params }) => {
    mswState.doctorScheduleHits.push({
      id: params.doctorId,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.doctorScheduleErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    if (mswState.doctorScheduleErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.doctorScheduleErrorMode === "forbidden") {
      return fail(403, "Access denied");
    }
    const stored = mswState.doctorSchedules[params.doctorId];
    if (!stored) {
      return fail(404, "Doctor not found");
    }
    return ok(
      {
        doctorId: Number(params.doctorId),
        workingDays: [...stored.workingDays],
        timeSlots: stored.timeSlots.map((slot) => ({ ...slot })),
      },
      "Schedule retrieved successfully",
    );
  }),
  http.put("*/doctors/:doctorId/schedule", async ({ request, params }) => {
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    mswState.doctorScheduleWriteHits.push({
      id: params.doctorId,
      body,
      authorization: request.headers.get("Authorization"),
    });
    if (mswState.doctorScheduleErrorMode === "network-error") {
      return HttpResponse.error();
    }
    if (mswState.doctorScheduleErrorMode === "server-error") {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    if (mswState.doctorScheduleErrorMode === "forbidden") {
      return fail(403, "Access denied");
    }
    if (!(params.doctorId in mswState.doctorSchedules)) {
      return fail(404, "Doctor not found");
    }
    const workingDays = Array.isArray(body?.workingDays)
      ? body.workingDays
      : null;
    const timeSlots = Array.isArray(body?.timeSlots) ? body.timeSlots : null;
    if (!workingDays || !timeSlots) {
      return fail(400, "workingDays and timeSlots are required");
    }
    if (workingDays.length === 0 || timeSlots.length === 0) {
      return fail(400, "Working days and time slots cannot be empty");
    }
    if (workingDays.some((day) => !WORKING_DAYS.includes(day))) {
      return fail(400, "Unknown working day");
    }
    if (
      timeSlots.some(
        (slot) => !isValidTime(slot?.start) || !isValidTime(slot?.end),
      )
    ) {
      return fail(400, "Time slots must use HH:mm");
    }
    if (timeSlots.some((slot) => slot.end <= slot.start)) {
      return fail(400, "A time slot must end after it starts");
    }
    const conflicts = findConflictingSlots(timeSlots);
    if (conflicts.length > 0) {
      return HttpResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          message: "Time slot conflict",
          data: { conflictingSlots: conflicts },
        },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }
    mswState.doctorSchedules[params.doctorId] = {
      workingDays: [...workingDays],
      timeSlots: timeSlots.map((slot) => ({ start: slot.start, end: slot.end })),
    };
    return ok(
      {
        doctorId: Number(params.doctorId),
        workingDays,
        timeSlots,
      },
      "Schedule saved successfully",
    );
  }),
  http.get("*/reports/:id", protectedReply),
  http.get("*", ({ request }) => {
    const path = new URL(request.url).pathname;
    if (path.endsWith("/forbidden")) {
      return fail(403, "You do not have permission to view this resource");
    }
    if (path.endsWith("/rate-limited")) {
      return fail(429, "Too many requests. Please slow down.");
    }
    if (path.endsWith("/server-error")) {
      return fail(500, "Internal server error");
    }
    return HttpResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: "Not found" },
      { status: 404 },
    );
  }),
  http.post("*/auth/refresh", async ({ request }) => {
    mswState.refreshCount += 1;
    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    mswState.refreshTokens.push(body?.refreshToken ?? null);
    if (!mswState.refreshValid) {
      return fail(401, "Refresh token invalid");
    }
    mswState.tokenValid = true;
    return ok({
      accessToken: "new-access",
      refreshToken: "new-refresh",
      expiresIn: 7200,
    });
  }),
];

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";

import ConsultationPage, {
  CLINICAL_NOTES_MAX_LENGTH,
  CONCURRENT_CHANGE_MESSAGE,
} from "../../../src/pages/doctor/ConsultationPage";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../../msw/handlers";

const appointment = (overrides = {}) => ({
  appointmentId: 101,
  patientId: 7,
  doctorId: 3,
  appointmentDate: "2026-10-14",
  timeSlot: "09:30:00",
  status: "SCHEDULED",
  reason: "Follow-up visit",
  ...overrides,
});

const historyEntry = (overrides = {}) => ({
  appointmentId: 55,
  patientId: 7,
  doctorId: 3,
  appointmentDate: "2026-09-01",
  timeSlot: "10:00:00",
  status: "COMPLETED",
  reason: "Follow-up visit",
  clinicalNotes: "Follow-up in two weeks.",
  ...overrides,
});

const renderAt = (path) =>
  render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[path]}>
      <Routes>
        <Route
          path="/doctor/consultation/:appointmentId"
          element={<ConsultationPage />}
        />
        <Route
          path="/doctor/today-schedule"
          element={<h1>Todayâ€™s schedule</h1>}
        />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );

const seedBasic = () => {
  mswState.appointmentByIdResponses = { 101: appointment() };
};

/**
 * The page does not expose ARIA landmarks/labels directly, so we anchor on
 * visible headings and text that the component actually renders.
 */
const waitForLoaded = async () => {
  await screen.findByText("Consultation Room");
  await screen.findByText("Patient Details");
};

const notesTextarea = () => screen.getByPlaceholderText(/diagnosis, advice/i);

const addMedicationItem = async (user, overrides = {}) => {
  const fields = {
    medicineName: "Amoxicillin",
    dosage: "1 capsule",
    frequency: "Three times daily",
    durationDays: "7",
    quantity: "21",
    ...overrides,
  };
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/medicine name/i), {
    target: { value: fields.medicineName },
  });
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/dosage/i), {
    target: { value: fields.dosage },
  });
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/frequency/i), {
    target: { value: fields.frequency },
  });
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/duration \(days\)/i), {
    target: { value: fields.durationDays },
  });
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/quantity/i), {
    target: { value: fields.quantity },
  });
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /add medication/i }));
};

afterEach(resetMswState);

describe("ConsultationPage (DOC-04) â€” full consultation workflow", () => {
  it("loads booking, current notes and previous clinical notes", async () => {
    // Current encounter notes ride on the appointment read itself â€” the page
    // no longer issues a separate GET .../clinical-notes while loading.
    mswState.appointmentByIdResponses = {
      101: { ...appointment(), clinicalNotes: "BP 120/80" },
    };
    mswState.historyResponses = [
      {
        patientId: 7,
        appointments: [historyEntry()],
        prescriptions: [],
      },
    ];
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();

    // Booking from GET /api/appointments; name from GET /api/patients/7.
    expect(screen.getByText("Jane Doe")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Scheduled")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(mswState.protectedHits[0].id).toBe("7");

    expect(notesTextarea()).toHaveValue("BP 120/80");
    expect(mswState.clinicalNotesHits).toHaveLength(0); // no separate fetch

    // Previous encounters from GET ../patients/7/history.
    expect(screen.getByText("Previous Clinical Notes")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Follow-up in two weeks.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(mswState.historyHits[0].patientId).toBe("7");
  });

  it("saves clinical notes via PUT /api/appointments/{id}", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    seedBasic();
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();
    fireEvent.change(notesTextarea(), {
      target: { value: "Started antibiotics." },
    });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /save notes/i }));

    expect(
      await screen.findByText("Clinical notes saved."),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(mswState.appointmentUpdateHits).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(mswState.appointmentUpdateHits[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: "101",
      body: { clinicalNotes: "Started antibiotics." },
    });
    // Only notes are sent â€” date, reason and doctor are untouched.
    expect(Object.keys(mswState.appointmentUpdateHits[0].body)).toEqual([
      "clinicalNotes",
    ]);
  });

  it("caps clinical notes at the backend limit of 2000 characters", async () => {
    seedBasic();
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();
    expect(notesTextarea()).toHaveAttribute(
      "maxlength",
      String(CLINICAL_NOTES_MAX_LENGTH),
    );
    expect(
      screen.getByText(new RegExp(`0\\s*/\\s*${CLINICAL_NOTES_MAX_LENGTH}`)),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("surfaces the backend rejection when notes cannot be saved", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    seedBasic();
    mswState.appointmentUpdateErrorMode = "cancelled";
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /save notes/i }));

    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(
      "Clinical notes cannot be added to a cancelled appointment",
    );
  });

  it("issues a prescription with free-text items via POST /api/prescriptions", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    seedBasic();
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();
    await addMedicationItem(user);

    expect(screen.getByText(/Amoxicillin/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /transmit prescription/i }),
    );

    expect(await screen.findByText("Prescription issued.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(mswState.prescriptionCreateHits).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(mswState.prescriptionCreateHits[0].body).toMatchObject({
      patientId: 7,
      appointmentId: 101,
      items: [
        {
          medicineName: "Amoxicillin",
          dosage: "1 capsule",
          frequency: "Three times daily",
          durationDays: 7,
          quantity: 21,
        },
      ],
    });
    // The issued prescription is listed for this consultation.
    expect(screen.getByText(/Prescription #9001/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("blocks an empty prescription item with a validation message", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    seedBasic();
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /add medication/i }));

    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(
      /fill in medicine name/i,
    );
    expect(mswState.prescriptionCreateHits).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("shows an empty state when no medicines were added", async () => {
    seedBasic();
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();
    expect(
      await screen.findByText("No medicines added yet."),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("completes the appointment from this page only (AC4)", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    seedBasic();
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();
    await user.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /complete consultation/i }),
    );

    expect(mswState.queueCompleteHits).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(mswState.queueCompleteHits[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: "101",
      body: { status: "COMPLETED" },
    });
    expect(
      await screen.findByText("Consultation marked as completed."),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    // Status reflects the server response and the action is retired.
    expect(screen.getByText("Completed")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /complete consultation/i }),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    // Notes become read-only for completed appointments.
    expect(
      screen.getByText(
        /this consultation is completed; its clinical notes are read-only/i,
      ),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("shows the concurrent-change message on conflict and reloads (409)", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    // The screen reloads after the conflict, so the same booking is served
    // again (the per-id fixture is not consumed).
    mswState.appointmentByIdResponses = { 101: appointment() };
    mswState.queueCompleteErrorMode = "conflict";
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();
    await user.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /complete consultation/i }),
    );

    expect(
      await screen.findByText(CONCURRENT_CHANGE_MESSAGE),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    // The screen reloads so the doctor sees the refreshed state.
    await waitFor(() => expect(mswState.appointmentByIdHits).toHaveLength(2));
    expect(screen.getByText("Scheduled")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("treats this backend's 400 conflict reports the same way", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    seedBasic();
    mswState.queueCompleteErrorMode = "bad-request";
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();
    await user.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /complete consultation/i }),
    );

    expect(
      await screen.findByText(CONCURRENT_CHANGE_MESSAGE),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    await waitFor(() => expect(mswState.appointmentByIdHits).toHaveLength(2));
  });

  it("surfaces other completion failures as an error toast", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    seedBasic();
    mswState.queueCompleteErrorMode = "server-error";
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();
    await user.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /complete consultation/i }),
    );

    expect(
      await screen.findByText(
        "Unable to complete the consultation. Please try again.",
      ),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    // Still scheduled â€” no optimistic change.
    expect(screen.getByText("Scheduled")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("ignores duplicate completion clicks while the request is in flight", async () => {
    seedBasic();
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();
    const button = screen.getByRole("button", {
      name: /complete consultation/i,
    });
    fireEvent.click(button);
    fireEvent.click(button);

    await waitFor(() => expect(mswState.queueCompleteHits).toHaveLength(1));
    expect(
      await screen.findByText("Consultation marked as completed."),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("locks the form on cancelled appointments", async () => {
    mswState.appointmentByIdResponses = {
      101: { ...appointment(), status: "CANCELLED" },
    };
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();
    expect(
      screen.getByText(
        "Clinical notes cannot be added to a cancelled appointment.",
      ),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /save notes/i }),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /complete consultation/i }),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("shows the empty history state when the patient has no previous notes", async () => {
    seedBasic();
    mswState.historyResponses = [
      { patientId: 7, appointments: [], prescriptions: [] },
    ];
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();
    expect(
      await screen.findByText("No previous clinical notes for this patient."),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("degrades to no-notes when the fix/44 endpoints are unavailable", async () => {
    seedBasic();
    mswState.clinicalNotesErrorMode = "not-found";
    mswState.historyErrorMode = "not-found";
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    // The booking still renders even without the clinical-notes backend.
    await waitForLoaded();
    expect(notesTextarea()).toHaveValue("");
    expect(
      screen.getByText("No previous clinical notes for this patient."),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("reports an unreadable appointment as a load failure (404)", async () => {
    mswState.appointmentByIdErrorMode = "not-found";
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/404");

    // A 404 from GET /appointments/{id} is a failed read, so the page shows
    // the retryable load error rather than the schedule panel.
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(
      /unable to load the consultation/i,
    );
    expect(screen.queryByText(/appointment not available/i)).toBeNull();
    expect(mswState.protectedHits).toHaveLength(0); // no patient fetch
  });

  it("surfaces load failures with a working retry", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mswState.appointmentByIdErrorMode = "server-error";
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(
      /unable to load the consultation/i,
    );

    mswState.appointmentByIdErrorMode = null;
    mswState.appointmentByIdResponses = { 101: appointment() };
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /retry/i }));

    await waitForLoaded();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("alert")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("links back to the doctor's schedule", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    seedBasic();
    mswState.appointmentByIdResponses = {
      101: { ...appointment(), status: "COMPLETED" },
    };
    renderAt("/doctor/consultation/101");

    await waitForLoaded();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /back to schedule/i }));
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("heading", { name: /today.s Schedule/i }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { Provider } from "react-redux";
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";

import DoctorDashboardPage from "../../../src/pages/doctor/DoctorDashboardPage";
import { setupStore } from "../../../src/app/store";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../../msw/handlers";

const renderPage = () => {
  const testStore = setupStore({
    auth: {
      accessToken: "test-token",
      role: "DOCTOR",
      authStatus: "authenticated",
    },
  });
  return render(
    <Provider store={testStore}>
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={["/doctor/dashboard"]}>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/doctor/dashboard" element={<DoctorDashboardPage />} />
          <Route path="/queue" element={<h1>Shared queue</h1>} />
        </Routes>
      </MemoryRouter>
    </Provider>,
  );
};

afterEach(resetMswState);

describe("DoctorDashboardPage", () => {
  it("renders queue records and status counts from the same response", async () => {
    mswState.queueResponses = [
      [
        { appointmentId: 101, status: "SCHEDULED" },
        { appointmentId: 102, status: "SCHEDULED" },
        { appointmentId: 103, status: "COMPLETED" },
      ],
    ];
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText("Appointment #101")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Appointment #102")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Appointment #103")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByText("Total Patients").parentElement,
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    const waitingBadge = screen.getAllByText("SCHEDULED")[0];
    expect(waitingBadge.closest("article")).toHaveTextContent("2");
    const completedBadge = screen.getAllByText("COMPLETED")[0];
    expect(completedBadge.closest("article")).toHaveTextContent("1");
    expect(mswState.queueHits).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("renders the patient's full name supplied by the queue response", async () => {
    mswState.queueResponses = [
      [
        {
          appointmentId: 55,
          patientId: 4,
          status: "SCHEDULED",
          patientFullName: "Priya Raman",
        },
        {
          appointmentId: 56,
          patientId: 5,
          status: "SCHEDULED",
          patientFullName: "   ",
        },
      ],
    ];
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText("Priya Raman")).toBeInTheDocument();
    // A blank name degrades to the `Patient <id>` label rather than a blank.
    expect(screen.getByText("Patient 5")).toBeInTheDocument();
    // The appointment label is only a last resort.
    expect(screen.queryByText("Appointment #55")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("shows a distinct empty state with zero total and keeps queue navigation", async () => {
    mswState.queueResponses = [[]];
    renderPage();
    expect(
      await screen.findByText("No Appointments Scheduled"),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByText("Total Patients").parentElement,
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("alert")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("shows loading feedback without zero totals while the request is pending", async () => {
    mswState.queueErrorMode = "delay";
    mswState.queueResponses = [[{ appointmentId: 1, status: "SCHEDULED" }]];
    renderPage();
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("status", { name: "Loading todayâ€™s metrics..." }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Total Patients")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await screen.findByText("Appointment #1")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("allows retry after errors and replaces the error with fresh queue data", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mswState.queueErrorMode = "server-error";
    renderPage();
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(
      "Unable to load summary metrics",
    );

    mswState.queueErrorMode = null;
    mswState.queueResponses = [[{ appointmentId: 9, status: "SCHEDULED" }]];
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Retry Connection" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("Appointment #9")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByText("SCHEDULED")).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(screen.queryByRole("alert")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(mswState.queueHits).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("keeps error state retryable after a failed retry", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mswState.queueErrorMode = "network-error";
    renderPage();
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Retry Connection" }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(
      "Please check your connection",
    );
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Retry Connection" }),
    ).toBeEnabled();
    expect(mswState.queueHits).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("does not display invented patient details or appointment data", async () => {
    mswState.queueResponses = [
      [
        {
          appointmentId: 4,
          patientId: 1,
          doctorId: 3,
          appointmentDate: "2026-10-14",
          timeSlot: "09:00:00",
          status: "SCHEDULED",
          queuePosition: 1,
        },
      ],
    ];
    renderPage();
    // The record carries a real patient id but no name, so the label is the
    // derived `Patient <id>` â€” never an invented one.
    await screen.findByText("Patient 1");
    expect(screen.queryByText("Appointment #4")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.queryByText(/Jane Doe|vitals|consultation id|sample/i),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
