FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\pages\doctor\PatientDetails.test.jsx
========================================
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";

import PatientDetails from "../../../src/pages/doctor/PatientDetails";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../../msw/handlers";

/** Renders the record view for a given `:patientId`, as the router would. */
const renderRecord = (patientId) =>
  render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[`/doctor/patient-records/${patientId}`]}>
      <Routes>
        <Route
          path="/doctor/patient-records/:patientId"
          element={<PatientDetails />}
        />
        <Route
          path="/doctor/patient-records"
          element={<div data-testid="directory">All patients</div>}
        />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );

afterEach(() => {
  resetMswState();
});

/** The record view now loads profile + history in one GET .../history read. */
const seedHistory = (patientId, fullName) => {
  mswState.historyResponses = [
    {
      patient: { patientId, fullName },
      patientId,
      appointments: [],
      prescriptions: [],
    },
  ];
};

describe("PatientDetails â€” the record opened from the directory", () => {
  it("loads the patient identified by the route param", async () => {
    seedHistory(7, "Jane Doe");
    renderRecord(7);

    expect(await screen.findByText("Jane Doe")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("#7")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("links back to the patient directory", async () => {
    seedHistory(7, "Jane Doe");
    renderRecord(7);
    await screen.findByText("Jane Doe");

    const back = screen.getByRole("link", { name: /all patients/i });
    expect(back).toHaveAttribute("href", "/doctor/patient-records");
  });

  it("shows an error with a way back when the record cannot be read", async () => {
    mswState.historyErrorMode = "server-error";

    renderRecord(7);

    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("heading", {
        name: /unable to load this patient record/i,
      }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("link", { name: /back to all patients/i }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("treats a missing record as a failure rather than a blank page", async () => {
    mswState.historyErrorMode = "not-found";

    renderRecord(404);

    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("heading", {
        name: /unable to load this patient record/i,
      }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("link", { name: /back to all patients/i }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\pages\doctor\PatientRecordsPage.test.jsx
========================================
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { Provider } from "react-redux";
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";

import PatientRecordsPage from "../../../src/pages/doctor/PatientRecordsPage";
import PatientDetails from "../../../src/pages/doctor/PatientDetails";
import { setupStore } from "../../../src/app/store";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../../msw/handlers";

/** PAGE_SIZE is 10, so 12 patients are enough to span two pages. */
const makePatients = (count) =>
  Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => ({
    patientId: index + 1,
    fullName: `Patient ${index + 1}`,
  }));

const renderPage = (initialPath = "/doctor/patient-records") => {
  const store = setupStore({
    auth: {
      accessToken: "test-token",
      role: "DOCTOR",
      authStatus: "authenticated",
    },
  });

  return render(
    <Provider store={store}>
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[initialPath]}>
        <Routes>
          <Route
            path="/doctor/patient-records"
            element={<PatientRecordsPage />}
          />
          <Route
            path="/doctor/patient-records/:patientId"
            element={<PatientDetails />}
          />
        </Routes>
      </MemoryRouter>
    </Provider>,
  );
};

afterEach(() => {
  resetMswState();
});

describe("PatientRecordsPage â€” all patients with pagination", () => {
  it("lists the directory and shows only the first page", async () => {
    mswState.patientsResponses = [makePatients(12)];

    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText(/12\s*patients/)).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(screen.getByText("#1")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Patient 10")).toBeInTheDocument();
    // Row 11 belongs to page 2.
    expect(screen.queryByText("Patient 11")).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(screen.getByText(/Page\s*1\s*of\s*2/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("moves to the second page with Next", async () => {
    mswState.patientsResponses = [makePatients(12)];

    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText(/12\s*patients/);

    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Next" }));

    expect(await screen.findByText("Patient 11")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Patient 12")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Patient 1")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Page\s*2\s*of\s*2/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("hides the pager when everything fits on one page", async () => {
    mswState.patientsResponses = [makePatients(3)];

    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText(/3\s*patients/);

    expect(screen.getByText("Patient 3")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Next" }),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("opens the selected patient when View More is clicked", async () => {
    mswState.patientsResponses = [makePatients(12)];

    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText(/12\s*patients/);

    // PatientDetails reads the profile from GET /patients/:id/history.
    mswState.historyResponses = [
      {
        patient: { patientId: 1, fullName: "Jane Doe" },
        patientId: 1,
        appointments: [],
        prescriptions: [],
      },
    ];
    fireEvent.click(screen.getAllByRole("button", { name: "View More" })[0]);

    expect(await screen.findByText("Jane Doe")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("#1")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("link", { name: /All patients/i }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("surfaces a failed directory load with a retry", async () => {
    mswState.patientsErrorMode = "server-error";

    renderPage();

    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: /unable to load patients/i }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("alert")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Retry" })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("retries successfully after the failure clears", async () => {
    mswState.patientsErrorMode = "server-error";

    renderPage();
    await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: /unable to load patients/i });

    mswState.patientsErrorMode = null;
    mswState.patientsResponses = [makePatients(2)];
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Retry" }));

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText(/2\s*patients/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    });
    expect(screen.queryByRole("alert")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\pages\doctor\TodaySchedulePage.test.jsx
========================================
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import {
  act,
  fireEvent,
  render,
  screen,
  waitFor,
} from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { Provider } from "react-redux";
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";

import TodaySchedulePage from "../../../src/pages/doctor/TodaySchedulePage";
import ConsultationPage from "../../../src/pages/doctor/ConsultationPage";
import { setupStore } from "../../../src/app/store";
import { QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS } from "../../../src/hooks/useQueuePolling";
import {
  formatDisplayDate,
  toLocalDateString,
} from "../../../src/utils/dateUtils";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../../msw/handlers";

const scheduled = (overrides = {}) => ({
  appointmentId: 101,
  patientId: 1,
  doctorId: 3,
  appointmentDate: toLocalDateString(),
  timeSlot: "09:00:00",
  status: "SCHEDULED",
  queuePosition: 1,
  ...overrides,
});

const renderPage = () => {
  const testStore = setupStore({
    auth: {
      accessToken: "test-token",
      role: "DOCTOR",
      authStatus: "authenticated",
    },
  });
  return render(
    <Provider store={testStore}>
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={["/doctor/today-schedule"]}>
        <Routes>
          <Route
            path="/doctor/today-schedule"
            element={<TodaySchedulePage />}
          />
          <Route
            path="/doctor/consultation/:appointmentId"
            element={<ConsultationPage />}
          />
        </Routes>
      </MemoryRouter>
    </Provider>,
  );
};

afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
  resetMswState();
});

describe("TodaySchedulePage â€” doctor's consultation queue", () => {
  it("AC1: renders the queue in server order with names, times and statuses", async () => {
    // Names arrive on the appointments response itself â€” the page no longer
    // issues a separate `GET /patients` lookup per row.
    mswState.appointmentsResponses = [
      [
        scheduled({ patientFullName: "Jane Doe" }),
        scheduled({
          appointmentId: 102,
          patientId: 2,
          patientFullName: "Sam Lee",
          timeSlot: "09:30:00",
          queuePosition: 2,
        }),
        scheduled({
          appointmentId: 103,
          patientId: 9,
          status: "COMPLETED",
          queuePosition: 3,
        }),
      ],
    ];
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByRole("table")).toBeInTheDocument();

    const rows = screen.getAllByRole("row").slice(1); // drop header row
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(3);
    // Server-authoritative order preserved, no client re-sorting.
    expect(rows[0]).toHaveTextContent("Jane Doe");
    expect(rows[0]).toHaveTextContent("09:00");
    expect(rows[0]).toHaveTextContent("Scheduled");
    expect(rows[1]).toHaveTextContent("Sam Lee");
    expect(rows[1]).toHaveTextContent("09:30");
    expect(rows[2]).toHaveTextContent("Completed");
    // Missing names degrade to a placeholder rather than invented labels.
    expect(rows[2]).toHaveTextContent("\u2014");

    // The page is scoped to the local calendar date.
    expect(
      screen.getByText(formatDisplayDate(toLocalDateString())),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("heading", { name: /todayâ€™s schedule/i }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("AC1: Start Consultation navigates to the DOC-04 consultation route", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mswState.appointmentsResponses = [[scheduled()]];
    renderPage();

    await screen.findByRole("table");
    await user.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Start Consultation" }),
    );

    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("heading", {
        name: "Consultation Room",
      }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("AC3: shows the exact empty-state text when the date has no appointments", async () => {
    mswState.appointmentsResponses = [[]];
    renderPage();

    expect(
      await screen.findByText(
        "No appointments scheduled for the selected date.",
      ),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("table")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("re-fetches when the doctor picks another date (server-scoped read)", async () => {
    mswState.appointmentsResponses = [
      [{ ...scheduled(), patientFullName: "Jane Doe" }], // today
      // GET /appointments is scoped to the requested ?date, so this response
      // only carries records for the date that was just picked.
      [
        {
          ...scheduled({ appointmentDate: "2020-05-05" }),
          patientFullName: "Sam Lee",
        },
      ],
    ];
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByRole("table");

    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("Date"), {
      target: { value: "2020-05-05" },
    });

    expect(await screen.findByText("Sam Lee")).toBeInTheDocument();
    // One request per date, each carrying the selected date.
    expect(mswState.appointmentsHits).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(
      mswState.appointmentsHits.map((hit) =>
        new URL(hit.url).searchParams.get("date"),
      ),
    ).toEqual([toLocalDateString(), "2020-05-05"]);
    expect(screen.queryByText("Jane Doe")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByRole("row")).toHaveLength(2); // header + one row
  });

  it("AC2: surfaces load failures with a working retry", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mswState.appointmentsErrorMode = "server-error";
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(
      "Unable to load the schedule",
    );

    mswState.appointmentsErrorMode = null;
    mswState.appointmentsResponses = [[scheduled()]];
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Retry" }));

    expect(await screen.findByRole("table")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("alert")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("never offers queue mutations or admin-only actions on the schedule page", async () => {
    mswState.appointmentsResponses = [[scheduled()]];
    renderPage();

    await screen.findByRole("table");
    expect(
      screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /check\s*in/i }),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /no\s*show/i }),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    // Completion happens on the consultation page only.
    expect(
      screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /complete/i }),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    // A scheduled row offers only Start Consultation; plus the header Refresh.
    expect(screen.getAllByRole("button")).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("polls the queue about every 2 seconds", async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers({ shouldAdvanceTime: true });
    mswState.appointmentsResponses = [[scheduled()], [scheduled()]];
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByRole("table")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(mswState.appointmentsHits).toHaveLength(1);

    act(() => {
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS);
    });
    await waitFor(() => expect(mswState.appointmentsHits).toHaveLength(2));

    act(() => {
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS);
    });
    // Third poll has no queued response left and falls back to `[]`;
    // the request itself proves the cadence continues.
    await waitFor(() => expect(mswState.appointmentsHits).toHaveLength(3));
  });

  it("shows a loading spinner on the first load only", async () => {
    mswState.appointmentsErrorMode = "delay";
    mswState.appointmentsResponses = [[scheduled()]];
    renderPage();

    expect(
      screen.getByRole("status", { name: "Loading appointment queue" }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await screen.findByRole("table")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";

import ForgotPasswordPage from "../../../src/pages/public/ForgotPasswordPage";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../../msw/handlers";

const renderAt = (location = "/forgot-password") =>
  render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[location]}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/forgot-password" element={<ForgotPasswordPage />} />
        <Route
          path="/login"
          element={<div data-testid="login-page">Login Page</div>}
        />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );

afterEach(() => {
  resetMswState();
  vi.clearAllMocks();
});

describe("ForgotPasswordPage", () => {
  it("renders the forgot password form correctly", () => {
    renderAt();
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("heading", { name: /forgot password\?/i }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/registered email/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /send reset instructions/i }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("validates empty email input", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderAt();

    await user.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /send reset instructions/i }),
    );

    expect(screen.getByText(/this field is required/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(mswState.forgotPasswordHits.length).toBe(0);
  });

  it("validates invalid email format", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderAt();

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText(/registered email/i), "notanemail");
    await user.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /send reset instructions/i }),
    );

    expect(
      screen.getByText(/please enter a valid email address/i),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(mswState.forgotPasswordHits.length).toBe(0);
  });

  it("successfully submits request and shows confirmation state", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderAt();

    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/registered email/i),
      "doctor@onecare.com",
    );
    await user.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /send reset instructions/i }),
    );

    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(
        screen.getByText(
          "If an account exists for this email, a reset link has been sent.",
        ),
      ).toBeInTheDocument(),
    );

    expect(mswState.forgotPasswordHits).toEqual([
      { email: "doctor@onecare.com" },
    ]);
    expect(screen.queryByText(/doctor@onecare.com/i)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("link", { name: /return to login/i }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("uses the same neutral confirmation for an address with no matching account", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderAt();
    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/registered email/i),
      "unknown@example.com",
    );
    await user.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /send reset instructions/i }),
    );
    expect(
      await screen.findByText(
        "If an account exists for this email, a reset link has been sent.",
      ),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/unknown@example.com/i)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("displays rate limiting error message on 429 status", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mswState.forgotPasswordErrorMode = "rate-limit";
    renderAt();

    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/registered email/i),
      "doctor@onecare.com",
    );
    await user.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /send reset instructions/i }),
    );

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(/too many attempts/i);
    });
  });

  it("uses a generic message for network errors", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mswState.forgotPasswordErrorMode = "network-error";
    renderAt();
    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/registered email/i),
      "any@example.com",
    );
    await user.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /send reset instructions/i }),
    );
    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(
      "Something went wrong. Please try again shortly.",
    );
    expect(
      screen.queryByText(/internal server error/i),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("displays server error message on 500 status", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mswState.forgotPasswordErrorMode = "server-error";
    renderAt();

    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/registered email/i),
      "doctor@onecare.com",
    );
    await user.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", { name: /send reset instructions/i }),
    );

    expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(
      "Something went wrong. Please try again shortly.",
    );
    expect(
      screen.queryByText(/internal server error/i),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\pages\public\LoginPage.test.jsx

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from "react-router-dom";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";

import LoginPage from "../../../src/pages/public/LoginPage";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../../msw/handlers";

const renderAt = (location = "/login") =>
  render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[location]}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
        <Route
          path="/admin/dashboard"
          element={<div data-testid="admin-dashboard">Admin Dashboard</div>}
        />
        <Route
          path="/doctor/dashboard"
          element={<div data-testid="doctor-dashboard">Doctor Dashboard</div>}
        />
        <Route
          path="/pharmacist/dashboard"
          element={
            <div data-testid="pharmacist-dashboard">Pharmacist Dashboard</div>
          }
        />
        <Route
          path="/super-admin/dashboard"
          element={
            <div data-testid="super-admin-dashboard">Super Admin Dashboard</div>
          }
        />
        <Route path="/forgot-password" element={<div>Forgot Password</div>} />
        <Route
          path="/doctor/patients"
          element={<div data-testid="patients">Patients</div>}
        />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );

const fillForm = async (username, password) => {
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText(/^username/i), username);
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText(/^password/i), password);
};

const submitForm = async () => {
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /sign in securely/i }));
};

afterEach(() => {
  resetMswState();
  vi.clearAllMocks();
});

describe("LoginPage", () => {
  describe("AC1: Happy Path - valid credentials redirect to role dashboard", () => {
    const roles = [
      { role: "ADMIN", testId: "admin-dashboard" },
      { role: "DOCTOR", testId: "doctor-dashboard" },
      { role: "PHARMACIST", testId: "pharmacist-dashboard" },
      { role: "SUPER_ADMIN", testId: "super-admin-dashboard" },
    ];

    roles.forEach(({ role, testId }) => {
      it(`redirects ${role} to dashboard`, async () => {
        mswState.loginValid = true;
        mswState.loginRole = role;

        renderAt("/login");
        await fillForm("test-user", "password123");
        await submitForm();

        await waitFor(() => {
          expect(screen.getByTestId(testId)).toBeInTheDocument();
        });
      });
    });

    it("redirects to preserved location when from state exists", async () => {
      mswState.loginValid = true;
      mswState.loginRole = "DOCTOR";

      render(
        <MemoryRouter
          initialEntries={[
            {
              pathname: "/login",
              state: { from: "/doctor/patients" },
            },
          ]}
        >
          <Routes>
            <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
            <Route
              path="/doctor/dashboard"
              element={
                <div data-testid="doctor-dashboard">Doctor Dashboard</div>
              }
            />
            <Route
              path="/doctor/patients"
              element={<div data-testid="patients">Patients</div>}
            />
          </Routes>
        </MemoryRouter>,
      );

      await fillForm("test-user", "password123");
      await submitForm();

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByTestId("patients")).toBeInTheDocument();
      });
    });
  });

  describe("AC2: Error Handling - invalid credentials", () => {
    it("shows 'Invalid username or password.' with no hint which was wrong", async () => {
      mswState.loginValid = false;
      mswState.loginErrorMode = null;

      renderAt("/login");
      await fillForm("wrong-user", "wrong-pass");
      await submitForm();

      await waitFor(() => {
        const alerts = screen.getAllByRole("alert");
        const apiError = alerts.find((a) =>
          a.textContent.includes("Invalid username or password"),
        );
        expect(apiError).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      expect(screen.getByLabelText(/^password/i)).toHaveValue("");
    });

    it("clears password field on invalid credentials", async () => {
      mswState.loginValid = false;
      mswState.loginErrorMode = null;

      renderAt("/login");
      await fillForm("wrong-user", "wrong-pass");
      await submitForm();

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByLabelText(/^password/i)).toHaveValue("");
      });
    });
  });

  describe("AC3: Edge Case - locked account", () => {
    it("shows lockout message with three routes to regain access", async () => {
      mswState.loginValid = false;
      mswState.loginErrorMode = "lockout";

      renderAt("/login");
      await fillForm("locked-user", "password123");
      await submitForm();

      await waitFor(() => {
        const alerts = screen.getAllByRole("alert");
        const lockoutError = alerts.find((a) =>
          a.textContent.includes("Account locked"),
        );
        expect(lockoutError).toBeInTheDocument();
        expect(lockoutError).toHaveTextContent("Super Admin");
        expect(lockoutError).toHaveTextContent("Forgot Password");
      });

      expect(screen.getByLabelText(/^password/i)).toHaveValue("");
    });
  });

  describe("AC4: Edge Case - rate limit (429)", () => {
    it("shows 'too many attempts' message and clears password", async () => {
      mswState.loginValid = false;
      mswState.loginErrorMode = "rate-limit";

      renderAt("/login");
      await fillForm("test-user", "password123");
      await submitForm();

      await waitFor(() => {
        const alerts = screen.getAllByRole("alert");
        const rateLimitError = alerts.find((a) =>
          a.textContent.includes("Too many attempts"),
        );
        expect(rateLimitError).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      expect(screen.getByLabelText(/^password/i)).toHaveValue("");
    });
  });

  describe("UI Interactions", () => {
    it("toggles password visibility with ARIA attributes", async () => {
      renderAt("/login");

      const passwordInput = screen.getByLabelText(/^password/i);
      expect(passwordInput).toHaveAttribute("type", "password");

      const toggleButton = screen.getByRole("button", {
        name: /show password/i,
      });
      await userEvent.click(toggleButton);

      expect(passwordInput).toHaveAttribute("type", "text");
      expect(toggleButton).toHaveAttribute("aria-label", "Hide password");
      expect(toggleButton).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");

      await userEvent.click(toggleButton);

      expect(passwordInput).toHaveAttribute("type", "password");
      expect(toggleButton).toHaveAttribute("aria-label", "Show password");
      expect(toggleButton).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
    });

    it("has forgot password link to /forgot-password", async () => {
      renderAt("/login");

      const link = screen.getByRole("link", { name: /forgot password/i });
      expect(link).toHaveAttribute("href", "/forgot-password");
    });

    it("shows session expired message when reason=session-expired", () => {
      renderAt("/login?reason=session-expired");

      expect(screen.getByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(
        "Your session has expired. Please sign in again.",
      );
    });

    it("shows a reset success message from navigation state and consumes it", async () => {
      const user = userEvent.setup();
      render(
        <MemoryRouter
          initialEntries={[
            {
              pathname: "/login",
              state: {
                passwordResetSuccess:
                  "Your password has been reset. You can now sign in.",
              },
            },
          ]}
        >
          <Routes>
            <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
          </Routes>
        </MemoryRouter>,
      );
      expect(await screen.findByRole("status")).toHaveTextContent(
        "Your password has been reset.",
      );
      await user.type(screen.getByLabelText(/^username/i), "user");
      await user.type(screen.getByLabelText(/^password/i), "password");
      await submitForm();
      await waitFor(() =>
        expect(screen.queryByRole("status")).not.toBeInTheDocument(),
      );
    });

    it("validates required fields on empty submit", () => {
      renderAt("/login");

      fireEvent.click(
        screen.getByRole("button", { name: /sign in securely/i }),
      );

      expect(screen.getAllByText("This field is required.")).toHaveLength(2);
    });

    it("renders form panel with OneCare branding", () => {
      renderAt("/login");

      expect(screen.getByText("OneCare Family Clinic")).toBeInTheDocument();
      expect(screen.getByText("Welcome back")).toBeInTheDocument();
      expect(screen.getByText("Sign in to OneCare")).toBeInTheDocument();
    });
  });
});

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";

import ResetPasswordPage from "../../../src/pages/public/ResetPasswordPage";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../../msw/handlers";

const LocationDisplay = () => {
  const location = useLocation();
  return (
    <output data-testid="location">{`${location.pathname}${location.search}${location.hash}`}</output>
  );
};

const renderAt = (location = "/reset-password", state = null) =>
  render(
    <MemoryRouter
      initialEntries={[
        {
          pathname: location.split(/[?#]/)[0],
          search: location.includes("?")
            ? `?${location.split("?")[1].split("#")[0]}`
            : "",
          hash: location.includes("#") ? `#${location.split("#").at(-1)}` : "",
          state,
        },
      ]}
    >
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/reset-password" element={<ResetPasswordPage />} />
        <Route
          path="/login"
          element={<div data-testid="login-page">Login Page</div>}
        />
        <Route
          path="/forgot-password"
          element={<div data-testid="forgot-page">Forgot Page</div>}
        />
      </Routes>
      <LocationDisplay />
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );

afterEach(() => {
  resetMswState();
  vi.clearAllMocks();
});

describe("ResetPasswordPage", () => {
  it("renders the reset password form with manual token field when no token in URL", () => {
    renderAt("/reset-password");
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("heading", { name: /reset your password/i }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/reset token/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/^new password/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/confirm new password/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("hides manual token input when token is present in URL query", () => {
    renderAt("/reset-password?token=secret-token-123");
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText(/reset token/i)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/^new password/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/confirm new password/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("removes only the token from the URL and still submits the captured token", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderAt("/reset-password?keep=yes&token=still-secret#form");
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(screen.getByTestId("location")).toHaveTextContent(
        "/reset-password?keep=yes#form",
      ),
    );
    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/^new password/i),
      "BrandNewPass888!",
    );
    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/confirm new password/i),
      "BrandNewPass888!",
    );
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /reset password/i }));
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(mswState.resetPasswordHits).toEqual([
        { token: "still-secret", newPassword: "BrandNewPass888!" },
      ]),
    );
  });

  it("validates empty fields", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderAt("/reset-password");

    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /reset password/i }));

    const requiredErrors = screen.getAllByText(/this field is required/i);
    expect(requiredErrors.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
  });

  it("validates password length requirements (< 8 characters)", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderAt("/reset-password?token=valid-token");

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText(/^new password/i), "short");
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText(/confirm new password/i), "short");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /reset password/i }));

    expect(
      screen.getByText(/password must be at least 8 characters/i),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(mswState.resetPasswordHits.length).toBe(0);
  });

  it("validates password mismatch", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderAt("/reset-password?token=valid-token");

    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/^new password/i),
      "SecurePassword123!",
    );
    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/confirm new password/i),
      "Different123!",
    );
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /reset password/i }));

    expect(screen.getByText(/passwords do not match/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(mswState.resetPasswordHits.length).toBe(0);
  });

  it("successfully resets password with valid token and password matching", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderAt("/reset-password?token=token-abc-123");

    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/^new password/i),
      "BrandNewPass888!",
    );
    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/confirm new password/i),
      "BrandNewPass888!",
    );
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /reset password/i }));

    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(screen.getByTestId("login-page")).toBeInTheDocument(),
    );

    expect(mswState.resetPasswordHits).toEqual([
      {
        token: "token-abc-123",
        newPassword: "BrandNewPass888!",
      },
    ]);
  });

  it("shows error when token is invalid or expired", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mswState.resetPasswordErrorMode = "invalid-token";
    renderAt("/reset-password?token=expired-token");

    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/^new password/i),
      "BrandNewPass888!",
    );
    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/confirm new password/i),
      "BrandNewPass888!",
    );
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /reset password/i }));

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(
        screen.getByRole("heading", { name: /this reset link is invalid/i }),
      ).toBeInTheDocument();
      expect(
        screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /reset password/i }),
      ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    });
  });

  it("links from the invalid-link screen to forgot password and login", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mswState.resetPasswordErrorMode = "invalid-token";
    renderAt("/reset-password?token=expired");
    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/^new password/i),
      "BrandNewPass888!",
    );
    await user.type(
      screen.getByLabelText(/confirm new password/i),
      "BrandNewPass888!",
    );
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /reset password/i }));
    await user.click(
      await screen.findByRole("link", { name: /request a new link/i }),
    );
    expect(screen.getByTestId("forgot-page")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("toggles password visibility", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderAt("/reset-password?token=token-123");

    const passwordInput = screen.getByLabelText(/^new password/i);
    expect(passwordInput).toHaveAttribute("type", "password");

    const toggleBtn = screen.getByRole("button", { name: /show password/i });
    await user.click(toggleBtn);
    expect(passwordInput).toHaveAttribute("type", "text");

    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /hide password/i }));
    expect(passwordInput).toHaveAttribute("type", "password");
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\pages\styleguide\ThemeReferencePage.test.jsx
========================================
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { render } from "@testing-library/react";
import postcss from "postcss";
import path from "node:path";
import tailwindcss from "tailwindcss";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import config from "../../../tailwind.config.js";
import { colors, contrastPairs, fontSize } from "../../../src/theme/tokens.js";
import {
  evaluateContrastPairs,
  hexToRgbTriplet,
} from "../../../src/utils/contrast.js";
import ThemeReferencePage from "../../../src/pages/styleguide/ThemeReferencePage.jsx";

const INDEX_CSS = path.resolve(process.cwd(), "src/index.css");
const SOURCE_CSS = readFileSync(INDEX_CSS, "utf8");

const buildCss = async (overrides = {}) => {
  const result = await postcss([
    tailwindcss({ ...config, ...overrides }),
  ]).process(SOURCE_CSS, {
    from: INDEX_CSS,
  });
  return result.css;
};

const collectUsedClasses = (container) => {
  const classes = new Set();
  for (const element of container.querySelectorAll("*")) {
    for (const name of element.classList) {
      classes.add(name);
    }
  }
  return [...classes];
};

describe("Colour / type reference page", () => {
  it("renders the palette, type scale and contrast results", () => {
    const { container } = render(<ThemeReferencePage />);

    expect(screenHeading(container, "Theme reference")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(container.textContent).toContain("primary-600");
    expect(container.textContent).toContain("text-base");
    expect(container.textContent).toContain("Status indicators");

    for (const pair of contrastPairs) {
      expect(container.textContent).toContain(pair.id);
    }
  });

  it("only uses token utilities â€” no raw colours in the markup", () => {
    const source = readFileSync(
      path.resolve(
        process.cwd(),
        "src/pages/styleguide/ThemeReferencePage.jsx",
      ),
      "utf8",
    );

    expect(source).not.toMatch(/#[0-9a-f]{3,8}\b/i);
  });

  it("shows every declared pair as passing on the page", () => {
    const report = evaluateContrastPairs(contrastPairs, colors);

    expect(report.passed).toBe(true);
    expect(report.results.every((result) => result.ratio >= 4.5)).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("AC3 â€” integration: theme token change rebuilds everywhere", () => {
  it("builds CSS containing the declared token values from the page markup", async () => {
    const { container } = render(<ThemeReferencePage />);
    const css = await buildCss();

    expect(collectUsedClasses(container).length).toBeGreaterThan(10);
    expect(css).toContain(`rgb(${hexToRgbTriplet(colors.primary[600])}`); // primary-600
    expect(css).toContain(`rgb(${hexToRgbTriplet(colors.primary[700])}`); // primary-700
    expect(css).toContain(`font-size: ${fontSize.base[0]}`); // 18px base type
    expect(css).toContain(".rounded-2xl");
  });

  it("reflects a token change in the rebuilt CSS with no component edits", async () => {
    const { container } = render(<ThemeReferencePage />);
    const usedClasses = collectUsedClasses(container);
    const originalTriplet = hexToRgbTriplet(colors.primary[600]);

    const before = await buildCss();
    expect(ruleFor(before, ".bg-primary-600")).toContain(
      `rgb(${originalTriplet}`,
    );

    const changedColors = structuredClone(colors);
    changedColors.primary[600] = colors.secondary[600];
    const replacementTriplet = hexToRgbTriplet(changedColors.primary[600]);

    const after = await buildCss({
      theme: {
        ...config.theme,
        extend: { ...config.theme.extend, colors: changedColors },
      },
    });

    // Scoped to the token's own rule: other selectors (e.g. Tailwind's
    // default blue-600) may coincidentally share the same RGB value.
    const changedRule = ruleFor(after, ".bg-primary-600");
    expect(changedRule).toContain(`rgb(${replacementTriplet}`);
    expect(changedRule).not.toContain(`rgb(${originalTriplet}`);

    // The component markup is untouched â€” the same token classes are still emitted.
    expect(usedClasses).toContain("bg-primary-600");
    expect(after).toContain(".bg-primary-600");
    expect(after).toContain(`font-size: ${fontSize.base[0]}`);
  });
});

function ruleFor(css, selector) {
  const escaped = selector.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
  const match = new RegExp(`${escaped}\\s*\\{[^}]*\\}`).exec(css);
  return match ? match[0] : "";
}

function screenHeading(container, text) {
  return [...container.querySelectorAll("h1")].find(
    (heading) => heading.textContent === text,
  );
}

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import {
  fireEvent,
  render,
  screen,
  waitFor,
  cleanup,
} from "@testing-library/react";
import { Provider } from "react-redux";
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";

import { store } from "../../src/app/store";
import { clearSession, setSession } from "../../src/features/auth/authSlice";
import { ROLES } from "../../src/constants/roles";
import AppRoutes from "../../src/routes/AppRoutes";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../msw/handlers";

const LocationDisplay = () => {
  const location = useLocation();

  return <div data-testid="location">{location.pathname}</div>;
};

const seed = (payload) => {
  store.dispatch(setSession(payload));
};

const renderAppAt = (initialPath) =>
  render(
    <Provider store={store}>
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[initialPath]}>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="*" element={<AppRoutes />} />
        </Routes>
        <LocationDisplay />
      </MemoryRouter>
    </Provider>,
  );

const expectLocation = (path) => {
  expect(screen.getByTestId("location")).toHaveTextContent(path);
};

beforeEach(() => {
  store.dispatch(clearSession());
  resetMswState();

  // Explicitly establish default mock state for every test.
  mswState.loginValid = true;
});

afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  resetMswState();
});

describe("AppRoutes â€” CFG-03 role-based routing (integration)", () => {
  it("AC1: a Doctor opening /doctor/dashboard sees the doctor dashboard", async () => {
    seed({
      accessToken: "a",
      role: ROLES.DOCTOR,
    });

    renderAppAt("/doctor/dashboard");

    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("heading", {
        name: /today.s appointments/i,
      }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expectLocation("/doctor/dashboard");
  });

  it("AC1: each role lands on its own dashboard", async () => {
    const cases = [
      {
        role: ROLES.ADMIN,
        path: "/admin/dashboard",
        heading: /AdminDashboardPage/i,
      },
      {
        role: ROLES.DOCTOR,
        path: "/doctor/dashboard",
        heading: /today.s appointments/i,
      },
      {
        role: ROLES.PHARMACIST,
        path: "/pharmacist/dashboard",
        heading: /PharmacistDashboardPage/i,
      },
      {
        role: ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN,
        path: "/super-admin/dashboard",
        heading: /SuperAdminDashboardPage/i,
      },
    ];

    for (const { role, path, heading } of cases) {
      store.dispatch(clearSession());
      seed({
        accessToken: "a",
        role,
      });

      const { unmount } = renderAppAt(path);

      try {
        expect(
          await screen.findByRole("heading", {
            name: heading,
          }),
        ).toBeInTheDocument();

        expectLocation(path);
      } finally {
        unmount();
      }
    }
  });

  it("AC2: a Pharmacist opening an admin route is redirected to their dashboard", async () => {
    seed({
      accessToken: "a",
      role: ROLES.PHARMACIST,
    });

    renderAppAt("/admin/dashboard");

    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("heading", {
        name: /PharmacistDashboardPage/i,
      }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expectLocation("/pharmacist/dashboard");
    });
  });

  it("AC3: an unauthenticated user opening a protected route is sent to /login", async () => {
    renderAppAt("/admin/dashboard");

    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("heading", {
        name: /sign in to onecare/i,
      }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expectLocation("/login");
    });
  });

  it("AC4: the shared Queue screen renders for every allowed role", async () => {
    const allowed = [ROLES.ADMIN, ROLES.DOCTOR, ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN];

    for (const role of allowed) {
      store.dispatch(clearSession());
      seed({
        accessToken: "a",
        role,
      });

      const { unmount } = renderAppAt("/queue");

      try {
        // Replace this with the actual visible queue heading
        // from your Queue page if its text differs.
        expect(
          await screen.findByRole("heading", {
            name: /queue/i,
          }),
        ).toBeInTheDocument();

        expectLocation("/queue");
      } finally {
        unmount();
      }
    }
  });

  it("AC4: a Pharmacist is redirected away from the shared Queue screen", async () => {
    seed({
      accessToken: "a",
      role: ROLES.PHARMACIST,
    });

    renderAppAt("/queue");

    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("heading", {
        name: /PharmacistDashboardPage/i,
      }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expectLocation("/pharmacist/dashboard");
    });
  });

  it("public routes stay reachable without a session", () => {
    renderAppAt("/home");

    expectLocation("/home");
  });

  it("the site root redirects to the public home page", async () => {
    renderAppAt("/");

    await waitFor(() => {
      expectLocation("/home");
    });
  });

  it("an unknown URL renders the public 404 page instead of a blank screen", async () => {
    renderAppAt("/no-such-page");

    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("heading", {
        name: /page not found/i,
      }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expectLocation("/no-such-page");
  });

  const signIn = async (username) => {
    await screen.findByRole("heading", {
      name: /sign in to onecare/i,
    });

    const usernameInput = screen.getByRole("textbox", {
      name: /username/i,
    });

    const passwordInput = screen.getByLabelText(/^password/i);

    fireEvent.change(usernameInput, {
      target: { value: username },
    });

    fireEvent.change(passwordInput, {
      target: { value: "secret" },
    });

    fireEvent.click(
      screen.getByRole("button", {
        name: /sign in securely/i,
      }),
    );
  };

  it("AC1: a Doctor signs in and lands on /doctor/dashboard", async () => {
    mswState.loginRole = ROLES.DOCTOR;

    renderAppAt("/login");

    await signIn("doctor@onecare.test");

    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("heading", {
        name: /today.s appointments/i,
      }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expectLocation("/doctor/dashboard");
    });
  });

  it("AC1: after login the user returns to the originally requested protected route", async () => {
    mswState.loginRole = ROLES.ADMIN;

    renderAppAt("/queue");

    await screen.findByRole("heading", {
      name: /sign in to onecare/i,
    });

    await signIn("admin@onecare.test");

    expect(
      await screen.findByRole("heading", {
        name: /queue/i,
      }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expectLocation("/queue");
    });
  });

  it("rejected credentials keep the visitor on /login with an error", async () => {
    mswState.loginValid = false;

    renderAppAt("/login");

    await signIn("doctor@onecare.test");

    expect(
      await screen.findByText("Invalid username or password."),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    expectLocation("/login");
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\routes\navigation.test.js

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";

import { ROLES } from "../../src/constants/roles";
import { ROUTE_ACCESS } from "../../src/constants/routeAccess";
import { NAV_ITEMS } from "../../src/constants/navigation";

/** Number of navigation steps a path represents (segment count). */
const pathDepth = (path) => path.split("/").filter(Boolean).length;

const MAX_NAV_STEPS = 3;

describe("AC6 â€” common tasks within â‰¤ 3 navigation steps per role", () => {
  it("defines a menu for every staff role", () => {
    for (const role of Object.values(ROLES)) {
      expect(NAV_ITEMS[role]).toBeDefined();
      expect(NAV_ITEMS[role].length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });

  it("keeps every menu entry within the step budget", () => {
    for (const [role, items] of Object.entries(NAV_ITEMS)) {
      for (const item of items) {
        expect(
          pathDepth(item.to),
          `${role} menu item "${item.id}" (${item.to}) exceeds ${MAX_NAV_STEPS} steps`,
        ).toBeLessThanOrEqual(MAX_NAV_STEPS);
      }
    }
  });

  it("only links menu entries the role is allowed to open", () => {
    for (const [role, items] of Object.entries(NAV_ITEMS)) {
      for (const item of items) {
        const [, group] = item.to.split("/");
        const allowedRoles = ROUTE_ACCESS[group];

        expect(
          allowedRoles,
          `${role} menu item "${item.id}" points at unknown route group "${group}"`,
        ).toBeDefined();
        expect(
          allowedRoles,
          `${role} menu item "${item.id}" is not in ROUTE_ACCESS.${group}`,
        ).toContain(role);
      }
    }
  });

  it("keeps each role's dashboard exactly one step from its group root", () => {
    for (const [role, items] of Object.entries(NAV_ITEMS)) {
      const dashboard = items.find((item) => item.id === "dashboard");
      expect(dashboard, `${role} has no dashboard entry`).toBeDefined();
      expect(pathDepth(dashboard.to)).toBe(2);
    }
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\routes\RequireAuth.test.jsx

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { Provider } from "react-redux";
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";

import { store } from "../../src/app/store";
import { clearSession, setSession } from "../../src/features/auth/authSlice";
import { ROLES } from "../../src/constants/roles";
import { ROUTE_ACCESS } from "../../src/constants/routeAccess";
import RequireAuth from "../../src/routes/RequireAuth";

const LocationDisplay = () => {
  const location = useLocation();
  return (
    <div data-testid="location">
      {location.pathname}
      {location.state?.from ? `|from:${location.state.from}` : ""}
    </div>
  );
};

const seed = (payload) => store.dispatch(setSession(payload));

const renderGuardedRoute = (
  initialPath,
  allowedRoles,
  pageTestId = "protected-page",
) =>
  render(
    <Provider store={store}>
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[initialPath]}>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/login" element={<div data-testid="login-page" />} />
          <Route
            path="/admin/dashboard"
            element={<div data-testid="admin-page" />}
          />
          <Route
            path="/pharmacist/dashboard"
            element={<div data-testid="pharmacist-page" />}
          />
          <Route element={<RequireAuth allowedRoles={allowedRoles} />}>
            <Route
              path="/protected"
              element={<div data-testid={pageTestId} />}
            />
          </Route>
        </Routes>
        <LocationDisplay />
      </MemoryRouter>
    </Provider>,
  );

const expectLocation = (path) =>
  expect(screen.getByTestId("location")).toHaveTextContent(path);

beforeEach(() => {
  store.dispatch(clearSession());
});

describe("RequireAuth â€” CFG-03 route guard", () => {
  it("AC1: renders the guarded route for an allowed role", () => {
    seed({ accessToken: "a", role: ROLES.DOCTOR });

    renderGuardedRoute("/protected", [ROLES.DOCTOR]);

    expect(screen.getByTestId("protected-page")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expectLocation("/protected");
  });

  it("AC3: redirects an unauthenticated user to /login", () => {
    renderGuardedRoute("/protected", [ROLES.DOCTOR]);

    expect(screen.getByTestId("login-page")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByTestId("protected-page")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expectLocation("/login");
  });

  it("AC3: keeps the attempted path in location state for post-login redirect", () => {
    renderGuardedRoute("/protected?tab=1", [ROLES.DOCTOR]);

    expect(screen.getByTestId("location")).toHaveTextContent(
      "from:/protected?tab=1",
    );
  });

  it("AC2: redirects a wrong-role user to their own dashboard", () => {
    seed({ accessToken: "a", role: ROLES.PHARMACIST });

    renderGuardedRoute("/protected", [ROLES.ADMIN]);

    expect(screen.getByTestId("pharmacist-page")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByTestId("protected-page")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expectLocation("/pharmacist/dashboard");
  });

  it("AC4: renders a shared screen for each allowed role", () => {
    const sharedRoles = ROUTE_ACCESS.queue;
    expect(sharedRoles).toContain(ROLES.ADMIN);
    expect(sharedRoles).toContain(ROLES.DOCTOR);
    expect(sharedRoles).toContain(ROLES.SUPER_ADMIN);

    for (const role of sharedRoles) {
      store.dispatch(clearSession());
      seed({ accessToken: "a", role });

      const { unmount } = renderGuardedRoute("/protected", sharedRoles);

      expect(screen.getByTestId("protected-page")).toBeInTheDocument();
      expectLocation("/protected");
      unmount();
    }
  });

  it("AC4: redirects a non-allowed role away from a shared screen", () => {
    seed({ accessToken: "a", role: ROLES.PHARMACIST });

    renderGuardedRoute("/protected", ROUTE_ACCESS.queue);

    expect(screen.getByTestId("pharmacist-page")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expectLocation("/pharmacist/dashboard");
  });

  it("treats a missing allowedRoles list as open to any authenticated role", () => {
    seed({ accessToken: "a", role: ROLES.PHARMACIST });

    renderGuardedRoute("/protected", undefined);

    expect(screen.getByTestId("protected-page")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("redirects an authenticated user with no known role to /login", () => {
    seed({ accessToken: "a" });

    renderGuardedRoute("/protected", [ROLES.ADMIN]);

    expect(screen.getByTestId("login-page")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expectLocation("/login");
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from "vitest";
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import apiClient, {
  __resetAuthState,
  setNavigator,
  startTokenRefreshScheduler,
} from "../../src/services/apiClient";
import LoginPage from "../../src/pages/public/LoginPage";
import { clearTokens, getAccessToken, setTokens } from "../helpers/session";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../msw/handlers";

function ProtectedProbe({ path = "/patients/1" }) {
  const [state, setState] = useState({ phase: "loading" });

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    apiClient
      .get(path)
      .then((response) => {
        if (!cancelled) setState({ phase: "ok", data: response.data.data });
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setState({
            phase: "error",
            message: error.userMessage,
            status: error.status,
          });
        }
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [path]);

  if (state.phase === "loading") return <p>Loadingâ€¦</p>;
  if (state.phase === "error") return <p role="alert">{state.message}</p>;
  return <p data-testid="result">{state.data?.name ?? "loaded"}</p>;
}

function Harness({ path }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  useEffect(() => {
    setNavigator(navigate);
    return () => setNavigator(undefined);
  }, [navigate]);

  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
      <Route path="/protected" element={<ProtectedProbe path={path} />} />
      <Route path="*" element={<ProtectedProbe path={path} />} />
    </Routes>
  );
}

const renderApp = (path = "/patients/1") =>
  render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={["/protected"]}>
      <Harness path={path} />
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );

const seedSession = () =>
  setTokens({
    accessToken: "access-1",
    refreshToken: "refresh-1",
    expiresIn: 1800,
  });

beforeEach(() => {
  __resetAuthState();
  clearTokens();
  resetMswState();
});

afterEach(() => {
  __resetAuthState();
  clearTokens();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe("AC1 â€” MSW integration", () => {
  it("attaches the Bearer token once and renders the payload", async () => {
    seedSession();

    renderApp("/patients/1");

    expect(await screen.findByTestId("result")).toHaveTextContent("Jane Doe");
    expect(mswState.refreshCount).toBe(0);
    expect(mswState.protectedHits).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(mswState.protectedHits[0].authorization).toBe("Bearer access-1");
  });
});

describe("AC2 â€” MSW negative test (concurrent 401s)", () => {
  it("refreshes exactly once and retries every call", async () => {
    seedSession();
    mswState.tokenValid = false;

    const results = await Promise.all([
      apiClient.get("/patients/1"),
      apiClient.get("/patients/2"),
      apiClient.get("/patients/3"),
    ]);

    expect(results.map((response) => response.status)).toEqual([200, 200, 200]);
    expect(mswState.refreshCount).toBe(1);
    expect(mswState.refreshTokens).toEqual(["refresh-1"]);
    expect(mswState.protectedHits).toHaveLength(6);
    const retried = mswState.protectedHits.filter(
      (hit) => hit.authorization === "Bearer new-access",
    );
    expect(retried).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("new-access");
  });
});

describe("AC3 â€” MSW integration (failed refresh)", () => {
  it("redirects to /login with the session expired message", async () => {
    seedSession();
    mswState.tokenValid = false;
    mswState.refreshValid = false;

    renderApp("/patients/1");

    expect(
      await screen.findByText(
        "Your session has expired. Please sign in again.",
      ),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(mswState.refreshCount).toBe(1);
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByTestId("result")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe("AC4 â€” MSW integration (proactive refresh)", () => {
  it("refreshes once before expiry while active, never while idle", async () => {
    seedSession();
    renderApp("/patients/1");
    await screen.findByTestId("result");

    const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
    let active = true;
    const base = Date.now();
    let clock = base;
    const stop = startTokenRefreshScheduler({
      isUserActive: () => active,
      checkIntervalMs: 5,
      now: () => clock,
    });

    clock = base + 10 * 60 * 1000;
    await sleep(50);
    expect(mswState.refreshCount).toBe(0);

    clock = base + 26 * 60 * 1000;
    await waitFor(() => expect(mswState.refreshCount).toBe(1));
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("new-access");

    active = false;
    clock = base + 7000 * 1000;
    await sleep(50);
    expect(mswState.refreshCount).toBe(1);
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("new-access");

    stop();
  });
});

describe("AC5 â€” MSW integration (403 / 429 / 500)", () => {
  it.each([
    ["/forbidden", "You do not have permission to view this resource"],
    ["/rate-limited", "Too many requests. Please slow down."],
    ["/server-error", "Internal server error"],
  ])(
    "surfaces the message for %s without logging out",
    async (path, message) => {
      seedSession();

      renderApp(path);

      expect(await screen.findByRole("alert")).toHaveTextContent(message);
      expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("access-1");
      expect(mswState.refreshCount).toBe(0);
      expect(
        screen.queryByText(/session has expired/i),
      ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    },
  );
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\services\apiClient.test.js
