import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from "vitest";
import MockAdapter from "axios-mock-adapter";
import apiClient, {
  __resetAuthState,
  extractTokens,
  handleSessionExpired,
  normalizeExpiresInSeconds,
  refreshSession,
  setNavigator,
  startTokenRefreshScheduler,
  SESSION_EXPIRED_EVENT,
} from "../../src/services/apiClient";
import {
  clearTokens,
  getAccessToken,
  getRefreshToken,
  setTokens,
} from "../helpers/session";

const ok = (data, message = "ok") => ({ success: true, message, data });
const fail = (message) => ({ success: false, message, data: null });

const header = (config, name) => {
  const headers = config?.headers;
  if (!headers) return undefined;
  if (typeof headers.get === "function") {
    const value = headers.get(name);
    return value === undefined ? undefined : value;
  }
  return headers[name] ?? headers[name.toLowerCase()];
};

const wait = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

let mock;
let navigate;

beforeEach(() => {
  __resetAuthState();
  clearTokens();
  navigate = vi.fn();
  setNavigator(navigate);
  mock = new MockAdapter(apiClient);
});

afterEach(() => {
  mock.restore();
  __resetAuthState();
  clearTokens();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe("AC1 â€” request interceptor attaches the Bearer token", () => {
  it("attaches the token once to a protected call", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "access-1",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    mock.onGet("/patients/42").reply(200, ok({ id: 42 }));

    const response = await apiClient.get("/patients/42");

    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
    expect(mock.history.get).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(header(mock.history.get[0], "Authorization")).toBe(
      "Bearer access-1",
    );
  });

  it("sends no Authorization header when there is no token", async () => {
    mock.onGet("/public/config").reply(200, ok({}));

    await apiClient.get("/public/config");

    expect(header(mock.history.get[0], "Authorization")).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("does not attach a token to skipAuth requests", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "access-1",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    mock.onPost("/auth/login").reply(200, ok({ accessToken: "fresh" }));

    await apiClient.post("/auth/login", { user: "a" }, { skipAuth: true });

    expect(header(mock.history.post[0], "Authorization")).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe("AC2 â€” 401 refreshes once and retries every call", () => {
  it("shares a single refresh across concurrent 401s and retries all", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "expired-access",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    let tokenValid = false;

    mock
      .onGet(/\/reports\/.*/)
      .reply((config) =>
        tokenValid
          ? [200, ok({ id: config.url })]
          : [401, fail("Access token expired")],
      );
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(() => {
      tokenValid = true;
      return [
        200,
        ok({
          accessToken: "new-access",
          refreshToken: "new-refresh",
          expiresIn: 1800,
        }),
      ];
    });

    const results = await Promise.all([
      apiClient.get("/reports/a"),
      apiClient.get("/reports/b"),
      apiClient.get("/reports/c"),
    ]);

    expect(results.map((r) => r.status)).toEqual([200, 200, 200]);
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(mock.history.get).toHaveLength(6);
    const retried = mock.history.get.filter(
      (entry) => header(entry, "Authorization") === "Bearer new-access",
    );
    expect(retried).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("new-access");
    expect(navigate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("refreshSession returns the same in-flight promise", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "expired-access",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(
      200,
      ok({
        accessToken: "new-access",
        refreshToken: "new-refresh",
        expiresIn: 1800,
      }),
    );

    const [first, second] = await Promise.all([
      refreshSession(),
      refreshSession(),
    ]);

    expect(first).toBe("new-access");
    expect(second).toBe("new-access");
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});

describe("AC3 â€” failed refresh ends the session exactly once", () => {
  it("rejects, does not retry, clears auth and redirects to /login", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "expired-access",
      refreshToken: "bad-refresh",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    mock.onGet("/reports/a").reply(401, fail("Access token expired"));
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(401, fail("Refresh token invalid"));

    const received = await apiClient.get("/reports/a").then(
      () => null,
      (error) => error,
    );

    expect(received.status).toBe(401);
    expect(received.userMessage).toBe("Access token expired");
    expect(mock.history.get).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(navigate).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(navigate).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/login?reason=session-expired");
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBeNull();
    expect(getRefreshToken()).toBeNull();
  });

  it("redirects only once for concurrent failures", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "expired-access",
      refreshToken: "bad-refresh",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    const events = [];
    const listener = () => events.push(SESSION_EXPIRED_EVENT);
    window.addEventListener(SESSION_EXPIRED_EVENT, listener);
    mock.onGet(/\/reports\/.*/).reply(401, fail("Access token expired"));
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(401, fail("Refresh token invalid"));

    await Promise.all([
      apiClient.get("/reports/a").catch(() => {}),
      apiClient.get("/reports/b").catch(() => {}),
    ]);

    window.removeEventListener(SESSION_EXPIRED_EVENT, listener);
    expect(navigate).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(events).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("ends the session without calling refresh when no refresh token exists", async () => {
    setTokens({ accessToken: "expired-access", expiresIn: 1800 });
    mock.onGet("/reports/a").reply(401, fail("Access token expired"));

    await apiClient.get("/reports/a").catch(() => {});

    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(navigate).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/login?reason=session-expired");
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBeNull();
  });

  it("does not treat a 401 from a public (skipAuth) call as a session failure", async () => {
    mock.onPost("/auth/login").reply(401, fail("Invalid credentials"));

    const received = await apiClient
      .post("/auth/login", { email: "a@b.c" }, { skipAuth: true })
      .then(
        () => null,
        (error) => error,
      );

    expect(received.userMessage).toBe("Invalid credentials");
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(navigate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("does not loop when the retry itself is a 401", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "expired-access",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    mock.onGet("/reports/a").reply(401, fail("Access token expired"));
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(
      200,
      ok({
        accessToken: "new-access",
        refreshToken: "new-refresh",
        expiresIn: 1800,
      }),
    );

    await apiClient.get("/reports/a").catch(() => {});

    expect(mock.history.get).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(navigate).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});

describe("AC4 â€” proactive refresh honours activity", () => {
  it("refreshes once before expiry while the user is active", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "access-1",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(
      200,
      ok({
        accessToken: "access-2",
        refreshToken: "refresh-2",
        expiresIn: 7200,
      }),
    );

    const base = Date.now();
    let clock = base;
    const stop = startTokenRefreshScheduler({
      isUserActive: () => true,
      checkIntervalMs: 5,
      now: () => clock,
    });

    clock = base + 10 * 60 * 1000;
    await wait(40);
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(0);

    clock = base + 26 * 60 * 1000;
    await wait(40);
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("access-2");

    clock = base + 30 * 60 * 1000;
    await wait(40);
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(1);

    stop();
  });

  it("never refreshes proactively while the user is idle", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "access-1",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(
      200,
      ok({
        accessToken: "access-2",
        refreshToken: "refresh-2",
        expiresIn: 7200,
      }),
    );

    const base = Date.now();
    let clock = base;
    const stop = startTokenRefreshScheduler({
      isUserActive: () => false,
      checkIntervalMs: 5,
      now: () => clock,
    });

    clock = base + 60 * 60 * 1000;
    await wait(40);
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("access-1");

    stop();
    clock = base + 120 * 60 * 1000;
    await wait(40);
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("does nothing without an expiry timestamp", async () => {
    const stop = startTokenRefreshScheduler({
      isUserActive: () => true,
      checkIntervalMs: 5,
    });
    await wait(30);
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(0);
    stop();
  });
});

describe("AC4b â€” expiry normalisation and the visibility gate", () => {
  it("reads a millisecond expiresIn as seconds", () => {
    expect(normalizeExpiresInSeconds(1800000)).toBe(1800);
    expect(normalizeExpiresInSeconds(1800)).toBe(1800);
  });

  it("rejects a non-positive or non-numeric expiresIn", () => {
    expect(normalizeExpiresInSeconds(0)).toBeNull();
    expect(normalizeExpiresInSeconds(-5)).toBeNull();
    expect(normalizeExpiresInSeconds("soon")).toBeNull();
    expect(normalizeExpiresInSeconds(undefined)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("accepts snake_case fields and falls back to the 30-minute default", () => {
    const snake = extractTokens({
      success: true,
      data: {
        access_token: "a",
        refresh_token: "r",
        expires_in: 1800,
      },
    });
    expect(snake).toMatchObject({
      accessToken: "a",
      refreshToken: "r",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });

    expect(extractTokens({ data: { accessToken: "a" } }).expiresIn).toBe(1800);
  });

  it("converts an absolute expiry into seconds from now", () => {
    const inTenMinutes = new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
    const tokens = extractTokens({
      data: { accessToken: "a", expiresAt: inTenMinutes },
    });
    expect(tokens.expiresIn).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(595);
    expect(tokens.expiresIn).toBeLessThanOrEqual(600);
  });

  it("fires for an idle user when canExtendSession permits it", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "access-1",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    // A long-lived replacement token moves expiry outside the leeway window,
    // so the rapid ticks below cannot trigger a second refresh.
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(
      200,
      ok({
        accessToken: "access-2",
        refreshToken: "refresh-2",
        expiresIn: 7200,
      }),
    );

    const base = Date.now();
    let clock = base;
    // isUserActive is left at its inactive default on purpose: only the
    // visibility predicate is allowed to extend the session.
    const stop = startTokenRefreshScheduler({
      canExtendSession: () => true,
      checkIntervalMs: 5,
      now: () => clock,
    });

    clock = base + 26 * 60 * 1000;
    await wait(40);

    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("access-2");

    stop();
  });

  it("does not refresh while the tab is hidden, even inside the window", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "access-1",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(
      200,
      ok({
        accessToken: "access-2",
        refreshToken: "refresh-2",
        expiresIn: 1800,
      }),
    );

    const base = Date.now();
    let clock = base;
    const stop = startTokenRefreshScheduler({
      canExtendSession: () => false,
      checkIntervalMs: 5,
      now: () => clock,
    });

    clock = base + 60 * 60 * 1000;
    await wait(40);

    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("access-1");

    stop();
  });

  it("reports a failed proactive refresh instead of swallowing it", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "access-1",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    // A 500 â€” not a 401. A 401 from the refresh endpoint is meant to end the
    // session; a transient server failure must leave it untouched.
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(500, fail("Internal server error"));

    const onRefreshError = vi.fn();
    const base = Date.now();
    let clock = base;
    const stop = startTokenRefreshScheduler({
      canExtendSession: () => true,
      checkIntervalMs: 5,
      now: () => clock,
      onRefreshError,
    });

    clock = base + 26 * 60 * 1000;
    await wait(40);

    // Reported rather than swallowed: expiry never moves on failure, so each
    // tick retries until the token leaves the leeway window.
    expect(onRefreshError).toHaveBeenCalled();
    // A failed proactive refresh must never end the session on its own.
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("access-1");
    expect(navigate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    stop();
  });
});

describe("AC5 â€” 403 / 429 / 500 surface the envelope without logging out", () => {
  it.each([
    [403, "You do not have permission to view this resource"],
    [429, "Too many requests. Please slow down."],
    [500, "Internal server error"],
  ])(
    "surfaces the message for %i and keeps the session",
    async (status, message) => {
      setTokens({
        accessToken: "access-1",
        refreshToken: "refresh-1",
        expiresIn: 1800,
      });
      mock.onGet("/reports/summary").reply(status, {
        success: false,
        message,
        data: { retryAfter: 30 },
      });

      const received = await apiClient.get("/reports/summary").then(
        () => null,
        (error) => error,
      );

      expect(received.status).toBe(status);
      expect(received.success).toBe(false);
      expect(received.message).toBe(message);
      expect(received.userMessage).toBe(message);
      expect(received.data).toEqual({ retryAfter: 30 });
      expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("access-1");
      expect(navigate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(0);
    },
  );

  it("falls back to a generic message when the body is not the envelope", async () => {
    mock.onGet("/reports/summary").reply(500, "<html>boom</html>");

    const received = await apiClient.get("/reports/summary").then(
      () => null,
      (error) => error,
    );

    expect(received.status).toBe(500);
    expect(received.userMessage).toBe(
      "Something went wrong. Please try again.",
    );
    expect(navigate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("keeps the session on a network error", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "access-1",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    mock.onGet("/reports/summary").networkError();

    const received = await apiClient.get("/reports/summary").then(
      () => null,
      (error) => error,
    );

    expect(received.status).toBeNull();
    expect(received.userMessage).toBe(
      "Unable to reach the server. Please try again.",
    );
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("access-1");
    expect(navigate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

describe("edge cases and seams", () => {
  it("falls back to window.location.assign when no navigator is injected", () => {
    setNavigator(undefined);

    expect(() => handleSessionExpired()).not.toThrow();
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBeNull();
    expect(getRefreshToken()).toBeNull();
  });

  it("rejects when the refresh payload carries no access token", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "access-1",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(200, ok({}));

    await expect(refreshSession()).rejects.toThrow(/access token/i);
    expect(navigate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("access-1");
  });

  it("skips scheduler ticks while a refresh is already in flight", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "access-1",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 1800,
    });
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(
      () =>
        new Promise((resolve) =>
          setTimeout(
            () =>
              resolve([
                200,
                ok({
                  accessToken: "access-2",
                  refreshToken: "refresh-2",
                  expiresIn: 7200,
                }),
              ]),
            60,
          ),
        ),
    );

    let active = true;
    const clock = Date.now() + 26 * 60 * 1000;
    const inFlight = refreshSession();
    const stop = startTokenRefreshScheduler({
      isUserActive: () => active,
      checkIntervalMs: 5,
      now: () => clock,
    });

    await wait(40);
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(1);

    active = false;
    await inFlight;
    stop();
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("access-2");
  });

  it("is inactive-safe when no isUserActive callback is supplied", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "access-1",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 60,
    });
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(
      200,
      ok({
        accessToken: "access-2",
        refreshToken: "refresh-2",
        expiresIn: 7200,
      }),
    );

    const stop = startTokenRefreshScheduler({ checkIntervalMs: 5 });
    await wait(50);
    stop();

    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("access-1");
  });

  it("swallows a failed proactive refresh without ending the session", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "access-1",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 60,
    });
    mock
      .onPost("/auth/refresh")
      .reply(500, { success: false, message: "boom", data: null });

    const stop = startTokenRefreshScheduler({
      isUserActive: () => true,
      checkIntervalMs: 5,
    });
    await wait(50);
    stop();

    expect(mock.history.post.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(navigate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("access-1");
  });

  it("__resetAuthState stops running schedulers", async () => {
    setTokens({
      accessToken: "access-1",
      refreshToken: "refresh-1",
      expiresIn: 60,
    });
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(
      200,
      ok({
        accessToken: "access-2",
        refreshToken: "refresh-2",
        expiresIn: 7200,
      }),
    );

    startTokenRefreshScheduler({
      isUserActive: () => true,
      checkIntervalMs: 5,
    });
    __resetAuthState();

    await wait(50);
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import {
  adaptAppointmentsResponse,
  adaptClinicalNotesResponse,
  adaptPatientHistoryResponse,
  findAppointment,
  getAppointments,
  getClinicalNotes,
  getPatientHistory,
  saveClinicalNotes,
} from "../../src/services/appointmentService";
import { store } from "../../src/app/store";
import { clearSession, setSession } from "../../src/features/auth/authSlice";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../msw/handlers";

const appointment = (overrides = {}) => ({
  appointmentId: 101,
  patientId: 1,
  doctorId: 3,
  appointmentDate: "2026-10-14",
  timeSlot: "09:00:00",
  status: "SCHEDULED",
  reason: "Follow-up visit",
  ...overrides,
});

afterEach(() => {
  store.dispatch(clearSession());
  resetMswState();
});

describe("appointmentService", () => {
  it("adapts the appointments envelope and drops records without an id", () => {
    expect(
      adaptAppointmentsResponse({
        success: true,
        message: "ok",
        data: [appointment(), { status: "SCHEDULED" }, null],
      }),
    ).toEqual([appointment()]);

    expect(() =>
      adaptAppointmentsResponse({ success: true, data: { items: [] } }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
    expect(() =>
      adaptAppointmentsResponse({ success: false, data: [] }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
  });

  it("requests GET /api/appointments through the authenticated client", async () => {
    store.dispatch(setSession({ accessToken: "a-access", role: "DOCTOR" }));
    mswState.appointmentsResponses = [[appointment()]];
    const appointments = await getAppointments();

    expect(appointments).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(new URL(mswState.appointmentsHits[0].url).pathname).toMatch(
      /\/api\/appointments$/,
    );
    expect(mswState.appointmentsHits[0].authorization).toBe("Bearer a-access");
  });

  it("fetches a single appointment by id", async () => {
    // The consultation screen reads one appointment directly rather than
    // filtering the doctor's list.
    store.dispatch(setSession({ accessToken: "a-access", role: "DOCTOR" }));
    mswState.appointmentByIdResponses = {
      102: appointment({ appointmentId: 102 }),
    };
    const found = await findAppointment("102");

    expect(found?.appointmentId).toBe(102);
    expect(mswState.appointmentByIdHits).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(mswState.appointmentByIdHits[0].id).toBe("102");
    expect(mswState.appointmentByIdHits[0].authorization).toBe(
      "Bearer a-access",
    );
  });

  it("rejects for appointments the doctor cannot read", async () => {
    mswState.appointmentByIdErrorMode = "not-found";
    await expect(findAppointment(999)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      response: { status: 404 },
    });
  });

  it("propagates fetch failures for callers to surface", async () => {
    mswState.appointmentsErrorMode = "server-error";
    await expect(getAppointments()).rejects.toMatchObject({
      response: { status: 500 },
    });
  });
});

describe("appointmentService â€” clinical notes (fix/44 contract)", () => {
  it("adapts the clinical-notes response and rejects malformed payloads", () => {
    expect(
      adaptClinicalNotesResponse({
        success: true,
        message: "Clinical notes retrieved successfully",
        data: {
          appointmentId: 101,
          patientId: 7,
          doctorId: 3,
          clinicalNotes: "Note",
        },
      }),
    ).toEqual({
      appointmentId: 101,
      patientId: 7,
      doctorId: 3,
      clinicalNotes: "Note",
    });

    // Null notes come back as an empty string, not null.
    expect(
      adaptClinicalNotesResponse({
        success: true,
        data: { appointmentId: 1, clinicalNotes: null },
      }).clinicalNotes,
    ).toBe("");

    expect(() =>
      adaptClinicalNotesResponse({ success: true, data: {} }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
  });

  it("reads notes via GET /appointments/{id}/clinical-notes", async () => {
    mswState.clinicalNotesResponses = [
      { appointmentId: 101, patientId: 7, doctorId: 3, clinicalNotes: "A" },
    ];
    const notes = await getClinicalNotes(101);
    expect(notes.clinicalNotes).toBe("A");
    expect(mswState.clinicalNotesHits[0].id).toBe("101");
  });

  it("saves notes via PUT /appointments/{id} with only clinicalNotes", async () => {
    store.dispatch(setSession({ accessToken: "a-access", role: "DOCTOR" }));
    await saveClinicalNotes(101, "Started antibiotics.");

    expect(mswState.appointmentUpdateHits).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(mswState.appointmentUpdateHits[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: "101",
      body: { clinicalNotes: "Started antibiotics." },
      authorization: "Bearer a-access",
    });
  });

  it("surfaces the backend's cancelled-appointment rejection", async () => {
    mswState.appointmentUpdateErrorMode = "cancelled";
    await expect(saveClinicalNotes(101, "x")).rejects.toMatchObject({
      response: { status: 400 },
    });
  });

  it("adapts and fetches the patient history", () => {
    expect(
      adaptPatientHistoryResponse({
        success: true,
        data: { patientId: 7, appointments: null, prescriptions: null },
      }),
    ).toEqual({
      patient: null,
      patientId: 7,
      appointments: [],
      prescriptions: [],
    });

    // A nested patient object supplies both the label and the id.
    expect(
      adaptPatientHistoryResponse({
        success: true,
        data: {
          patient: { patientId: 7, fullName: "Jane Doe" },
          appointments: [],
          prescriptions: [],
        },
      }),
    ).toEqual({
      patient: { patientId: 7, fullName: "Jane Doe" },
      patientId: 7,
      appointments: [],
      prescriptions: [],
    });

    expect(() =>
      adaptPatientHistoryResponse({ success: true, data: {} }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
  });

  it("fetches history via GET /patients/{id}/history", async () => {
    mswState.historyResponses = [
      { patientId: 7, appointments: [], prescriptions: [] },
    ];
    const history = await getPatientHistory(7);
    expect(history.patientId).toBe(7);
    expect(mswState.historyHits[0].patientId).toBe("7");
  });
});

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { store } from "../../src/app/store";
import { clearSession, setSession } from "../../src/features/auth/authSlice";
import {
  adaptAuditLog,
  adaptAuditLogsPageResponse,
  getAuditLogs,
} from "../../src/services/auditService";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../msw/handlers";

afterEach(() => {
  store.dispatch(clearSession());
  resetMswState();
});

/** The real /api/audit-logs wire entries, as served by the mock. */
const LOGIN_ENTRY = {
  logId: 2,
  userId: 1,
  username: "superadmin",
  actionType: "LOGIN",
  entityType: "AUTH",
  entityId: null,
  description: "LOGIN outcome=SUCCESS target=AuthServiceImpl.login",
  ipAddress: "0:0:0:0:0:0:0:1",
  timestamp: "2026-10-20T10:54:46",
};

describe("auditService â€” /api/audit-logs Spring Page contract", () => {
  it("adapts one wire entry, keeping ids and normalising case", () => {
    expect(adaptAuditLog({ ...LOGIN_ENTRY, actionType: "login" })).toEqual({
      logId: 2,
      userId: 1,
      username: "superadmin",
      actionType: "LOGIN",
      entityType: "AUTH",
      entityId: null,
      description: "LOGIN outcome=SUCCESS target=AuthServiceImpl.login",
      ipAddress: "0:0:0:0:0:0:0:1",
      timestamp: "2026-10-20T10:54:46",
    });

    // Numeric ids survive as display strings; nulls stay null.
    expect(adaptAuditLog({ logId: 5, userId: 7, entityId: 12 })).toMatchObject(
      {
        logId: 5,
        userId: 7,
        entityId: "12",
      },
    );
    expect(adaptAuditLog({ logId: 6, userId: null }).userId).toBeNull();
  });

  it("adapts the page envelope and rejects malformed payloads", () => {
    const page = adaptAuditLogsPageResponse({
      success: true,
      message: "Audit logs retrieved successfully",
      data: {
        content: [LOGIN_ENTRY],
        totalElements: 1,
        totalPages: 1,
        number: 0,
        size: 20,
        first: true,
        last: true,
      },
    });

    expect(page).toMatchObject({
      page: 0,
      size: 20,
      totalElements: 1,
      totalPages: 1,
      first: true,
      last: true,
    });
    expect(page.content).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(page.content[0].logId).toBe(2);

    expect(() =>
      adaptAuditLogsPageResponse({ success: true, data: { items: [] } }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
    expect(() =>
      adaptAuditLogsPageResponse({ success: false, data: { content: [] } }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
  });

  it("fetches the default page through the authenticated client", async () => {
    store.dispatch(
      setSession({ accessToken: "a-access", role: "SUPER_ADMIN" }),
    );

    const result = await getAuditLogs();

    // Newest first, exactly as the backend orders the trail.
    expect(result.content.map((log) => log.logId)).toEqual([2, 1]);
    expect(result).toMatchObject({
      page: 0,
      size: 20,
      totalElements: 2,
      totalPages: 1,
      first: true,
      last: true,
    });

    const hit = mswState.auditLogsHits[0];
    const url = new URL(hit.url);
    expect(url.pathname).toMatch(/\/api\/audit-logs$/);
    expect(url.searchParams.get("page")).toBe("0");
    expect(url.searchParams.get("size")).toBe("20");
    expect(hit.authorization).toBe("Bearer a-access");
  });

  it("slices server-side pages and echoes the paging metadata", async () => {
    const firstPage = await getAuditLogs({ page: 0, size: 1 });
    expect(firstPage.content.map((log) => log.logId)).toEqual([2]);
    expect(firstPage).toMatchObject({
      totalElements: 2,
      totalPages: 2,
      first: true,
      last: false,
    });

    const secondPage = await getAuditLogs({ page: 1, size: 1 });
    expect(secondPage.content.map((log) => log.logId)).toEqual([1]);
    expect(secondPage).toMatchObject({
      page: 1,
      totalPages: 2,
      first: false,
      last: true,
    });
  });

  it("surfaces server failures for the page to render", async () => {
    mswState.auditLogsErrorMode = "server-error";

    const error = await getAuditLogs().catch((caught) => caught);

    expect(error.status).toBe(500);
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\services\authService.test.js
========================================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from "vitest";
import MockAdapter from "axios-mock-adapter";
import apiClient, { __resetAuthState } from "../../src/services/apiClient";
import authService, {
  login,
  logout,
  refresh,
  getCurrentUser,
  isAuthenticated,
} from "../../src/services/authService";
import {
  clearTokens,
  getAccessToken,
  getRefreshToken,
} from "../helpers/session";
import { store } from "../../src/app/store";
import { selectRole, selectUser } from "../../src/features/auth/authSlice";

const ok = (data, message = "ok") => ({ success: true, message, data });

const header = (config, name) => {
  const headers = config?.headers;
  if (!headers) return undefined;
  if (typeof headers.get === "function") return headers.get(name);
  return headers[name] ?? headers[name.toLowerCase()];
};

let mock;

beforeEach(() => {
  __resetAuthState();
  clearTokens();
  mock = new MockAdapter(apiClient);
});

afterEach(() => {
  mock.restore();
  __resetAuthState();
  clearTokens();
});

describe("authService", () => {
  it("login stores tokens and resets the expired-session flag", async () => {
    mock.onPost("/auth/login").reply(
      200,
      ok({
        accessToken: "access-1",
        refreshToken: "refresh-1",
        expiresIn: 1800,
      }),
    );

    const payload = await login({ email: "a@b.c", password: "secret" });

    expect(payload.data.accessToken).toBe("access-1");
    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("access-1");
    expect(getRefreshToken()).toBe("refresh-1");
    expect(isAuthenticated()).toBe(true);
    expect(header(mock.history.post[0], "Authorization")).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("login accepts camelCase payloads", async () => {
    mock
      .onPost("/auth/login")
      .reply(200, ok({ accessToken: "a", refreshToken: "r", expiresIn: 1800 }));

    await login({ email: "a@b.c", password: "secret" });

    expect(getAccessToken()).toBe("a");
  });

  it("login stores the role and username from the response for the route guard (AC1)", async () => {
    mock.onPost("/auth/login").reply(
      200,
      ok({
        accessToken: "a",
        refreshToken: "r",
        expiresIn: 1800,
        role: "DOCTOR",
        username: "dr.house",
      }),
    );

    await login({ email: "d@o.c", password: "x" });

    expect(selectRole(store.getState())).toBe("DOCTOR");
    expect(selectUser(store.getState())).toBe("dr.house");
  });

  it("login throws when the response carries no access token", async () => {
    mock.onPost("/auth/login").reply(200, ok({}));

    await expect(login({ email: "a@b.c", password: "x" })).rejects.toThrow(
      /access token/i,
    );
    expect(isAuthenticated()).toBe(false);
  });

  it("login failure surfaces the envelope and keeps no session", async () => {
    mock.onPost("/auth/login").reply(401, {
      success: false,
      message: "Invalid credentials",
      data: null,
    });

    const error = await login({ email: "a@b.c", password: "wrong" }).catch(
      (e) => e,
    );

    expect(error.userMessage).toBe("Invalid credentials");
    expect(isAuthenticated()).toBe(false);
  });

  it("refresh goes through the central client once", async () => {
    mock.onPost("/auth/login").reply(
      200,
      ok({
        accessToken: "access-1",
        refreshToken: "refresh-1",
        expiresIn: 1800,
      }),
    );
    await login({ email: "a@b.c", password: "x" });
    mock.onPost("/auth/refresh").reply(
      200,
      ok({
        accessToken: "access-2",
        refreshToken: "refresh-2",
        expiresIn: 1800,
      }),
    );

    const token = await refresh();

    expect(token).toBe("access-2");
    expect(mock.history.post).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(header(mock.history.post[1], "Authorization")).toBeUndefined();
    expect(getRefreshToken()).toBe("refresh-2");
  });

  it("logout clears local state even when the API call fails", async () => {
    mock.onPost("/auth/login").reply(
      200,
      ok({
        accessToken: "access-1",
        refreshToken: "refresh-1",
        expiresIn: 1800,
      }),
    );
    await login({ email: "a@b.c", password: "x" });
    mock.onPost("/auth/logout").reply(500, {
      success: false,
      message: "Internal server error",
      data: null,
    });

    await logout();

    expect(getAccessToken()).toBeNull();
    expect(getRefreshToken()).toBeNull();
    expect(isAuthenticated()).toBe(false);
  });

  it("getCurrentUser sends the Bearer token", async () => {
    mock.onPost("/auth/login").reply(
      200,
      ok({
        accessToken: "access-1",
        refreshToken: "refresh-1",
        expiresIn: 1800,
      }),
    );
    await login({ email: "a@b.c", password: "x" });
    mock.onGet("/auth/me").reply(200, ok({ id: 7 }));

    const response = await getCurrentUser();

    expect(response.data.data).toEqual({ id: 7 });
    expect(header(mock.history.get[0], "Authorization")).toBe(
      "Bearer access-1",
    );
  });

  it("isAuthenticated is false with a cleared store", () => {
    expect(isAuthenticated()).toBe(false);
  });

  it("exposes the service surface", () => {
    expect(Object.keys(authService).sort()).toEqual([
      "forgotPassword",
      "getCurrentUser",
      "isAuthenticated",
      "login",
      "logout",
      "refresh",
      "resetPassword",
    ]);
  });

  it("forgotPassword sends post to /auth/forgot-password with email and skipAuth", async () => {
    mock.onPost("/auth/forgot-password").reply(200, ok(null));

    const res = await authService.forgotPassword("user@example.com");

    expect(res.success).toBe(true);
    expect(JSON.parse(mock.history.post[0].data)).toEqual({
      email: "user@example.com",
    });
    expect(header(mock.history.post[0], "Authorization")).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("resetPassword sends post to /auth/reset-password with token, newPassword and skipAuth", async () => {
    mock.onPost("/auth/reset-password").reply(200, ok(null));

    const res = await authService.resetPassword("tok-123", "newPass12345");

    expect(res.success).toBe(true);
    expect(JSON.parse(mock.history.post[0].data)).toEqual({
      token: "tok-123",
      newPassword: "newPass12345",
    });
    expect(header(mock.history.post[0], "Authorization")).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("never logs the password it sends", async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(console, "log").mockImplementation(() => {});
    mock
      .onPost("/auth/login")
      .reply(200, ok({ accessToken: "a", refreshToken: "r", expiresIn: 1800 }));

    await login({ email: "a@b.c", password: "super-secret" });

    const logged = spy.mock.calls.flat().map((value) => JSON.stringify(value));
    expect(logged.join("\n")).not.toContain("super-secret");
  });

  it("never logs reset credentials or token", async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(console, "log").mockImplementation(() => {});
    mock.onPost("/auth/reset-password").reply(200, ok(null));
    await authService.resetPassword("secret-reset-token", "SecretPassword123!");
    const logged = spy.mock.calls.flat().map((value) => JSON.stringify(value));
    expect(logged.join("\n")).not.toContain("secret-reset-token");
    expect(logged.join("\n")).not.toContain("SecretPassword123!");
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\services\doctorScheduleService.test.js
========================================
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { store } from "../../src/app/store";
import { clearSession, setSession } from "../../src/features/auth/authSlice";
import {
  adaptDoctorScheduleResponse,
  extractConflictSlots,
  getDoctorSchedule,
  setDoctorSchedule,
} from "../../src/services/doctorScheduleService";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../msw/handlers";

afterEach(() => {
  store.dispatch(clearSession());
  resetMswState();
});

/** The FR009/UC4 spec example, seeded into the mock for doctor 4. */
const SPEC_SCHEDULE = {
  doctorId: 4,
  workingDays: ["MONDAY", "TUESDAY", "WEDNESDAY", "THURSDAY", "FRIDAY"],
  timeSlots: [
    { start: "09:00", end: "12:00" },
    { start: "13:00", end: "17:00" },
  ],
};

describe("doctorScheduleService â€” FR009/UC4 working schedules", () => {
  it("adapts the envelope, uppercases days and pads times", () => {
    expect(
      adaptDoctorScheduleResponse({
        success: true,
        message: "Schedule retrieved successfully",
        data: {
          doctorId: "4",
          workingDays: ["monday", " friday "],
          timeSlots: [{ start: "9:00", end: "12:00" }],
        },
      }),
    ).toEqual({
      doctorId: 4,
      workingDays: ["MONDAY", "FRIDAY"],
      timeSlots: [{ start: "09:00", end: "12:00" }],
    });

    expect(() =>
      adaptDoctorScheduleResponse({ success: true, data: { doctorId: 4 } }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
    expect(() =>
      adaptDoctorScheduleResponse({
        success: true,
        data: { doctorId: 4, workingDays: ["MONDAY"], timeSlots: [{}] },
      }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
    expect(() =>
      adaptDoctorScheduleResponse({
        success: false,
        data: { doctorId: 4, workingDays: [], timeSlots: [] },
      }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
  });

  it("reads a configured schedule through the authenticated client", async () => {
    store.dispatch(
      setSession({ accessToken: "s-access", role: "SUPER_ADMIN" }),
    );

    const schedule = await getDoctorSchedule(4);

    expect(schedule).toEqual(SPEC_SCHEDULE);
    expect(mswState.doctorScheduleHits[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: "4",
      authorization: "Bearer s-access",
    });
  });

  it("returns empty sets for a doctor without a schedule", async () => {
    expect(await getDoctorSchedule(5)).toEqual({
      doctorId: 5,
      workingDays: [],
      timeSlots: [],
    });
  });

  it("reports an unknown doctor as 404", async () => {
    const error = await getDoctorSchedule(999).catch((caught) => caught);

    expect(error.status).toBe(404);
  });

  it("replaces the schedule and persists it in the mock backend", async () => {
    const payload = {
      workingDays: ["TUESDAY", "THURSDAY"],
      timeSlots: [{ start: "08:00", end: "11:30" }],
    };

    const saved = await setDoctorSchedule(4, payload);

    expect(saved).toEqual({ doctorId: 4, ...payload });
    expect(mswState.doctorScheduleWriteHits[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: "4",
      body: payload,
    });

    const reread = await getDoctorSchedule(4);
    expect(reread).toEqual({ doctorId: 4, ...payload });
  });

  it("surfaces slot conflicts with the overlapping pair and saves nothing", async () => {
    const before = JSON.stringify(mswState.doctorSchedules["4"]);

    const error = await setDoctorSchedule(4, {
      workingDays: ["MONDAY"],
      timeSlots: [
        { start: "09:00", end: "12:00" },
        { start: "11:00", end: "13:00" },
      ],
    }).catch((caught) => caught);

    expect(error.status).toBe(400);
    expect(error.userMessage).toMatch(/Time slot conflict/);
    expect(extractConflictSlots(error)).toEqual([
      { start: "09:00", end: "12:00" },
      { start: "11:00", end: "13:00" },
    ]);
    expect(JSON.stringify(mswState.doctorSchedules["4"])).toBe(before);
  });

  it("enforces the backend's validation rules without persisting", async () => {
    // Empty day or slot sets.
    const empty = await setDoctorSchedule(4, {
      workingDays: [],
      timeSlots: [{ start: "09:00", end: "10:00" }],
    }).catch((caught) => caught);
    expect(empty.status).toBe(400);

    // end â‰¤ start.
    const reversed = await setDoctorSchedule(4, {
      workingDays: ["MONDAY"],
      timeSlots: [{ start: "12:00", end: "09:00" }],
    }).catch((caught) => caught);
    expect(reversed.status).toBe(400);

    // Unknown doctor id.
    const unknown = await setDoctorSchedule(999, {
      workingDays: ["MONDAY"],
      timeSlots: [{ start: "09:00", end: "10:00" }],
    }).catch((caught) => caught);
    expect(unknown.status).toBe(404);
  });

  it("rejects malformed payloads before any request", async () => {
    const hits = mswState.doctorScheduleWriteHits.length;

    await expect(
      setDoctorSchedule(4, { workingDays: "MONDAY", timeSlots: [] }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/must be arrays/i);
    await expect(
      setDoctorSchedule(4, {
        workingDays: ["MONDAY"],
        timeSlots: [{ start: "9am", end: "10:00" }],
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/HH:mm/i);

    expect(mswState.doctorScheduleWriteHits.length).toBe(hits);
  });
});

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import {
  adaptPatientResponse,
  adaptPatientsResponse,
  getPatient,
  getPatients,
} from "../../src/services/patientService";
import { store } from "../../src/app/store";
import { clearSession, setSession } from "../../src/features/auth/authSlice";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../msw/handlers";

afterEach(() => {
  store.dispatch(clearSession());
  resetMswState();
});

describe("patientService â€” verified PatientResponse shape", () => {
  it("adapts a single patient (patientId / fullName)", () => {
    expect(
      adaptPatientResponse({
        success: true,
        message: "Patient retrieved successfully",
        data: { patientId: 42, fullName: "Jane Doe" },
      }),
    ).toEqual({ patientId: 42, fullName: "Jane Doe" });

    // Missing name degrades to a label instead of inventing one.
    expect(
      adaptPatientResponse({ success: true, data: { patientId: 42 } }),
    ).toEqual({ patientId: 42, fullName: "Patient 42" });

    expect(() =>
      adaptPatientResponse({ success: true, data: { fullName: "No id" } }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
    expect(() =>
      adaptPatientResponse({ success: false, data: { patientId: 1 } }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
  });

  it("adapts the directory and skips records without an id", () => {
    expect(
      adaptPatientsResponse({
        success: true,
        message: "Patients retrieved successfully",
        data: [
          { patientId: 1, fullName: "Jane Doe" },
          { patientId: 2, fullName: "  " },
          { fullName: "No id" },
          null,
        ],
      }),
    ).toEqual([
      { patientId: 1, fullName: "Jane Doe" },
      { patientId: 2, fullName: "Patient 2" },
    ]);

    expect(() =>
      adaptPatientsResponse({ success: true, data: { items: [] } }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
  });

  it("fetches one patient through the authenticated client", async () => {
    store.dispatch(setSession({ accessToken: "p-access", role: "DOCTOR" }));
    mswState.tokenValid = true;
    const patient = await getPatient(7);

    expect(patient).toEqual({ patientId: 7, fullName: "Jane Doe" });
    expect(mswState.protectedHits[0].id).toBe("7");
    expect(mswState.protectedHits[0].authorization).toBe("Bearer p-access");
  });

  it("requests the patient directory through the authenticated client", async () => {
    store.dispatch(setSession({ accessToken: "p-access", role: "DOCTOR" }));
    mswState.patientsResponses = [[{ patientId: 7, fullName: "Sam Lee" }]];
    const patients = await getPatients();

    expect(patients).toEqual([{ patientId: 7, fullName: "Sam Lee" }]);
    expect(new URL(mswState.patientsHits[0].url).pathname).toMatch(
      /\/api\/patients$/,
    );
    expect(mswState.patientsHits[0].authorization).toBe("Bearer p-access");
  });

  it("propagates directory failures for callers to swallow", async () => {
    mswState.patientsErrorMode = "server-error";
    await expect(getPatients()).rejects.toMatchObject({
      response: { status: 500 },
    });
  });
});

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import {
  adaptPrescriptionResponse,
  createPrescription,
} from "../../src/services/prescriptionService";
import { store } from "../../src/app/store";
import { clearSession, setSession } from "../../src/features/auth/authSlice";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../msw/handlers";

afterEach(() => {
  store.dispatch(clearSession());
  resetMswState();
});

describe("prescriptionService â€” verified POST /api/prescriptions contract", () => {
  it("adapts the prescription response and rejects malformed payloads", () => {
    expect(
      adaptPrescriptionResponse({
        success: true,
        message: "Prescription created successfully",
        data: {
          prescriptionId: 9001,
          patientId: 7,
          appointmentId: 101,
          date: "2026-10-14",
          status: "ISSUED",
          items: [{ medicineName: "Amoxicillin" }],
          createdAt: "2026-10-14T09:00:00",
        },
      }),
    ).toMatchObject({ prescriptionId: 9001, status: "ISSUED" });

    expect(() =>
      adaptPrescriptionResponse({ success: true, data: {} }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
  });

  it("creates a prescription with free-text EXTERNAL_PURCHASE items", async () => {
    store.dispatch(setSession({ accessToken: "rx-access", role: "DOCTOR" }));
    const prescription = await createPrescription({
      patientId: 7,
      appointmentId: 101,
      items: [
        {
          medicineName: "Amoxicillin",
          dosage: "1 capsule",
          frequency: "Three times daily",
          durationDays: 7,
          quantity: 21,
        },
      ],
    });

    expect(prescription.prescriptionId).toBe(9001);
    expect(mswState.prescriptionCreateHits).toHaveLength(1);
    const { body, authorization } = mswState.prescriptionCreateHits[0];
    expect(authorization).toBe("Bearer rx-access");
    expect(body).toMatchObject({
      patientId: 7,
      appointmentId: 101,
      items: [
        {
          itemType: "EXTERNAL_PURCHASE",
          medicineName: "Amoxicillin",
          dosage: "1 capsule",
          frequency: "Three times daily",
          durationDays: 7,
          quantity: 21,
        },
      ],
    });
    // Clinical notes live on the appointment â€” never on the prescription.
    expect(body.clinicalNotes).toBeUndefined();
    // No medicineId: doctors hold no MEDICINE_READ permission.
    expect(body.items[0].medicineId).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("surfaces backend rejections for callers to display", async () => {
    mswState.prescriptionCreateErrorMode = "validation";
    await expect(
      createPrescription({ patientId: 7, appointmentId: 101, items: [] }),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ response: { status: 400 } });
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\services\queueService.test.js

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  adaptQueueResponse,
  completeAppointment,
  getTodayQueue,
} from "../../src/services/queueService";
import { store } from "../../src/app/store";
import { clearSession, setSession } from "../../src/features/auth/authSlice";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../msw/handlers";

afterEach(() => {
  store.dispatch(clearSession());
  resetMswState();
});

describe("queueService", () => {
  it("adapts the backend appointment response and rejects invalid records", () => {
    const record = {
      appointmentId: 9,
      patientId: 1,
      doctorId: 3,
      appointmentDate: "2026-10-14",
      timeSlot: "09:00:00",
      status: "SCHEDULED",
      queuePosition: 1,
    };
    expect(
      adaptQueueResponse({
        success: true,
        message: "Queue retrieved successfully",
        data: [record],
      }),
      // No name on the wire: the adapter supplies the `Patient <id>` label.
    ).toEqual([{ ...record, patientFullName: "Patient 1" }]);
    expect(() =>
      adaptQueueResponse({ success: true, data: { records: [record] } }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
    expect(() =>
      adaptQueueResponse({
        success: true,
        data: [{ id: 10, status: "SCHEDULED" }],
      }),
    ).toThrow(/record format/i);
  });

  it("surfaces patientFullName and degrades to a label, never to a blank", () => {
    const names = adaptQueueResponse({
      success: true,
      data: [
        {
          appointmentId: 1,
          patientId: 7,
          status: "SCHEDULED",
          patientFullName: "  Jane Doe  ",
        },
        {
          appointmentId: 2,
          patientId: 8,
          status: "SCHEDULED",
          patientFullName: "   ",
        },
        {
          appointmentId: 3,
          status: "SCHEDULED",
          patientFullName: "Sam Lee",
        },
        { appointmentId: 4, status: "SCHEDULED" },
      ],
    }).map((record) => record.patientFullName);

    expect(names).toEqual([
      "Jane Doe", // trimmed
      "Patient 8", // blank name falls back to the id label
      "Sam Lee", // a name without an id still wins
      null, // neither â€” the page renders "â€”"
    ]);
  });

  it("requests today's `/api/queue/today` through the authenticated shared client", async () => {
    store.dispatch(setSession({ accessToken: "queue-access", role: "DOCTOR" }));
    const record = { appointmentId: 9, status: "SCHEDULED" };
    mswState.queueResponses = [[record]];
    const records = await getTodayQueue();

    // Neither a name nor a patient id: the field is present but empty, so the
    // caller decides what to render.
    expect(records).toEqual([{ ...record, patientFullName: null }]);

    expect(
      new URL(mswState.queueHits[0].url, "http://localhost").pathname,
    ).toMatch(/\/(api\/)?queue\/today$/);
    expect(new URL(mswState.queueHits[0].url).search).toBe("");
  });

  it("marks an appointment COMPLETED via PATCH /queue/{id}/status", async () => {
    store.dispatch(setSession({ accessToken: "queue-access", role: "DOCTOR" }));
    const data = await completeAppointment(42);
    expect(data).toEqual({ appointmentId: 42, status: "COMPLETED" });
    expect(mswState.queueCompleteHits).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(mswState.queueCompleteHits[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: "42",
      body: { status: "COMPLETED" },
      authorization: "Bearer queue-access",
    });
  });

  it("surfaces conflicting status transitions as HTTP errors (409 and 400)", async () => {
    mswState.queueCompleteErrorMode = "conflict";
    await expect(completeAppointment(1)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      response: { status: 409 },
    });

    // This backend actually reports invalid transitions as 400.
    mswState.queueCompleteErrorMode = "bad-request";
    await expect(completeAppointment(1)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      response: { status: 400 },
    });
  });

  it("rejects malformed completion payloads", async () => {
    mswState.queueCompleteErrorMode = "server-error";
    await expect(completeAppointment(1)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      response: { status: 500 },
    });
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\services\rolePermissionService.test.js
========================================
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { store } from "../../src/app/store";
import { clearSession, setSession } from "../../src/features/auth/authSlice";
import {
  adaptRolePermissionsResponse,
  getRolePermissions,
  setRolePermissions,
} from "../../src/services/rolePermissionService";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../msw/handlers";

afterEach(() => {
  store.dispatch(clearSession());
  resetMswState();
});

/**
 * FR003/UC3 â€” configurable role permissions. The mock backend enforces
 * the same rules as the real one (400 unknown codes, 404 unknown role,
 * 422 BR004-006) and leaves the stored set untouched on every failure
 * (AC5), which is exactly what the Access Control screen relies on.
 */
describe("rolePermissionService â€” role permission governance", () => {
  it("adapts the envelope and rejects malformed payloads", () => {
    expect(
      adaptRolePermissionsResponse({
        success: true,
        message: "Permissions retrieved successfully",
        data: { role: "ADMIN", permissions: ["PATIENT_CREATE"] },
      }),
    ).toEqual({ role: "ADMIN", permissions: ["PATIENT_CREATE"] });

    expect(() =>
      adaptRolePermissionsResponse({ success: true, data: { role: "ADMIN" } }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
    expect(() =>
      adaptRolePermissionsResponse({
        success: true,
        data: { role: "ADMIN", permissions: [7] },
      }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
    expect(() =>
      adaptRolePermissionsResponse({
        success: false,
        data: { role: "ADMIN", permissions: [] },
      }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
  });

  it("reads a role's current set through the authenticated client", async () => {
    store.dispatch(
      setSession({ accessToken: "sa-access", role: "SUPER_ADMIN" }),
    );

    const result = await getRolePermissions("ADMIN");

    expect(result.role).toBe("ADMIN");
    expect(result.permissions).toContain("PATIENT_CREATE");
    expect(mswState.rolePermissionReadHits[0]).toMatchObject({
      role: "ADMIN",
      authorization: "Bearer sa-access",
    });
  });

  it("reports an unknown role as 404", async () => {
    const error = await getRolePermissions("PATIENT").catch((caught) => caught);

    expect(error.status).toBe(404);
  });

  it("replaces a role's full set and persists it in the mock backend", async () => {
    const updated = await setRolePermissions("DOCTOR", [
      "PATIENT_READ",
      "APPOINTMENT_READ",
    ]);

    expect(updated).toEqual({
      role: "DOCTOR",
      permissions: ["PATIENT_READ", "APPOINTMENT_READ"],
    });
    expect(mswState.rolePermissions.DOCTOR).toEqual([
      "PATIENT_READ",
      "APPOINTMENT_READ",
    ]);

    const reread = await getRolePermissions("DOCTOR");
    expect(reread.permissions).toEqual([
      "PATIENT_READ",
      "APPOINTMENT_READ",
    ]);
  });

  it("rejects unknown permission codes with 400 and saves nothing", async () => {
    const before = [...mswState.rolePermissions.DOCTOR];

    const error = await setRolePermissions("DOCTOR", ["NOT_A_PERMISSION"]).catch(
      (caught) => caught,
    );

    expect(error.status).toBe(400);
    expect(error.userMessage).toMatch(/Unknown permission/);
    expect(mswState.rolePermissions.DOCTOR).toEqual(before);
  });

  it("enforces the backend policy rules BR004-006 without persisting", async () => {
    // BR006 â€” a role's permission set cannot be empty.
    const empty = await setRolePermissions("DOCTOR", []).catch((caught) => caught);
    expect(empty.status).toBe(422);
    expect(empty.userMessage).toMatch(/BR006/);

    // BR005 â€” the SUPER_ADMIN role's own set cannot be modified.
    const sa = await setRolePermissions("SUPER_ADMIN", ["PATIENT_READ"]).catch(
      (caught) => caught,
    );
    expect(sa.status).toBe(422);
    expect(sa.userMessage).toMatch(/BR005/);

    // BR004 â€” SYSTEM_BACKUP is privileged, SUPER_ADMIN only.
    const backup = await setRolePermissions("ADMIN", ["SYSTEM_BACKUP"]).catch(
      (caught) => caught,
    );
    expect(backup.status).toBe(422);
    expect(backup.userMessage).toMatch(/BR004/);

    // Nothing above mutated the stored sets (AC5).
    expect(mswState.rolePermissions.SUPER_ADMIN.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(mswState.rolePermissions.ADMIN).not.toContain("SYSTEM_BACKUP");
    expect(mswState.rolePermissions.DOCTOR).not.toEqual([]);
  });

  it("surfaces a missing ROLE_PERMISSION_UPDATE as 403", async () => {
    mswState.rolePermissionsErrorMode = "forbidden";

    const error = await setRolePermissions("DOCTOR", ["PATIENT_READ"]).catch(
      (caught) => caught,
    );

    expect(error.status).toBe(403);
    expect(error.userMessage).toMatch(/Access denied/);
  });

  it("rejects malformed permission payloads before any request", async () => {
    const hits = mswState.rolePermissionWriteHits.length;

    await expect(
      setRolePermissions("DOCTOR", "PATIENT_READ"),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/permissions must be an array/i);
    expect(mswState.rolePermissionWriteHits.length).toBe(hits);
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\services\systemBackupService.test.js
========================================
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { store } from "../../src/app/store";
import { clearSession, setSession } from "../../src/features/auth/authSlice";
import {
  adaptBackupListResponse,
  adaptBackupRecord,
  createBackup,
  downloadBackup,
  formatBytes,
  listBackups,
  parseContentDisposition,
  restoreBackup,
} from "../../src/services/systemBackupService";
import { mswState, resetMswState } from "../msw/handlers";

afterEach(() => {
  store.dispatch(clearSession());
  resetMswState();
});

/** The FR010/UC5 spec example, seeded into the mock as the newest backup. */
const SPEC_BACKUP = {
  backupId: 2,
  createdAt: "2026-10-19T10:15:30.123",
  filename: "onecare-backup-20261019T101530.backup",
  sizeBytes: 48213,
};

describe("systemBackupService â€” FR010/UC5 backup & restore", () => {
  it("normalises a record and sorts the list newest first", () => {
    expect(
      adaptBackupRecord({
        backupId: "3",
        createdAt: "2026-10-19T10:15:30.123",
        filename: "onecare-backup.backup",
        sizeBytes: "48213",
      }),
    ).toEqual({
      backupId: 3,
      createdAt: "2026-10-19T10:15:30.123",
      filename: "onecare-backup.backup",
      sizeBytes: 48213,
    });

    expect(() => adaptBackupRecord(null)).toThrow(/record format/i);
    expect(() => adaptBackupRecord({ filename: "x.backup" })).toThrow(
      /record format/i,
    );

    expect(
      adaptBackupListResponse({
        success: true,
        data: [
          {
            backupId: 1,
            createdAt: "2026-10-12T03:00:08.902",
            filename: "older.backup",
            sizeBytes: 46880,
          },
          SPEC_BACKUP,
        ],
      }).map((backup) => backup.backupId),
    ).toEqual([2, 1]);

    expect(() =>
      adaptBackupListResponse({ success: false, data: [] }),
    ).toThrow(/list response/i);
    expect(() =>
      adaptBackupListResponse({ success: true, data: { backupId: 1 } }),
    ).toThrow(/list response/i);
  });

  it("lists stored backups through the authenticated client", async () => {
    store.dispatch(
      setSession({ accessToken: "s-access", role: "SUPER_ADMIN" }),
    );

    const backups = await listBackups();

    expect(backups).toEqual([SPEC_BACKUP, mswState.systemBackups[1]]);
    expect(backups[0].backupId).toBeGreaterThan(backups[1].backupId);
    expect(mswState.systemBackupHits[0]).toMatchObject({
      authorization: "Bearer s-access",
    });
  });

  it("creates a backup and shows it in the next listing", async () => {
    const record = await createBackup();

    expect(record.backupId).toBe(3);
    expect(record.filename).toMatch(/^onecare-backup-\d{8}T\d{6}\.backup$/);
    expect(record.sizeBytes).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(mswState.systemBackupCreateHits).toHaveLength(1);

    const listed = await listBackups();
    expect(listed[0]).toEqual(record);
  });

  it("streams the encrypted file and keeps its real filename", async () => {
    const { blob, filename } = await downloadBackup(1);

    expect(blob).toBeInstanceOf(Blob);
    expect(blob.size).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(filename).toBe("onecare-backup-20261012T030008.backup");
    expect(mswState.systemBackupDownloadHits[0]).toMatchObject({ id: "1" });
  });

  it("reports an unknown backup as 404 on download", async () => {
    const error = await downloadBackup(999).catch((caught) => caught);

    expect(error.status).toBe(404);
  });

  it("restores from a stored backupId and returns the per-table counts", async () => {
    store.dispatch(
      setSession({ accessToken: "s-access", role: "SUPER_ADMIN" }),
    );

    const counts = await restoreBackup({ backupId: 2, confirm: true });

    expect(counts).toEqual({ users: 12, patients: 340, appointments: 1250 });
    expect(mswState.systemRestoreHits[0]).toMatchObject({
      backupId: "2",
      fileName: null,
      authorization: "Bearer s-access",
    });
  });

  it("refuses an unconfirmed restore before any request (AC4)", async () => {
    const hits = mswState.systemRestoreHits.length;

    await expect(restoreBackup({ backupId: 2 })).rejects.toThrow(
      /confirmation/i,
    );
    await expect(restoreBackup({ confirm: true })).rejects.toThrow(
      /backupId or an uploaded/i,
    );

    expect(mswState.systemRestoreHits.length).toBe(hits);
  });

  it("surfaces the backend's confirmation and lookup errors", async () => {
    const unknown = await restoreBackup({
      backupId: 999,
      confirm: true,
    }).catch((caught) => caught);
    expect(unknown.status).toBe(404);
    expect(unknown.userMessage).toMatch(/Unknown backupId/);

    mswState.systemRestoreErrorMode = "corrupt";
    const corrupt = await restoreBackup({ backupId: 2, confirm: true }).catch(
      (caught) => caught,
    );
    expect(corrupt.status).toBe(400);
    expect(corrupt.userMessage).toMatch(/Checksum mismatch/);

    mswState.systemRestoreErrorMode = "forbidden";
    const forbidden = await restoreBackup({
      backupId: 2,
      confirm: true,
    }).catch((caught) => caught);
    expect(forbidden.status).toBe(403);
  });

  it("parses Content-Disposition filenames and formats sizes", () => {
    expect(
      parseContentDisposition(
        'attachment; filename="onecare-backup-20261019T101530.backup"',
      ),
    ).toBe("onecare-backup-20261019T101530.backup");
    expect(parseContentDisposition("attachment; filename=plain.backup")).toBe(
      "plain.backup",
    );
    expect(parseContentDisposition("attachment")).toBeNull();
    expect(parseContentDisposition(undefined)).toBeNull();

    expect(formatBytes(0)).toBe("â€”");
    expect(formatBytes(900)).toBe("900 B");
    expect(formatBytes(512)).toBe("0.5 KB");
    expect(formatBytes(48213)).toBe("47.1 KB");
  });
});

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\services\systemConfigService.test.js
========================================
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import apiClient from "../../src/services/apiClient";
import {
  DEFAULT_BASE_URL,
  applySessionBaseUrl,
  normalizeBaseUrl,
  testBackendHealth,
} from "../../src/services/systemConfigService";

/**
 * The probe logic is pure outcome-classification, so the tests inject
 * fetch stubs instead of hitting the network: mapping ok/status/throw/
 * abort to "healthy"/"responding"/"unreachable"/"timeout" is exactly
 * what the config screen's copy depends on.
 */
const fetchOk = async () => ({ ok: true, status: 200 });
const fetchNotFound = async () => ({ ok: false, status: 404 });
const fetchNetworkError = async () => {
  throw new TypeError("Failed to fetch");
};
const fetchHanging =
  (_url, { signal }) =>
  new Promise((_resolve, reject) => {
    signal.addEventListener("abort", () =>
      reject(new DOMException("The operation was aborted.", "AbortError")),
    );
  });

describe("normalizeBaseUrl", () => {
  it("trims whitespace and drops trailing slashes", () => {
    expect(normalizeBaseUrl(" http://localhost:8080/api/ ")).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      url: "http://localhost:8080/api",
      error: "",
    });
    expect(normalizeBaseUrl("https://api.example.test/api///").url).toBe(
      "https://api.example.test/api",
    );
  });

  it("rejects URLs without an http(s) protocol", () => {
    expect(normalizeBaseUrl("localhost:8080/api").ok).toBe(false);
    expect(normalizeBaseUrl("ftp://files.example.test").ok).toBe(false);
    expect(normalizeBaseUrl("").ok).toBe(false);
    expect(normalizeBaseUrl(null).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(normalizeBaseUrl(undefined).error).toMatch(/http/i);
  });
});

describe("testBackendHealth", () => {
  it("classifies a 2xx answer as healthy", async () => {
    const result = await testBackendHealth("http://localhost:8080/api", {
      fetchImpl: fetchOk,
    });
    expect(result).toEqual({ outcome: "healthy", status: 200 });
  });

  it("classifies any other HTTP answer as responding â€” the server is up", async () => {
    const result = await testBackendHealth("http://localhost:8080/api", {
      fetchImpl: fetchNotFound,
    });
    expect(result).toEqual({ outcome: "responding", status: 404 });
  });

  it("classifies a transport failure as unreachable", async () => {
    const result = await testBackendHealth("http://localhost:8080/api", {
      fetchImpl: fetchNetworkError,
    });
    expect(result).toEqual({ outcome: "unreachable", status: null });
  });

  it("aborts a probe that exceeds the time budget", async () => {
    const result = await testBackendHealth("http://localhost:8080/api", {
      fetchImpl: fetchHanging,
      timeoutMs: 5,
    });
    expect(result).toEqual({ outcome: "timeout", status: null });
  });

  it("never sends a request for an invalid base URL", async () => {
    let called = false;
    const spy = async () => {
      called = true;
      return { ok: true, status: 200 };
    };

    const result = await testBackendHealth("not-a-url", { fetchImpl: spy });

    expect(result).toEqual({ outcome: "invalid", status: null });
    expect(called).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("applySessionBaseUrl", () => {
  it("re-points the shared client for the session only", () => {
    const original = apiClient.defaults.baseURL;

    try {
      expect(applySessionBaseUrl("https://api.example.test/api")).toBe(
        "https://api.example.test/api",
      );
      expect(apiClient.defaults.baseURL).toBe("https://api.example.test/api");
    } finally {
      apiClient.defaults.baseURL = original;
    }

    expect(apiClient.defaults.baseURL).not.toBe(
      "https://api.example.test/api",
    );
  });

  it("rejects invalid URLs without touching the client", () => {
    const original = apiClient.defaults.baseURL;

    expect(() => applySessionBaseUrl("nope")).toThrow(/http/i);
    expect(apiClient.defaults.baseURL).toBe(original);
  });

  it("exposes the build-time environment default", () => {
    expect(typeof DEFAULT_BASE_URL).toBe("string");
  });
});

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  adaptUserResponse,
  adaptUsersResponse,
  assignUserRole,
  createUser,
  deactivateUser,
  getUsers,
  resetUserPassword,

  updateUserEmail,
} from "../../src/services/userService";

});

describe("userService â€” staff account directory", () => {
  it("adapts the paginated directory and derives account state", () => {
    expect(
      adaptUsersResponse({
        success: true,
        message: "Users retrieved successfully",
        data: { content: [
          {
            userId: 1,
            username: "superadmin",
            email: "superadmin@onecare.test",
            role: "DOCTOR",
            isActive: true,
          },
          {
            userId: 2,
            username: "disabled",
            role: "super_admin",
            isActive: false,
          },
        ], totalPages: 1, totalElements: 2 },
      }),
    ).toEqual([
      {
        userId: 1,
        fullName: "superadmin",
        username: "superadmin",
        email: "superadmin@onecare.test",
        role: "DOCTOR",
        status: "ACTIVE",
        lockedUntil: null,
      },
      {
        userId: 2,
        fullName: "disabled",
        username: "disabled",
        email: "",
        role: "SUPER_ADMIN",
        status: "INACTIVE",
        lockedUntil: null,
      },
    ]);

    expect(() =>
      adaptUsersResponse({ success: true, data: { items: [] } }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
    expect(() =>
      adaptUsersResponse({ success: false, data: [] }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
  });

  it("derives account state from isActive and the lock window", () => {
    expect(
      adaptUserResponse({
        success: true,
        data: {
          id: 7,
          username: "CHEN-8831",
          role: "PHARMACIST",
          dept: "Central Dispensary",
          isActive: false,
        },
      }),
    ).toEqual({
      userId: 7,
      fullName: "CHEN-8831",
      username: "CHEN-8831",
      email: "",
      role: "PHARMACIST",
      status: "INACTIVE",
      lockedUntil: null,
    });

    // AuthServiceImpl sets lockedUntil when the failed-login counter
    // trips (the API answers 423 until that moment).
    const lockedUntil = new Date(Date.now() + 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
    expect(
      adaptUserResponse({
        success: true,
        data: {
          userId: 8,
          username: "VANCE-1102",
          role: "ADMIN",
          isActive: true,
          lockedUntil,
        },
      }),
    ).toMatchObject({ status: "LOCKED", lockedUntil });

    // An expired lock window reads as active again.
    expect(
      adaptUserResponse({
        success: true,
        data: {
          userId: 8,
          username: "VANCE-1102",
          role: "ADMIN",
          isActive: true,
          lockedUntil: new Date(Date.now() - 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
        },
      }),
    ).toMatchObject({ status: "ACTIVE", lockedUntil: null });

    expect(() =>
      adaptUserResponse({ success: true, data: { fullName: "No id" } }),
    ).toThrow(/response format/i);
  });

  it("fetches the directory through the authenticated client", async () => {
    store.dispatch(
      setSession({ accessToken: "u-access", role: "SUPER_ADMIN" }),
    );
    mswState.usersResponses = [
      { content: [
        {
          userId: 1,
          username: "VANCE-1102",
          role: "ADMIN",
          isActive: true,
        },
      ], totalPages: 1, totalElements: 1 },
    ];

    const users = await getUsers();

    expect(users).toEqual([
      {
        userId: 1,
        fullName: "VANCE-1102",
        username: "VANCE-1102",
        email: "",
        role: "ADMIN",
        status: "ACTIVE",
        lockedUntil: null,
      },
    ]);
    expect(new URL(mswState.usersHits[0].url).pathname).toMatch(
      /\/api\/users$/,
    );
    expect(mswState.usersHits[0].authorization).toBe("Bearer u-access");
  });

  it("provisions a new account with the normalised payload", async () => {
    store.dispatch(
      setSession({ accessToken: "u-access", role: "SUPER_ADMIN" }),
    );

    await createUser({
      fullName: "Dr. Arthur Pendelton",
      username: "PENDELTON-3312",
      email: "a.pendelton@onecare.clinic",
      role: "doctor",
    });

    expect(mswState.userCreateHits).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(mswState.userCreateHits[0].body).toMatchObject({
      fullName: "Dr. Arthur Pendelton",
      username: "PENDELTON-3312",
      email: "a.pendelton@onecare.clinic",
      role: "DOCTOR",
    });
    expect(mswState.userCreateHits[0].authorization).toBe(
      "Bearer u-access",
    );
  });

  it("triggers the admin password reset through the authenticated client", async () => {
    store.dispatch(
      setSession({ accessToken: "u-access", role: "SUPER_ADMIN" }),
    );

    await resetUserPassword(5);

    expect(mswState.userResetHits[0].id).toBe("5");
    expect(mswState.userResetHits[0].authorization).toBe(
      "Bearer u-access",
    );
  });

  it("updates email/role, unlocks and deactivates through the governance endpoints", async () => {
    store.dispatch(
      setSession({ accessToken: "u-access", role: "SUPER_ADMIN" }),
    );

    await updateUserEmail(9, "  new.email@onecare.clinic  ");
    expect(mswState.userEmailUpdateHits[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: "9",
      body: { email: "new.email@onecare.clinic" },
      authorization: "Bearer u-access",
    });

    await assignUserRole(9, "doctor");
    expect(mswState.userRoleUpdateHits[0].body).toEqual({ role: "DOCTOR" });

    await unlockUser(9);
    expect(mswState.userUnlockHits[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: "9",
      authorization: "Bearer u-access",
    });

    await deactivateUser(9);
    expect(mswState.userDeactivateHits[0]).toMatchObject({
      id: "9",
      authorization: "Bearer u-access",
    });
  });

  it("rejects an unknown role assignment with 400", async () => {
    const error = await assignUserRole(9, "PATIENT").catch((caught) => caught);

    expect(error.status).toBe(400);
    expect(mswState.userRoleUpdateHits[0].body).toEqual({ role: "PATIENT" });
  });

  it("propagates directory failures for callers to surface", async () => {
    mswState.usersErrorMode = "server-error";
    await expect(getUsers()).rejects.toMatchObject({
      response: { status: 500 },
    });
  });

FILE: D:\DevelopmentProject\Project\onecare-frontend\test\theme\tokens.test.js
